function AnonymisiereDaten(AColumn: TcolumnObj; ATyp:TAnonymisierungsDatenTyp): string;
begin
  result := AColumn.Value;
  case ATyp of
    pdName:                 result := QuotedStr('ImpName');
    pdVorname:              result := QuotedStr('ImpVorname');
    pdStrasse:              result := QuotedStr('Importweg');
    pdHausnr:               result := QuotedStr('3');
    pdHausUnterNr:          result := QuotedStr('a');
    pdOrt:                  result := QuotedStr('ImpOrt');
    pdPlz:                  result := QuotedStr('99990');
    pdSenderName:           result := QuotedStr('ImpSenderName');
    pdSenderOrt:            result := QuotedStr('ImpSenderOrt');
    pdSenderStrasse:        result := QuotedStr('ImportSenderweg');
    pdSenderHausNr:         result := QuotedStr('35');
    pdSenderPlz:            result := QuotedStr('99909');
    pdEmpfaengerName:       result := QuotedStr('ImpEmpfaengerName');
    pdEmpfaengerOrt:        result := QuotedStr('ImpEmpfaengerOrt');
    pdEmpfaengerStrasse:    result := QuotedStr('ImportEmpfaengerweg');
    pdEmpfaengerHausNr:     result := QuotedStr('35');
    pdEmpfaengerPlz:        result := QuotedStr('99909');
    pdAnsprechpartner:      result := QuotedStr('ImpAsp');
    pdAnsprechpartnerEmail: result := QuotedStr('ImpAspEmail');
    pdTelNr:                result := QuotedStr('0123456789');
    pdMail:                 result := QuotedStr('Email@Import.de');
  end;
end;

function TKundenExport.ExportKundenListe(AConnection: TecConnection; AViewContext: TViewContext; AKndIds: TelSimpleLongIdList; AMndId: Integer;
                      AFileName: String; AProdukteExportiert: Boolean; AExportOptionen: TExportOptionen): boolean;
begin
  Result := False;
  FExportOptionen := AExportOptionen;
  FWartenThread := TThreadedProgress.Create('Exportiere Kunden', true, 0);
  FEckdaten := TExportEckdaten.Create(AMndId, true);
  FGpAdrInfoManager := TGpAdrInfoExportManager.Create(AConnection, AViewContext);
  try
    fConnection := aConnection;
    FSdManager := TStammdatenExportImportManager.Create(fConnection, nil); // beim Export werden ATables nicht benötig
    FEckdaten.ExportMndId := AMndId;
    if AProdukteExportiert then
      FEckdaten.ExportMitPrd := 1
    else
      FEckdaten.ExportMitPrd := 0;
    fFileName := aFileName;
    Init(AMndId);

    if AKndIds.Count > 0 then
    begin
      ClearIds;
      FSdManager.Clear;
      FuelleKndIdListe(AKndIds);
      DoExport(FExportOptionen.DoGZip);
    end;
  finally
    FreeAndNil(FWartenThread);
    FreeAndNil(FSdManager);
    FreeAndNil(FEckdaten);
    FreeAndNil(FGpAdrInfoManager);
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenExport.DoExport(ADoGZip: Boolean);
var
  idx: Integer;
  mndId: Integer;
  MndMasterIds: TExportMndMasterIds;
  fStream: TFileStream;
  dbgInfo: String;
begin
  CheckModule;
  fNewLine := False;
  if ADoGZip then
  begin
    fStream := TFileStream.Create(FFileName, fmCreate or fmShareDenyWrite);
    fXmlStream := TJclGZIPCompressionStream.Create(fStream);
  end
   else
  begin
    fStream := nil;
    fXmlStream := TFileStream.Create(FFileName, fmCreate or fmShareDenyWrite);
  end;
  try
    WriteXmlStr('<?xml version="1.0" encoding="windows-1252"?>');
    fNewLine := True;
    WriteXmlStr('<ROOT xmlns="NS" >');
    WriteXmlStr('<' + XML_TAG_TABLES + ' ExportVersion="1.1">');
    for mndId in FMndMasterIds.KeyCollection do
    begin
      MndMasterIds := FMndMasterIds.Values[mndId];
      DoExportDaten(MndMasterIds);
    end;
    WriteXmlStr('</' + XML_TAG_TABLES + '>');

    FEckdaten.WriteExportData(fXmlStream);
    FSdManager.WriteExportData(fXmlStream);
    FGpAdrInfoManager.WriteExportData(fXmlStream);

    if FExportLog.Count > 0 then
    begin
      dbgInfo := '';
      for idx := 0 to FExportLog.Count - 1 do
        dbgInfo := dbgInfo +  FExportLog[idx] + CR_LF;
       TXmlUtils.WriteXmlStr(fXmlStream, '<!-- ExportLog:' + CR_LF + dbgInfo + '-->', true);
    end;

    if FExportOptionen.IsExportXmlDebug then
    begin
      dbgInfo := '';
      for mndId in FMndMasterIds.KeyCollection do
      begin
        if dbgInfo <> '' then
          dbgInfo := dbgInfo + CR_LF + CR_LF;
        dbgInfo := dbgInfo + FMndMasterIds.Values[mndId].GetDebugInfos;
      end;
      TXmlUtils.WriteXmlStr(fXmlStream, '<!-- DebugInfo Initial-SelectedIds:' + CR_LF + dbgInfo + '-->', true);
    end;

    FExportOptionen.WriteToXml(fXmlStream);
    WriteXmlStr('</ROOT>');
  finally
    FreeAndNil(fXmlStream);
    FreeAndNil(fStream);
  end;
  showInfoDialog('Der Kunde wurde erfolgreich nach ' + FFileName + ' exportiert');
end;

procedure TKundenExport.WriteXmlStr(xmlStr: String);
begin
  TXmlUtils.WriteXmlStr(fXmlStream, xmlStr, fNewLine);
end;

procedure TKundenExport.WriteChildTable(table: TTableObj);
var
  i: Integer;
  column: TcolumnObj;
  addOn: String;
  expObj: TExpImpTableObj;
  id: TLongId;
begin
  WriteXmlStr(' <Table Tablename="' + table.Tablename + '" ExportMndId=' + table.ExportMndId + addon + '>');
  addOn := '';
  if FExportOptionen.IsExportXmlDebug then
  begin
    expObj := FExportObjectManager.GetItemByIdentifier(table.Tablename);
    if (expObj <> nil) then
    begin
      column := table.GetColumnByName(expObj.IdFieldName);
      if (column <> nil) then
      begin
        id := TLongId.FromStr(column.Value);
        addon := expObj.IdDebugInfo[id];
        TXmlUtils.WriteXmlStr(fXmlStream, '<!-- DebugInfo:' + CR_LF + addOn + '-->', true);
      end;
    end;
  end;
  for i := 0 to table.Columns.Count - 1 do
  begin
    column := table.Columns[i];
    WriteXmlStr('    <Column CName="' + column.Columnname + '">' + column.Value + '</Column>');
  end;
  WriteXmlStr(' </Table>');
end;

procedure TKundenExport.CheckAndLogKndIdExport(AKndId: TLongId; AGrundFuerHinzufuegen: String; AMndKndIdListe: TelSimpleLongIdList);
var msg: String;
begin
  if not AMndKndIdListe.Contains(AKndId) then
  begin
    msg := 'Indirekt verknüpften Kunden nicht exportiert: KndId = ' + AKndId.ToStr;
    if AGrundFuerHinzufuegen <> '' then
      msg := msg + ' (' + AGrundFuerHinzufuegen + ')';
    AddToExportLog(msg);
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenExport.CheckModule;
begin
  fEinsmanTabellenVorhanden := TDBTableInfo.TableExists(ENERV);
  fEEG2017ZuschlagTabellenVorhanden := TDBTableInfo.TableExists(ENEAZU);
  fEPEXTabellenVorhanden := TDBTableInfo.TableExists(ENEAEAM);
end;

procedure TKundenExport.DoExportDaten(AMndMasterIds: TExportMndMasterIds);
var
  Qry: TSQLQuery;
  cnt, cntNeu, cntAlt: Integer;
  neueZpgs: Boolean;
  i: Integer;
  neueIdsSelektiert: Boolean;
  MndIds: TelSimpleLongIdList;
begin
  Qry := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  MndIds := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  try
    CheckIsCanceled;

    ClearIds;
    SetStatus('Exportiere Daten (MndId: ' + IntToStr(AMndMasterIds.MndId) + ' / KndIds: ' + AMndMasterIds.KndIds.GetUniqueCommaList + ')');
    FExportMndId := AMndMasterIds.MndId;
    FEckdaten.AddExportMndId(FExportMndId);
    MndIds.Add(TLongId.From(FExportMndId));
    InitSelects(FExportMndId);

    FExportObjectManager.ObjENKZB.DoInitialSelectByKndIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.KndIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjENKZE.DoInitialSelectByKndIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.KndIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjEDINV.DoInitialSelectByZptIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjEDINV.DoInitialSelectByMlkIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjEDQUO.DoInitialSelectByMlkIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.MlkIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjEDORD.DoInitialSelectByZptIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjEDORD.DoInitialSelectByMlkIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjEDUTZP.DoInitialSelectByZptIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjEDUTZP.DoInitialSelectByMlkIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.MlkIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjENLOKB.DoInitialSelectByAbnIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.AbnIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjENTRE.DoInitialSelectByZptIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjENTRE.DoInitialSelectByMlkIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.MlkIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjENSRE.DoInitialSelectByAbnIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.AbnIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjENSDAK.DoInitialSelectByZptIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    FExportObjectManager.ObjENNWM.DoInitialSelectByMlkIds(fConnection, AMndMasterIds.MndId, AMndMasterIds.MlkIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENKND, ENKND, 'knd_id', AMndMasterIds.KndIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENMESUeberKndId, ENMES, 'knd_id', AMndMasterIds.KndIds);
    CheckIsCanceled;

    if (TDBTableInfo.TableExists(fENZPTTR)) then
    begin
      ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPTTR, fENZPTTR, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);
    end;

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENMLU + ' WHERE ZPT_ID_MELO = :zpt_id', ENMLU, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENMES, ENMES, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPT, ENZPT, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectZPT_ENZPTH_DEBITOR, ENZPT, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPTH_DEBITOR, ENZPTH, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPT, ENZPT, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPK, ENZPK, 'zpk_id', FZpkIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPG, ENZPG, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    SetStatus('Exportiere Zpg-Daten (MndId: ' + IntToStr(AMndMasterIds.MndId) + ' / KndIds: ' + AMndMasterIds.KndIds.GetUniqueCommaList + ')');
    neueZpgs := true;
    // Generiert evtl. neue ZPGs
    cntAlt := FZpgIds.Count;
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENSMA, ENSMA, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);
    cnt := FZpgIds.Count - cntAlt;
    // Mehrfach-Verknüpfungen über GBZ
    // Generiert evtl. (indirekt) neue GerpIds, AbnIds und ZpgIds
    while neueZpgs do
    begin
      CheckIsCanceled;
      cntAlt := FZpgIds.Count;
      ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENGBZSlave, ENGBZ, 'zpg_id', FZpgIds);
      ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENGBZMaster, ENGBZ, 'zpg_id', FZpgIds);
      ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENGTW_ALT, ENGTW, 'zpg_id', FZpgIds);
      ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENGTW_NEU, ENGTW, 'zpg_id', FZpgIds);
      cntNeu := FZpgIds.Count - cntAlt;
      neueZpgs := cntNeu > 0;
      cnt := cnt + cntNeu;
    end;

    if cnt > 0 then
       ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPG_ZPG, ENZPG, 'zpg_id', FZpgIds);

    SetStatus('Exportiere weitere Daten (MndId: ' + IntToStr(AMndMasterIds.MndId) + ' / KndIds: ' + AMndMasterIds.KndIds.GetUniqueCommaList + ', #1)');
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENGERP, ENGERP, 'zpg_id', FZpgIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENGERP_ABN, ENGERP, 'abn_id', AMndMasterIds.AbnIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENABN, ENABN, 'abn_id', AMndMasterIds.AbnIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENABNGMD, ENABNGMD, 'abn_id', AMndMasterIds.AbnIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENMVA, ENMVA, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPO, ENZPO, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);


    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPOVR, ENZPOVR, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPOV, ENZPOV, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPOE, ENZPOE, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, FSelectENZPMPR, ENZPMPR, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, FSelectENZPMVR, ENZPMVR, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, FSelectENZPMV, ENZPMV, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPZ, ENZPZ, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPS, ENZPS, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPB, ENZPB, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPBD, ENZPBD, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENZPN, ENZPN, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENMLK, ENMLK, 'zpt_id', AMndMasterIds.ZptIds);

    ExportTabelleWithString(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENOBSMaLo, ENOBS, 'obs_bezeichnung', fObsBezeichnungenMaLo);

    SetStatus('Selektiere weitere Ids (MndId: ' + IntToStr(FExportMndId) + ' / KndIds: ' + AMndMasterIds.KndIds.GetUniqueCommaList + ')');
    repeat
      neueIdsSelektiert := false;
      for i := 0 to FExportObjectManager.Count-1 do
        if FExportObjectManager[i].PrepareSelectedIds(fConnection, self, FExportObjectManager) then
          neueIdsSelektiert := true;

       for i := 0 to FExportObjectManager.Count-1 do
          if FExportObjectManager[i].SelectIds(fConnection, FExportObjectManager) then
            neueIdsSelektiert := true;
    until not neueIdsSelektiert;

    SetStatus('Exportiere weitere Daten (MndId: ' + IntToStr(AMndMasterIds.MndId) + ' / KndIds: ' + AMndMasterIds.KndIds.GetUniqueCommaList + ', #3)');
    for i := 0 to FExportObjectManager.Count-1 do
      ExportTabelle(FExportObjectManager[i]);

    SetStatus('Exportiere weitere Daten (MndId: ' + IntToStr(AMndMasterIds.MndId) + ' / KndIds: ' + AMndMasterIds.KndIds.GetUniqueCommaList + ', #4)');
    ExportTabelleWithString(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, GetSelectENMPID(fSelectENMPID), ENMPID, 'mpid_nummer', FMpidNrList);
    ExportTabelleWithString(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENHZO, ENHZO, 'hzo_nr', FHzoNrList);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENABS, ENABS, 'knd_id', AMndMasterIds.KndIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENABT, ENABT, 'abs_id', TableIdMapping.Values[ENABS]);
    // Prozesse aus PRDM
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENPRZ, ENPRZ, 'prz_id', TableIdMapping.Values['ABT_PRZ']);

    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENABSR, ENABSR, 'knd_id', AMndMasterIds.KndIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENABSZ, ENABSZ, 'absr_id', TableIdMapping.Values[ENABSR]);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENVZR, ENVZR, 'knd_id', AMndMasterIds.KndIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENVZZ, ENVZZ, 'vzr_id', TableIdMapping.Values[ENVZR]);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENESA, ENESA, 'mes_id', AMndMasterIds.MesIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENEGA, ENEGA, 'mes_id', AMndMasterIds.MesIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENEWA, ENEWA, 'mes_id', AMndMasterIds.MesIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENESAM, ENESAM, 'esa_id', TableIdMapping.Values[ENESA]);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENEGAM, ENEGAM, 'ega_id', TableIdMapping.Values[ENEGA]);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENEWAM, ENEWAM, 'ewa_id', TableIdMapping.Values[ENEWA]);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENPRDU, ENPRDU, 'mes_id', AMndMasterIds.MesIds);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENPRDM, ENPRDM, 'prdu_id', TableIdMapping.Values[ENPRDU]);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENPRDT, ENPRDT, 'prdu_id', TableIdMapping.Values[ENPRDU]);
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENTUA, ENTUA, 'tua_id', TableIdMapping.Values[ENTUA]);
    // Prozesse aus PRDM
    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectENPRZ, ENPRZ, 'prz_id', TableIdMapping.Values[ENPRZ]);
//    ExportTabelle(AMndMasterIds.KndIds, fSelectCRRAB, CRRAB, 'rab_id', TableIdMapping.Values[CRRAB]);
  finally
    Qry.Free;
    MndIds.Free;
  end;
end;


procedure TKundenExport.FillTable(ATable: TTableObj; AQry: TSQLQuery);
var
  i: Integer;
  id: TLongId;
  column: TcolumnObj;
  msg, value, addon: String;
  expObj: TExpImpTableObj;
begin
  value := NULL_STRING;
  if AQry.Eof then
    exit;

  expObj := FExportObjectManager.GetItemByIdentifier(ATable.Tablename);

  for i := 0 to AQry.FieldList.Count - 1 do
  begin
    if AQry.Fields[i].IsNull then
      Continue;

    column := TcolumnObj.Create(AQry.FieldList.Fields[i].FieldName, AQry.FieldList.Fields[i].DataType, AQry.FieldList.Fields[i].DataSize);
    value := TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(AQry.Fields[i].AsString, column.FieldType);
    column.Value:= value;
    ATable.Columns.Add(column);
    FSdManager.AddUsedValue(FExportMndId, column.Columnname, column.FieldType, value);

    if (ATable.Tablename = ENNWMN
    ) then
    begin
      if (column.Columnname = 'NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP') then
      begin
        if (column.Value = IntToStr(NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP_LIEFERANT)) then
        begin
          FSdManager.AddUsedValue(FExportMndId, 'LIF_ID', ftLargeint, AQry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID').AsString);
        end
        else if (column.Value = IntToStr(NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP_NETZBETREIBER)) then
        begin
          FSdManager.AddUsedValue(FExportMndId, 'NBT_ID', ftLargeint, AQry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID').AsString);
        end
        else if (column.Value = IntToStr(NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP_MESSSTELLENBETREIBER)) then
        begin
          FSdManager.AddUsedValue(FExportMndId, 'MBT_ID', ftLargeint, AQry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID').AsString);
        end
        else if (column.Value = IntToStr(NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP_UEBERTRAGUNGSNETZBETREIBER)) then
        begin
          //ENMPT wird aktuell weder exportiert noch importiert
          FSdManager.AddUsedValue(FExportMndId, 'MPT_ID', ftLargeint, AQry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID').AsString);
        end;
      end;
    end;

    if (ATable.Tablename = ENNWS) and (column.Columnname = 'NBT_ID_NEU') then
      FSdManager.AddUsedValue(FExportMndId, 'NBT_ID', ftLargeint, value);

    if expObj <> nil then
       expObj.AnonymisiereFeldWert(column);

    if AnsiEndsText('_SENDER_NAME', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdSenderName)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_SENDER_STRASSE', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdSenderStrasse)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_SENDER_ORT', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdSenderOrt)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_SENDER_PLZ', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdSenderPlz)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_SENDER_HAUSNR', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdSenderHausNr)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_EMPF_NAME', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdEmpfaengerName)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_EMPF_STRASSE', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdEmpfaengerStrasse)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_EMPF_ORT', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdEmpfaengerOrt)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_EMPF_PLZ', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdEmpfaengerPlz)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_EMPF_HAUSNR', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdEmpfaengerHausNr)
    else
    if (AnsiEndsText('_STRASSE', column.Columnname) or
        AnsiEndsText('_SUCHF_STRASSE', column.Columnname) or
        AnsiEndsText('_SUCHFELDSTRASSE', column.Columnname)) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdStrasse)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_HAUSNR', column.Columnname) or
       AnsiEndsText('SUCHF_HAUSNR', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdHausnr)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_PLZ', column.Columnname) or
       AnsiEndsText('_SUCHF_PLZ', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdPlz)
    else
    if (AnsiEndsText('_ORT', column.Columnname) or
        AnsiEndsText('_SUCHF_ORT', column.Columnname) or
        AnsiEndsText('_SUCHFELDORT', column.Columnname)) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdOrt)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_HAUSUNTERNR', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdHausUnterNr)
    else
    if (AnsiEndsText('_LS_NAME', column.Columnname) or
        AnsiEndsText('_SUCHF_NAME', column.Columnname) or
        AnsiEndsText('_KND_NAME', column.Columnname) or
        AnsiEndsText('ANS_NAME', column.Columnname)) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdName)
    else
    if (AnsiEndsText('_LS_VORNAME', column.Columnname) or
        AnsiEndsText('_KND_VORNAME', column.Columnname) or
        AnsiEndsText('_SUCHF_VORNAME', column.Columnname) or
        AnsiEndsText('ANS_VORNAME', column.Columnname)) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdVorname)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_ANSPRECHPARTNER', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdAnsprechpartner)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('ASP_EMAIL', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdAnsprechpartnerEmail)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_EMAIL', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdMail)
    else
    if AnsiEndsText('_TELEFON', column.Columnname) then
      value := AnonymisiereDaten(column, pdTelNr)
    else
    if ('EPR_BEZEICHNUNG' =  column.Columnname) then
      value := QuotedStr('EPR_BEZEICHNUNG ' + IntToStr( ConnectionPool.GetSequenceValue(SEQ_ENCOMMON)));

    // NBT_NR wird für die Auflösung verwendet, wenn das als QuotedStr gespeichert wird,
    // kommt in SQL = AND NBT_NR = '''200001'''
    if (column.Columnname = 'NBT_NR') then
    begin
      value := EntferneHochkomma(value);
    end;
    column.Value:= value;

    if (ATable.Tablename = ENKND) then
    begin
      if (column.Columnname = 'KND_KUNDENINFO') then
        column.Value:= 'NULL'
      else if (column.Columnname = 'KND_STEUERNUMMER') then
        column.Value:= 'NULL'
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENKZB) then
    begin
      if (column.Columnname = 'KZB_KONTAKTMIT') then
        column.Value:= QuotedStr('IMPORT')
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENKZE) then
    begin
      if (column.Columnname = 'KZE_KONTAKTMIT') then
        column.Value:= QuotedStr('IMPORT')
    end;

    if (ATable.Tablename = ENKNV) and (column.Columnname = 'NBT_NR') then
      FSdManager.AddUsedNbtNr(FExportMndId, value, FieldAsInteger(AQry.FieldByName('KNV_ENERGIEART')));

    if (column.Columnname = 'RO_ID') then
      FGpAdrInfoManager.RegisterExportRoId(column.Value);
    if (column.Columnname = 'ABN_ID') then
      FGpAdrInfoManager.RegisterExportAbnId(column.Value);

    if (column.Columnname = 'MND_ID') And FexportOptionen.IsExportXmlDebug then
    begin
      if expObj <> nil then
      begin
        id := AQry.FieldByName(expObj.IdFieldName).AsLongId;
        addon := CR_LF + expObj.IdFieldName + ': ' + id.ToStr + CR_LF + expObj.IdDebugInfo[id];
      end
       else
      addon := '';

      if (ATable.ExportMndId <> AQry.FieldList.Fields[i].AsString) then
      begin
        msg := '1: Abweichender Mandant (1): ' + ATable.Tablename + ' MndIdIst: ' + AQry.FieldList.Fields[i].AsString + ' <-> MndIdSoll ' + ATable.ExportMndId + addon;
        if DebugHook > 0 then
          ShowErrorDialog(msg);
        AddToExportLog(msg);
      end;

      if (FExportMndId <> AQry.FieldList.Fields[i].AsInteger) then
      begin
        msg := '2: Abweichender Mandan (2): ' + ATable.Tablename + ' MndIdIst: ' + AQry.FieldList.Fields[i].AsString + ' <-> MndIdSoll ' + IntToStr(FExportMndId) + addon;
        if DebugHook > 0 then
          ShowErrorDialog(msg);
        AddToExportLog(msg);
      end;
    end;

   if (AQry.FieldList.Fields[i].FieldName = 'ZSTEP_LISTE') then
     LoadZstepListe(AQry, ATable);
  end;
end;

function TKundenExport.AddIdToList(AListe: TelSimpleLongIdList; AId: TLongId): Boolean;
begin
  Result := True;
  if AId.IsNull then
  begin
    Result := False;
    exit;
  end;

  if (not AListe.Contains(AId)) then
  begin
    AListe.Add(AId);
    Result := False;
  end;
end;

function TKundenExport.AddStringToList(AListe: TStringList; AValue: String): Boolean;
begin
  Result := True;
  if (AListe.IndexOf(AValue) = -1) then
  begin
    AListe.Add(AValue);
    Result := False;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenExport.AddToExportLog(AMsg: String);
begin
  if FExportLog.IndexOf(AMsg) < 0 then
    FExportLog.Add(AMsg);
end;

procedure TKundenExport.AddToMprdList(AMpidNummer: String);
begin
 if (FMpidNrList.IndexOf(AMpidNummer) = -1) then
     FMpidNrList.Add(AMpidNummer);
end;

function TKundenExport.CheckObSchonExportiert(ATableName: String; Qry: TSQLQuery; AMndKndIdListe: TelSimpleLongIdList): Boolean;
var
  i: Integer;
  expObj: TExpImpTableObj;
begin
  Result := False;
  for I := 0 to FExportObjectManager.Count - 1 do
  begin
    expObj := FExportObjectManager[i];
    if expObj.CheckObSchonExportiert(ATableName, Qry, AMndKndIdListe) then
      result := true;
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENZPG) then
  begin
    AddIdToList(FZaeIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZAE_ID').AsLongId);
    AddIdToList(FGerpIds, Qry.FieldByName('GERP_ID').AsLongId);
    AddIdToList(FZpgIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZPG_ID').AsLongId);
    result := AddIdToList(FZpgIdsExportiert, Qry.FieldByName('ZPG_ID').AsLongId);
    AddEexIdsByFkLinkId(Qry.FieldByName('ZPG_ID').AsLongId, EEX_FKTYP_ENZPG);
  end
   else
  if ATableName = ENSMA then
  begin
    AddIdToList(FZpgIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZPG_ID_SMGW').AsLongId);
    AddIdToList(FZpgIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZPG_ID_MME').AsLongId);
    AddIdToIdMapping(ENSMTP, Qry.FieldByName('SMTP_ID'));
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENGTW) then
  begin
    AddIdToList(FGtwIds, Qry.FieldByName('GTW_ID').AsLongId);
    AddIdToList(FGtaIds, Qry.FieldByName('GTA_ID').AsLongId);
    AddEexIdsByFkLinkId(Qry.FieldByName('GTA_ID').AsLongId, EEX_FKTYP_ENGTA);
    result := AddIdToList(FGtwIdsExportiert, Qry.FieldByName('GTW_ID').AsLongId);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENZPTTR) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FZpttrIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZPTTR_ID').AsLongId);
    AddEexIdsByFkLinkId(Qry.FieldByName('ZPT_ID_MALO').AsLongId, EEX_FKTYP_ENZPT);
    AddEexIdsByFkLinkId(Qry.FieldByName('ZPT_ID_TRANCHE').AsLongId, EEX_FKTYP_ENZPT);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = fENZPTTREANL) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FZpttrEanlIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZPTTREANL_ID').AsLongId);
  end
  else
  if (ATableName = ENZAE) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(fZaeIdsExportiert, Qry.FieldByName('ZAE_ID').AsLongId);
    AddIdToList(FZaeIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZAE_ID').AsLongId);
  end
  else
  if (ATableName = ENZAP) then
  begin
    AddIdToIdMapping(ENSMTP, Qry.FieldByName('SMTP_ID'));
  end
  else
  if (ATableName = ENEANL) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FEanlIdsExportiert, Qry.FieldByName('EANL_ID').AsLongId);
    if FEanlIdsExportiert.Count > 0 then
      FEckdaten.ExportEsmMnd := FExportMndId;
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENEAPW) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(fEAPWIds, Qry.FieldByName('EAPW_ID').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('BIG_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FBigIds, Qry.FieldByName('BIG_ID').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('LPR_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FLprIds, Qry.FieldByName('LPR_ID').AsLongId);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENKND) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FKndIdsExportiert, Qry.FieldByName('KND_ID').AsLongId);
    CheckAndLogKndIdExport(Qry.FieldByName('KND_ID').AsLongId, 'ENKND.KND_ID', AMndKndIdListe);
    AddEexIdsByFkLinkId(Qry.FieldByName('KND_ID').AsLongId, EEX_FKTYP_ENKND);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENEANB) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FEanbIds, Qry.FieldByName('EANB_ID').AsLongId);
    CheckAndLogKndIdExport(Qry.FieldByName('KND_ID').AsLongId, 'ENEANB.KND_ID', AMndKndIdListe);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENBIG) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FBigIds, Qry.FieldByName('BIG_ID').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('RGZ_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FRgzIds, Qry.FieldByName('RGZ_ID').AsLongId);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENZPN) then
  begin
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('NET_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FNetIds, Qry.FieldByName('NET_ID').AsLongId);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENZMB) then
  begin
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('MBT_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FMbtIds, Qry.FieldByName('MBT_ID').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('MBT_ID_MALO_ABW').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FMbtIds, Qry.FieldByName('MBT_ID_MALO_ABW').AsLongId);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENLPR) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FLprIds, Qry.FieldByName('LPR_ID').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('NBT_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FNbtIds, Qry.FieldByName('NBT_ID').AsLongId);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENNNB) then
  begin
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('NBT_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FNbtIds, Qry.FieldByName('NBT_ID').AsLongId);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENZPB) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FZpbIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZPB_ID').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('BIK_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FBikIds, Qry.FieldByName('BIK_ID').AsLongId);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENZPBD) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FZpbdIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZPBD_ID').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('ZRT_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FZrtIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZRT_ID').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('ZRT_ID_TLP').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FZrtIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZRT_ID_TLP').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('LPR_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FLprIds, Qry.FieldByName('LPR_ID').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('LPR_ID_TLP').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FLprIds, Qry.FieldByName('LPR_ID_TLP').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('BIG_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FBigIds, Qry.FieldByName('BIG_ID').AsLongId);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENEAVS) then
  begin
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('LIF_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FLifIds, Qry.FieldByName('LIF_ID').AsLongId);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENMES) then
  begin
    AddEexIdsByFkLinkId(Qry.FieldByName('MES_ID').AsLongId, EEX_FKTYP_ENMES);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('LIF_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FLifIds, Qry.FieldByName('LIF_ID').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('KND_ID').IsNull) then
      CheckAndLogKndIdExport(Qry.FieldByName('KND_ID').AsLongId, 'ENMES.KND_ID', AMndKndIdListe);
    AddEexIdsByFkLinkId(Qry.FieldByName('ZPT_ID').AsLongId, EEX_FKTYP_ENZPT);
  end
   else
  if (ATableName = ENLIF) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FLifIds, Qry.FieldByName('LIF_ID').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('KND_ID').IsNull) then
      CheckAndLogKndIdExport(Qry.FieldByName('KND_ID').AsLongId, 'ENLIF.KND_ID', AMndKndIdListe);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENZPT) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FZptIdsExportiert, Qry.FieldByName('ZPT_ID').AsLongId);
    AddStringToList(fMeteringcodes, Qry.FieldByName('ZPT_METERINGCODE').AsString);
    AddIdToList(FZpkIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZPK_ID').AsLongId);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENABN) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FAbnIdsExportiert, Qry.FieldByName('ABN_ID').AsLongId);
    AddEexIdsByFkLinkId(Qry.FieldByName('ABN_ID').AsLongId, EEX_FKTYP_ENABN);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENZPK) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FZpkIdsExportiert, Qry.FieldByName('ZPK_ID').AsLongId);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENGERP) then
    Result := AddIdToList(FGerpIdsExportiert, Qry.FieldByName('GERP_ID').AsLongId);

  if (ATableName = ENGBZ) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FGbzIds, Qry.FieldByName('GBZ_ID').AsLongId);
    AddIdToList(FZpgIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZPG_ID_MASTER').AsLongId);
    AddIdToList(FZpgIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZPG_ID_SLAVE').AsLongId);

    if (not Qry.FieldByName('GBZD_ID').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(TableIdMapping.Values[ENGBZD], Qry.FieldByName('GBZD_ID').AsLongId);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENERVZP) then
  begin
    AddIdToList(TableIdMapping.Values[ENERV], Qry.FieldByName('ERV_ID').AsLongId);
    Result := AddIdToList(TableIdMapping.Values[ENERVZP], Qry.FieldByName('ERVZP_ID').AsLongId);
  end
  else
  if (ATableName = ENERVA) then
  begin
    AddIdToList(TableIdMapping.Values[ENERV], Qry.FieldByName('ERV_ID').AsLongId);
    Result := AddIdToList(TableIdMapping.Values[ENERVA], Qry.FieldByName('ERVA_ID').AsLongId);
  end
  else
  if (ATableName = ENERVZT) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(TableIdMapping.Values[ENERVZT], Qry.FieldByName('ERVZT_ID').AsLongId);
  end
  else
  if (ATableName = ENERV) then
  begin
    // Es muss die andere Liste geprüft werden, sonst liefert die Prüfung TRUE für bereits
    // gesammelte ERV_IDs
    Result := AddIdToList(TableIdMapping.Values[ENERV+'EXP'], Qry.FieldByName('ERV_ID').AsLongId);
  end
  else
  if (ATableName = ENTRE) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(TableIdMapping.Values[ENTRE], Qry.FieldByName('TRE_ID').AsLongId);
  end
  else
  if (ATableName = ENEASEE) then
  begin
    AddIdToList(TableIdMapping.Values[ENTRE], Qry.FieldByName('TRE_ID').AsLongId);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENZPTH) then
    Result := AddIdToList(fZPTHIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZPTH_ID').AsLongId);

  if ((ATableName = ENPRZ) or (ATableName = ENSPZ)) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FBereitsExportiertePrzIds, Qry.FieldByName('PRZ_ID').AsLongId);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENLSCH) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(fLschIds, Qry.FieldByName('LSCH_ID').AsLongId);
    FExportObjectManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDMSC).SelectIdForExport(Qry.FieldByName('MSC_ID').AsLongId, ATableName + ', ' + Qry.FieldByName('LSCH_ID').AsString);
    if FExportOptionen.DoEexExport then
      FExportObjectManager.GetItemByIdentifier(CREPZ).SelectIdForExport(Qry.FieldByName('EPZ_ID').AsLongId, ATableName + ', ' + Qry.FieldByName('LSCH_ID').AsString);

    if (not Qry.FieldByName('PRZ_IDSTORNO').IsNull) then
    begin
      AddIdToList(fLschPrzIdStornos, Qry.FieldByName('PRZ_IDSTORNO').AsLongId);
    end;
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENLSMW) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FLsmwIds, Qry.FieldByName('LSMW_ID').AsLongId);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENLSMH) then
  begin
    Result := AddIdToList(FLsmhIds, Qry.FieldByName('LSMH_ID').AsLongId);
  end;

  if ((ATableName = ENKNV) or (ATableName = ENKNVA) or (ATableName = ENMVA) or (ATableName = ENFVB)) then
  begin
    AddIdToList(fVbaNummern, Qry.FieldByName('VBA_NR').AsLongId);
    if ((ATableName = ENKNV) and
        (not Qry.FieldByName('NBT_NR').IsNull) and
        (FNbtNrAusKnvList.IndexOf(Qry.FieldByName('NBT_NR').AsString) = -1)) then
    begin
      CheckNbtIdZuNbtNr(Qry.FieldByName('MND_ID').AsString, Qry.FieldByName('NBT_NR').AsString);
    end;
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENZPZ) then
  begin
    AddStringToList(fObsBezeichnungenMeLo, Qry.FieldByName('ZPZ_OBISCODE').AsString);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENZPO) then
  begin
    AddIdToIdMapping(ENVBE, Qry.FieldByName('VBE_ID'));

    if (Qry.FieldByName('ZPO_VERWENDUNG').AsString = '1') then
      AddStringToList(fObsBezeichnungenMeLo, Qry.FieldByName('ZPO_OBISCODE').AsString)
    else if (Qry.FieldByName('ZPO_VERWENDUNG').AsString = '2') then
      AddStringToList(fObsBezeichnungenMaLo, Qry.FieldByName('ZPO_OBISCODE').AsString);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENZPOE) then
  begin
    FExportObjectManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDMSC).SelectIdForExport(Qry.FieldByName('MSC_ID').AsLongId, ATableName + ', ' + Qry.FieldByName('ZPOE_ID').AsString);
    Result := AddIdToList(FZpoeIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZPOE_ID').AsLongId);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENVBA) then
  begin
    AddIdToList(fVbaNummern, Qry.FieldByName('VBA_NR').AsLongId);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENKVE) then
  begin
    AddIdToList(fVerIds, Qry.FieldByName('VER_ID').AsLongId);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENNWS) then
  begin
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('NBT_ID_NEU').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FNbtIds, Qry.FieldByName('NBT_ID_NEU').AsLongId);
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENNWMN) then
  begin
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID').IsNull) and (Qry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP').Value = NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP_LIEFERANT) then
      AddIdToList(FLifIds, Qry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID').AsLongId)
    else
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID').IsNull) and (Qry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP').Value = NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP_NETZBETREIBER) then
      AddIdToList(FNbtIds, Qry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID').AsLongId)
    else
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID').IsNull) and (Qry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP').Value = NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP_MESSSTELLENBETREIBER) then
      AddIdToList(FMbtIds, Qry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID').AsLongId)
    else
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID').IsNull) and (Qry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP').Value = NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP_UEBERTRAGUNGSNETZBETREIBER) then
     // AddIdToList(FMptIds, Qry.FieldByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID').AsLongId)
     //wird aktuell wederimportierrt noch exportiert
  end;


  if (ATableName = ENVER) then
  begin
    AddIdToList(fVetNummern, Qry.FieldByName('VET_NR').AsLongId);
  end;

  if ((ATableName = ENVFA) or (ATableName = ENVFAA) or (ATableName = ENVFAI)) then
  begin
    AddStringToList(fFldNamen, Qry.FieldByName('FLD_NAME').AsString);
//    if (not Qry.FieldByName('RAB_ID').IsNull) then
//      AddIdToList(TableIdMapping.Values[CRRAB], Qry.FieldByName('RAB_ID').AsLongId);

    if ((ATableName = ENVFA) or (ATableName = ENVFAA)) then
    begin
      if (not Qry.FieldByName('ART_ID').IsNull) then
        AddIdToList(FArtIds, Qry.FieldByName('ART_ID').AsLongId);

      if (not Qry.FieldByName('GART_ID').IsNull) then
        AddIdToList(FGartIds, Qry.FieldByName('GART_ID').AsLongId);
    end;
  end;

  if (ATableName = ENZLF) then
  begin
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('LIF_ID').IsNull) then

  else if (ATableName = ENZPS) then
  begin

      AddIdToList(FZmweIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZMWE_ID_BEGINN').AsLongId);
    if (not Qry.FieldByName('ZMWE_ID_ENDE').IsNull) then
      AddIdToList(FZmweIds, Qry.FieldByName('ZMWE_ID_ENDE').AsLongId);

  else if (ATableName = ENZMWE) then
