function TKundenExport.FillChildrenWithPreparedQuery(Qry: TSQLQuery; ATableName: String;
   aIdValueList: TDictionary<String, TLongId>;
   AMndKndIdListe: TelSimpleLongIdList): Integer;
var
  Child: TTableObj;
  i: Integer;
  value: TLongId;
begin
  CheckIsCanceled;
  result := 0;
  Qry.Close;
  if (Qry.Params.Count > 0) then
  begin
    for i := 0 to Qry.Params.Count - 1 do
    begin
      if (aIdValueList.TryGetValue(Qry.Params[i].Name, value)) then
      begin
        setDBParam(Qry.Params[i], value);
      end;
    end;
  end;

  Qry.Open;
  while not Qry.Eof do
  begin
    CheckIsCanceled;
    if (CheckObSchonExportiert(ATableName, Qry, AMndKndIdListe)) then
    begin
      Qry.Next;
      continue;
    end;
    result := result + 1;
    Child := TTableObj.Create;
    child.ExportMndId := IntToStr(FExportMndId);
    child.Tablename := ATableName;
    try
      FillTable(Child, Qry);
      WriteChildTable(Child);
    finally
      Child.Free;
    end;
    Qry.Next;
  end;
end;

function TKundenExport.FillChildrenWithPreparedQueryWithString(Qry: TSQLQuery; ATableName: String;
  aKeyValueList: TDictionary<String, String>; AMndKndIdListe: TelSimpleLongIdList): Integer;
var
  Child: TTableObj;
  i: Integer;
  value: String;
begin
  result := 0;
  Qry.Close;
  if (Qry.Params.Count > 0) then
  begin
    for i := 0 to Qry.Params.Count - 1 do
    begin
      if (aKeyValueList.TryGetValue(Qry.Params[i].Name, value)) then
      begin
        setDBParam(Qry.Params[i], value);
      end;
    end;
  end;

  Qry.Open;
  while not Qry.Eof do
  begin
    if (CheckObSchonExportiert(ATableName, Qry, AMndKndIdListe)) then
    begin
      Qry.Next;
      continue;
    end;
    result := result + 1;
    Child := TTableObj.Create;
    child.ExportMndId := IntToStr(FExportMndId);
    child.Tablename := ATableName;
    FillTable(Child, Qry);
    WriteChildTable(Child);
    Child.Free;
    Qry.Next;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenExport.DoProzessTabellen(AMndKndIdListe: TelSimpleLongIdList;
   ANurLieferscheinProzesse: Boolean);
var
  przQuery, lieferscheinPrzQry: TSQLQuery;
  przId, przIdStorno, przIdAbschlagStorno: TLongId;
  przIdList, przIdStornoList, przIdAbschlagStornoList, przIdList2, kndIdList: TelSimpleLongIdList;
  i, j: Integer;
begin
  przIdList := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  przIdList2 := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  kndIdList := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  przIdStornoList := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  przIdAbschlagStornoList := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  przQuery := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  lieferscheinPrzQry := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    przQuery.SQL.Add('SELECT DISTINCT');
    przQuery.SQL.Add('prz.prz_id');
    przQuery.SQL.Add(', vfm.knd_id');
    przQuery.SQL.Add(', rpr.prz_idstorno');
    przQuery.SQL.Add(', prz.prz_absstornoprz_id');
    przQuery.SQL.Add('FROM ' + ENPRZ + ' prz');
    przQuery.SQL.Add('JOIN ' + ENVFM + ' vfm ON vfm.prz_id = prz.prz_id');
    przQuery.SQL.Add('LEFT JOIN ' + ENRPR + ' rpr ON rpr.prz_id = prz.prz_id');
    przQuery.SQL.Add('  AND rpr.knd_id = vfm.knd_id');
    przQuery.SQL.Add('WHERE');
    przQuery.SQL.Add('prz.prz_id <> 0');
    przQuery.SQL.Add('AND NVL(prz.prz_proforma, 0) = 0');
    przQuery.SQL.Add('AND vfm_belegdatum >= ' + dtmLib32SystemZeit.getDatabaseDateString(FExportOptionen.StichtagVon));
    if (FExportOptionen.StichtagBis <> NULL_DATE) then
      przQuery.SQL.Add('AND vfm_belegdatum <= ' + dtmLib32SystemZeit.getDatabaseDateString(FExportOptionen.StichtagBis));
    przQuery.SQL.Add('AND vfm.knd_id = :knd_id');
    przQuery.Prepared := True;

    lieferscheinPrzQry.SQL.Clear;
    lieferscheinPrzQry.SQL.Add('SELECT DISTINCT');
    lieferscheinPrzQry.SQL.Add('prz.prz_id');
    lieferscheinPrzQry.SQL.Add(', rpr.prz_idstorno');
    lieferscheinPrzQry.SQL.Add(', prz.prz_absstornoprz_id');
    lieferscheinPrzQry.SQL.Add('FROM ' + ENPRZ + ' prz');
    lieferscheinPrzQry.SQL.Add('JOIN ' + ENRPR + ' rpr ON rpr.prz_id = prz.prz_id');
    lieferscheinPrzQry.SQL.Add('WHERE 1 = 1');
    lieferscheinPrzQry.SQL.Add('AND prz.prz_id <> 0');
    lieferscheinPrzQry.SQL.Add('AND rpr.knd_id = :knd_id');
    lieferscheinPrzQry.Prepared := True;

    for i := 0 to AMndKndIdListe.Count - 1 do
    begin
      przIdList.Clear;
      if (not ANurLieferscheinProzesse) then
      begin
        SetDBParam(przQuery.ParamByName('knd_id'), AMndKndIdListe.Items[i]);
        SetStatus('Exportiere Rechnungsdaten (MndId: ' + IntToStr(FExportMndId) + ' / KndId: ' + AMndKndIdListe.Items[i].ToStr + ')');
        przQuery.Open;
        while (not przQuery.Eof) do
        begin
          przId := przQuery.FieldByName('prz_id').AsLongId;
          przIdStorno := przQuery.FieldByName('prz_idstorno').AsLongId;
          przIdAbschlagStorno := przQuery.FieldByName('prz_absstornoprz_id').AsLongId;

          if (not przIdList.Contains(przId)) and not przId.IsNull then
            przIdList.Add(przId);

          if not przIdStorno.IsNull then
          begin
            if (not przIdStornoList.Contains(przIdStorno)) then
              przIdStornoList.Add(przIdStorno);
          end;

          if przIdAbschlagStorno.IsNull then
          begin
            if (not przIdAbschlagStornoList.Contains(przIdAbschlagStorno)) then
              przIdAbschlagStornoList.Add(przIdAbschlagStorno);
          end;
          przQuery.Next;
        end;
        przQuery.Close;
      end;

      SetDBParam(lieferscheinPrzQry.ParamByName('knd_id'), AMndKndIdListe.Items[i]);
      lieferscheinPrzQry.Open;
      while (not lieferscheinPrzQry.Eof) do
      begin
        przId := lieferscheinPrzQry.FieldByName('prz_id').AsLongId;
        przIdStorno := lieferscheinPrzQry.FieldByName('prz_idstorno').AsLongId;
        przIdAbschlagStorno := lieferscheinPrzQry.FieldByName('prz_absstornoprz_id').AsLongId;
        if (not przIdList.Contains(przId)) and not przId.IsNull then
          przIdList.Add(przId);

        if not przIdStorno.IsNull then
        begin
          if (not przIdStornoList.Contains(przIdStorno)) then
            przIdStornoList.Add(przIdStorno);
        end;

        if not przIdAbschlagStorno.IsNull then
        begin
          if (not przIdAbschlagStornoList.Contains(przIdAbschlagStorno)) then
            przIdAbschlagStornoList.Add(przIdAbschlagStorno);
        end;
        lieferscheinPrzQry.Next;
      end;
      lieferscheinPrzQry.Close;

      kndIdList.Clear;
      kndIdList.Add(AMndKndIdListe.Items[i]);

      for j := 0 to przIdAbschlagStornoList.Count - 1 do
      begin
        przIdList2.Clear;
        przIdList2.Add(przIdAbschlagStornoList.Items[j]);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENPRZ,  ENPRZ, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENSPZ,  ENSPZ, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENRPR,  ENRPR, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENFDEF, ENFDEF, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENFSU,  ENFSU, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENFVB,  ENFVB, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENKNV,  ENKNV, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENMLR,  ENMLR, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFI,  ENVFI, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVKZ,  ENVKZ, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFM,  ENVFM, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFS,  ENVFS, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFA,  ENVFA, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFAI, ENVFAI, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFME, ENVFME, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENGBT,  ENGBT, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENKNVA, ENKNVA, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFAA, ENVFAA, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectCRABRPG, CRABRPG, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectCRRABG, CRRABG, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
      end;

      for j := 0 to przIdStornoList.Count - 1 do
      begin
        przIdList2.Clear;
        przIdList2.Add(przIdStornoList.Items[j]);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENPRZ,  ENPRZ, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENSPZ,  ENSPZ, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENRPR,  ENRPR, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENFDEF, ENFDEF, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENFSU,  ENFSU, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENFVB,  ENFVB, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENKNV,  ENKNV, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENMLR,  ENMLR, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFI,  ENVFI, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVKZ,  ENVKZ, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFM,  ENVFM, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFS,  ENVFS, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFA,  ENVFA, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFAI, ENVFAI, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFME, ENVFME, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENGBT,  ENGBT, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENKNVA, ENKNVA, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFAA, ENVFAA, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectCRABRPG, CRABRPG, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectCRRABG, CRRABG, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
      end;

      for j := 0 to przIdList.Count - 1 do
      begin
        przIdList2.Clear;
        przIdList2.Add(przIdList.Items[j]);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENPRZ,  ENPRZ, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENSPZ,  ENSPZ, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENRPR,  ENRPR, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENFDEF, ENFDEF, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENFSU,  ENFSU, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENFVB,  ENFVB, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENKNV,  ENKNV, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENMLR,  ENMLR, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFI,  ENVFI, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVKZ,  ENVKZ, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFM,  ENVFM, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFS,  ENVFS, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFA,  ENVFA, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFAI, ENVFAI, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFME, ENVFME, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENGBT,  ENGBT, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENKNVA, ENKNVA, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectENVFAA, ENVFAA, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectCRABRPG, CRABRPG, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
        ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe, fSelectCRRABG, CRRABG, 'prz_id', przIdList2, 'knd_id', kndIdList);
      end;

      if przIdList.Count > 0 then
      begin
         if (FEckdaten.ExportMitRechnungsdaten.IndexOf(IntToStr(FExportMndId))<0) then
            FEckdaten.ExportMitRechnungsdaten.Add(IntToStr(FExportMndId));
      end;

    end;
  finally
    przQuery.Free;
    lieferscheinPrzQry.Free;
    przIdList.Free;
    przIdList2.Free;
    kndIdList.Free;
    przIdStornoList.Free;
    przIdAbschlagStornoList.Free;
  end;
end;

function TKundenExport.ExportTabelle(AMndKndIdListe: TelSimpleLongIdList; select: String; tablename: String; idFeldname1: String;
  idListe1: TelSimpleLongIdList; idFeldname2: String = ''; idListe2: TelSimpleLongIdList = nil): Integer;
var
  i: Integer;
  keyValueList: TDictionary<String, TLongId>;
begin
  CheckIsCanceled;
  SetAddonStatus(tablename);
  result := 0;
  keyValueList := TDictionary<String, TLongId>.Create;
  try
    qryUltimate.SQL.Clear;
    qryUltimate.SQL.Add(select);
    qryUltimate.DBPrepare;
    for i := 0 to idListe1.Count - 1 do
    begin
      CheckIsCanceled;
      keyValueList.Clear;
      keyValueList.Add(idFeldname1, idListe1.items[i]);
      if (idFeldname2 <> '') then
      begin
        keyValueList.Add(idFeldname2, idListe2.items[i]);
      end;
      result := result +
         FillChildrenWithPreparedQuery(qryUltimate, tablename, keyValueList, AMndKndIdListe);
    end;
  finally
    keyValueList.Free;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenExport.ExportTabelle(AExportObj: TExpImpTableObj);
var
  Child: TTableObj;
  qry: TSQLQuery;
  sql, baseSql: String;
  ids: TelSimpleLongIdList;
begin
  if AExportObj.IdsSelektiert.Count = 0 then
    exit;
  if not AExportObj.CanDoExport then
    exit;

  SetAddonStatus(AExportObj.TableName + ' (' + IntToStr(AExportObj.IdsSelektiert.Count) + ' Dts)');
  qry := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  ids := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create(AExportObj.IdsSelektiert.Count);
  try
    ids.AddRange(AExportObj.IdsSelektiert);
    baseSql := 'SELECT * ' + CR_LF +
           ' FROM ' + AExportObj.TableName + CR_LF +
           ' WHERE ' + AExportObj.DefaultFieldName + ' ' + BASISIDLISTTAG;
    sql :=  GetSqlMitIds(baseSql, ids);
    while sql <> '' do
    begin
      qry.SQL.Text := sql;
      qry.Open();
      while not qry.Eof do
      begin
        Child := TTableObj.Create;
        child.ExportMndId := IntToStr(FExportMndId);
        child.Tablename := AExportObj.TableName;
        try
          FillTable(Child, Qry);
          WriteChildTable(Child);
        finally
          Child.Free;
        end;
        qry.Next;
      end;
      sql :=  GetSqlMitIds(baseSql, ids);
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
    ids.Free;
  end;
end;

function TKundenExport.ExportTabelleWithString(AMndKndIdListe: TelSimpleLongIdList; select: String; tablename: String; feldname1: String;
  liste1: TStringList; feldname2: String = ''; liste2: TStringList = nil): Integer;
var
  i: Integer;
  keyValueList: TDictionary<String, String>;
begin
  CheckIsCanceled;
  SetAddonStatus(tablename);
  result := 0;
  keyValueList := TDictionary<String, String>.Create;
  try
    qryUltimate.SQL.Clear;
    qryUltimate.SQL.Add(select);
    for i := 0 to liste1.Count - 1 do
    begin
      keyValueList.Clear;
      keyValueList.Add(feldname1, liste1.Strings[i]);
      if (feldname2 <> '') then
      begin
        keyValueList.Add(feldname2, liste2.Strings[i]);
      end;
      result := result +
        FillChildrenWithPreparedQueryWithString(qryUltimate, tablename, keyValueList, AMndKndIdListe);
    end;
  finally
    keyValueList.Free;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenExport.FuelleKndIdListe(AMndKndIdListe: TelSimpleLongIdList);

var qryMesId,
    qryAbnIds,
    qryMesIdKnd,
    qryEanlIdKnd,
    qryEavKndId,
    qryEagt,
    qryKzeNextKnd,
    qryKndNextPrev,
    qryZpttr,
    qryZpth,
    qryTreByZptId,
    qryTreByMlkId,
    qryZptIdByTreId,
    qryMlkByTreId,
    qryLokByZptId,
    qryLokByMlkId,
    qryLokByTreId,
    qryLok,
    qryZmlkByMlkId,
    qryZmlkByZptId: TSQLQuery;


  function GetMasterIds(AMndId: Integer): TExportMndMasterIds;
  begin
    result := FMndMasterIds.Values[AMndId];
    if result = nil then
    begin
      Result := TExportMndMasterIds.Create(AMndId);
      FMndMasterIds.AddObject(AMndId, Result);
    end;
  end;

  function SelectKndIdsByZptIds(AMasterIds: TExportMndMasterIds): Boolean;
  var i: Integer;
  begin
    result := false;
    for i := 0 to AMasterIds.ZptIds.Count - 1 do
    begin
      qryMesIdKnd.ParamByName('ZPT_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.ZptIds[i];
      qryMesIdKnd.open;
      try
        while not qryMesIdKnd.Eof do
        begin
          if AMasterIds.AddKndId(qryMesIdKnd.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryMesIdKnd.Fields[0], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
            result := true;

          if AMasterIds.AddMesId(qryMesIdKnd.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryMesIdKnd.Fields[1], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
            result := true;

          qryMesIdKnd.Next;
        end;
      finally
        qryMesIdKnd.Close;
      end;
    end;

    if IsESMAktiv(AMasterIds.MndId) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to AMasterIds.ZptIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryEanlIdKnd.ParamByName('ZPT_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.ZptIds[i];
        qryEanlIdKnd.open;
        try
          while not qryMesIdKnd.Eof do
          begin
            if AMasterIds.AddKndId(qryEanlIdKnd.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryEanlIdKnd.Fields[0], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            if AMasterIds.AddEanlId(qryEanlIdKnd.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryEanlIdKnd.Fields[1], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            qryEanlIdKnd.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryEanlIdKnd.Close;
        end;
      end;

      for i := 0 to AMasterIds.KndIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryEavKndId.ParamByName('KND_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.KndIds[i];
        qryEavKndId.open;
        try
          while not qryEavKndId.Eof do
          begin
            if AMasterIds.AddZptId(qryEavKndId.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryEavKndId.Fields[0], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            if AMasterIds.AddEanlId(qryEavKndId.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryEavKndId.Fields[1], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            if AMasterIds.AddMesId(qryEavKndId.Fields[2].AsLongId, 'qryEavKndId.Fields[2], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            qryEavKndId.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryEavKndId.Close;
        end;
      end;

      for i := 0 to AMasterIds.KndIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryEagt.ParamByName('KND_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.KndIds[i];
        qryEagt.open;
        try
          while not qryEagt.Eof do
          begin
            if AMasterIds.AddKndId(qryEagt.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryEagt.Fields[0], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            if AMasterIds.AddEanlId(qryEagt.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryEagt.Fields[1], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            qryEagt.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryEagt.Close;
        end;
      end;
    end;
  end;

  function SelectKndIdsByKzeUndKndFieldss(AMasterIds: TExportMndMasterIds): Boolean;
  var i: Integer;
  begin
    result := false;
    for i := 0 to AMasterIds.KndIds.Count - 1 do
    begin
      qryKndNextPrev.ParamByName('KND_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.KndIds[i];
      qryKndNextPrev.open;
      try
        while not qryKndNextPrev.Eof do
        begin
          if AMasterIds.AddKndId(qryKndNextPrev.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryKndNextPrev.Fields[0], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then
            result := true;

          if AMasterIds.AddKndId(qryKndNextPrev.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryKndNextPrev.Fields[1], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then
            result := true;

          qryKndNextPrev.Next;
        end;
      finally
        qryKndNextPrev.Close;
      end;
    end;

    if FExportObjectManager.ObjENKZE.DoInitialSelectByKndIds(fConnection, AMasterIds.MndId, AMasterIds.KndIds) > 0 then
    begin
      for i := 0 to AMasterIds.KndIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryKzeNextKnd.ParamByName('KND_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.KndIds[i];
        qryKzeNextKnd.open;
        try
          while not qryKndNextPrev.Eof do
          begin
            if AMasterIds.AddKndId(qryKzeNextKnd.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryKzeNextKnd.Fields[0], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            qryKzeNextKnd.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryKzeNextKnd.Close;
        end;
      end;
    end;
  end;

  function SelectZptIds(AMasterIds: TExportMndMasterIds; AMaloCodes: TStrings): Boolean;
  var i: Integer;
  begin
    result := false;
    for i := 0 to AMasterIds.KndIds.Count - 1 do
    begin
      qryMesId.ParamByName('KND_ID') .AsLongId:= AMasterIds.KndIds[i];
      qryMesId.open;
      try
        while not qryMesId.Eof do
        begin
          if AMasterIds.AddZptId(qryMesId.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryMesId.Fields[0], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then // ZptId
            result := true;

          if AMasterIds.AddZptId(qryMesId.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryMesId.Fields[1], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then // MasterZptId
            result := true;

          if AMasterIds.AddZptId(qryMesId.Fields[2].AsLongId, 'qryMesId.Fields[2], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then // MasterZptId2
            result := true;

          if AMasterIds.AddAbnId(qryMesId.Fields[3].AsLongId, 'qryMesId.Fields[3], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then
            result := true;

          if AMasterIds.AddMesId(qryMesId.Fields[4].AsLongId, 'qryMesId.Fields[4], KndId = ' + AMasterIds.KndIds[i].ToStr) then // Mes-MasterMes
            result := true;

          qryMesId.Next;
        end;
      finally
        qryMesId.Close;
      end;
    end;

    if AMasterIds.HasNewZptIdsSinceLastReset then
    begin
      // Weitere vom Zpt ahbänige andere Zpts selektieren!
      for i := 0 to AMasterIds.ZptIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryZpth.Params[0].AsLongId := AMasterIds.ZptIds[i];
        qryZpth.open;
        try
          while not qryZpth.Eof do
          begin
            if AMasterIds.AddZptId(qryZpth.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryZpth.Fields[0], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            if AMasterIds.AddZptId(qryZpth.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryZpth.Fields[1], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;
            qryZpth.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryZpth.Close;
        end;
      end;
    end;

    // Über Zpttr selektieren
    if AMasterIds.HasNewZptIdsSinceLastReset then
    begin
      for i := 0 to AMasterIds.ZptIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryZpttr.Params[0].AsLongId := AMasterIds.ZptIds[i];
        qryZpttr.open;
        try
          while not qryZpttr.Eof do
          begin
            if AMasterIds.AddZptId(qryZpttr.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryZpttr.Fields[0], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            if AMasterIds.AddZptId(qryZpttr.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryZpttr.Fields[1], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            qryZpttr.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryZpttr.Close;
        end;
      end;
    end;

    // TreIds selektieren
    if AMasterIds.HasNewZptIdsSinceLastReset then
    begin
      for i := AMasterIds.LastZptIdsCount to AMasterIds.ZptIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryTreByZptId.Params[0].AsLongId := AMasterIds.ZptIds[i];
        qryTreByZptId.open;
        try
          while not qryTreByZptId.Eof do
          begin
            if AMasterIds.AddTreId(qryTreByZptId.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryTreByZptId.Fields[0], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            if AMasterIds.AddAbnId(qryTreByZptId.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryTreByZptId.Fields[1], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            qryTreByZptId.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryTreByZptId.Close;
        end;
      end;
    end;

    // MlkIds selektieren
    for i := 0 to AMasterIds.ZptIds.Count - 1 do
    begin
      qryZmlkByZptId.Params[0].AsLongId := AMasterIds.ZptIds[i];
      qryZmlkByZptId.open;
      try
        while not qryZmlkByZptId.Eof do
        begin
          if AMasterIds.AddMlkId(qryZmlkByZptId.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryZmlkByZptId.Fields[0], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
          begin
            result := true;
            if AMaloCodes.IndexOf(qryZmlkByZptId.Fields[1].AsString) <0 then
              AMaloCodes.Add(qryZmlkByZptId.Fields[1].AsString);

            qryZmlkByMlkId.Params[0].AsLongId := qryZmlkByZptId.Fields[0].AsLongId;
            try
              qryZmlkByMlkId.Open();
              while not qryZmlkByMlkId.Eof do
              begin
                if AMasterIds.AddZptId(qryZmlkByMlkId.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryZmlkByMlkId.Fields[0], MlkId = ' + qryZmlkByZptId.Fields[0].AsLongId.ToStr) then
                  result := true;
                qryZmlkByMlkId.Next;
              end;
            finally
              qryZmlkByMlkId.close;
            end;
          end;
          qryZmlkByZptId.Next;
        end;
      finally
        qryZmlkByZptId.Close;
      end;
    end;

    if AMasterIds.HasNewMlkIdsSinceLastReset then
    begin
      for i := AMasterIds.LastMlkIdsCount to AMasterIds.MlkIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryTreByMlkId.Params[0].AsLongId := AMasterIds.MlkIds[i];
        qryTreByMlkId.open;
        try
          while not qryTreByMlkId.Eof do
          begin
            if AMasterIds.AddTreId(qryTreByMlkId.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryTreByMlkId.Fields[1], MlkId = ' + AMasterIds.MlkIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            if AMasterIds.AddAbnId(qryTreByMlkId.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryTreByMlkId.Fields[1], MlkId = ' + AMasterIds.MlkIds[i].ToStr) then
              result := true;

            qryTreByMlkId.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryTreByMlkId.Close;
        end;
      end;
    end;

    // ZptIds über TreIds selektieren
    if AMasterIds.HasNewTreIdsSinceLastReset then
    begin
      // restliche ZptIds, MlkIds und AbnIds ahnand der TreIdsptIds selektieren!
      for i := AMasterIds.LastTreIdsCount  to AMasterIds.TreIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryZptIdByTreId.ParamByName('TRE_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.TreIds[i];
        try
          qryZptIdByTreId.Open();
          if not qryZptIdByTreId.Eof and
             AMasterIds.AddTreId(qryZptIdByTreId.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryZptIdByTreId.Fields[0], TreId = ' + AMasterIds.TreIds[i].ToStr) then
           result := true;
        finally
          qryZptIdByTreId.Close;
        end;

        qryMlkByTreId.ParamByName('TRE_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.TreIds[i];
        try
          qryMlkByTreId.Open();
          if not qryMlkByTreId.Eof and
             AMasterIds.AddTreId(qryMlkByTreId.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryMlkByTreId.Fields[0], TreId = ' + AMasterIds.TreIds[i].ToStr) then
           result := true;
        finally
          qryMlkByTreId.Close;
        end;

        qryLokByTreId.ParamByName('TRE_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.TreIds[i];
        try
          qryLokByTreId.Open();
          if not qryLokByTreId.Eof and
             AMasterIds.AddLokbId(qryLokByTreId.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryLokByTreId.Fields[0], TreId = ' + AMasterIds.TreIds[i].ToStr) then
           result := true;
        finally
          qryLokByTreId.Close;
        end;
      end;
    end;

    // LokBuendel
    if AMasterIds.HasNewZptIdsSinceLastReset then
    begin
      for i := AMasterIds.LastZptIdsCount  to AMasterIds.ZptIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        // ZPT_ID_MELO, MLK_ID, NLK_ID, TRE_ID
        qryLokByZptId.ParamByName('ZPT_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.ZptIds[i];
        try
          qryLokByZptId.Open();
          while not qryLokByZptId.Eof do
          begin
            // LokB
            if AMasterIds.AddLokbId(qryLokByZptId.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryLokByZptId.Fields[0], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

            // AbnId
            if AMasterIds.AddAbnId(qryLokByZptId.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryLokByZptId.Fields[1], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

            // ZPT_ID_MELO
            if not qryLokByZptId.Fields[2].IsNull and
               AMasterIds.AddZptId(qryLokByZptId.Fields[2].AsLongId, 'qryLokByZptId.Fields[2], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

            // MLK_ID
            if not qryLokByZptId.Fields[3].IsNull and
               AMasterIds.AddMlkId(qryLokByZptId.Fields[3].AsLongId, 'qryLokByZptId.Fields[3], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

            // TRE_ID
            if not qryLokByZptId.Fields[4].IsNull and
               AMasterIds.AddTreId(qryLokByZptId.Fields[4].AsLongId, 'qryLokByZptId.Fields[4], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

             qryLokByZptId.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryLokByZptId.Close;
        end;
      end;
    end;

    if AMasterIds.HasNewMlkIdsSinceLastReset then
    begin
      for i := AMasterIds.LastMlkIdsCount to AMasterIds.MlkIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryLokByMlkId.ParamByName('MLK_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.MlkIds[i];
        try
          qryLokByMlkId.Open();
          while not qryLokByMlkId.Eof do
          begin
            // LokB
            if AMasterIds.AddLokbId(qryLokByMlkId.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryLokByMlkId.Fields[0], MlkId = ' + AMasterIds.MlkIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

            // AbnId
            if AMasterIds.AddAbnId(qryLokByMlkId.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryLokByMlkId.Fields[1], MlkId = ' + AMasterIds.MlkIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

            // ZPT_ID_MELO
            if not qryLokByMlkId.Fields[2].IsNull and
               AMasterIds.AddZptId(qryLokByMlkId.Fields[2].AsLongId, 'qryLokByMlkId.Fields[2], MlkId = ' + AMasterIds.MlkIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

            // MLK_ID
            if not qryLokByMlkId.Fields[3].IsNull and
               AMasterIds.AddMlkId(qryLokByMlkId.Fields[3].AsLongId, 'qryLokByMlkId.Fields[3], MlkId = ' + AMasterIds.MlkIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

            // TRE_ID
            if not qryLokByMlkId.Fields[4].IsNull and
               AMasterIds.AddTreId(qryLokByMlkId.Fields[4].AsLongId, 'qryLokByMlkId.Fields[4], MlkId = ' + AMasterIds.MlkIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

             qryLokByMlkId.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryLokByMlkId.Close;
        end;
      end;
    end;

    // Lokationsbündel nachselektieren
    if AMasterIds.HasNewLokbIdsSinceLastReset then
    begin
      for i := AMasterIds.LastLokbIdsCount  to AMasterIds.LokbIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryLok.ParamByName('LOKB_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.LokbIds[i];
        try
          // ZPT_ID_MELO, MLK_ID, NLK_ID, TRE_ID
          qryLok.Open();
          while not qryLok.Eof do
          begin
            // ZPT_ID_MELO
            if not qryLok.Fields[0].IsNull and
               AMasterIds.AddZptId(qryLok.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryLok.Fields[0], LokbId = ' + AMasterIds.LokbIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

            // MLK_ID
            if not qryLok.Fields[1].IsNull and
               AMasterIds.AddMlkId(qryLok.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryLok.Fields[1], LokbId = ' + AMasterIds.LokbIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

            // TRE_ID
            if not qryLok.Fields[2].IsNull and
               AMasterIds.AddTreId(qryLok.Fields[2].AsLongId, 'qryLok.Fields[2], LokbId = ' + AMasterIds.LokbIds[i].ToStr) then
               result := true;

             qryLok.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryLok.Close;
        end;
      end;
    end;

    if AMasterIds.HasNewZptIdsSinceLastReset then
    begin
      // restliche AbnIds der ZptIds selektieren!
      for i := AMasterIds.LastZptIdsCount to AMasterIds.ZptIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryAbnIds.ParamByName('ZPT_ID').AsLongId := AMasterIds.ZptIds[i];
        try
          qryAbnIds.Open();
          if not qryAbnIds.Eof then
            AMasterIds.AddAbnId(qryAbnIds.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryAbnIds.Fields[0], ZptId = ' + AMasterIds.ZptIds[i].ToStr)
        finally
          qryAbnIds.Close;
        end;
      end;
    end;
  end;

  procedure DoInitialSelects(AMndId: TMndIdParam; AMaloCodes: TStringList);
  var idsAdded: Boolean;
  begin
    idsAdded := true;
    while idsAdded do
    begin
      GetMasterIds(AMndId).ResetCounter;
      while idsAdded do
        idsAdded := SelectZptIds(GetMasterIds(AMndId), AMaloCodes);

      idsAdded := SelectKndIdsByZptIds(GetMasterIds(AMndId));
      if SelectKndIdsByKzeUndKndFieldss(GetMasterIds(AMndId)) then
        idsAdded := true;
    end;
  end;

var
  i, mndId: Integer;
  qryZptIds: TSQLQuery;
  maloCodes: TStringList;
  masterIdsOther: TExportMndMasterIds;


begin
  qryMesId := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryMesId.SQL.Text := 'SELECT zpt.ZPT_ID, ZPT_MASTERZPT_ID, ZPT_MASTERZPT_ID2, zpt.abn_id, mes.mes_id FROM ' + ENMES + ' mes JOIN ' + ENZPT + ' zpt ON zpt.zpt_id = mes.zpt_id WHERE KND_ID = :KND_ID';
  qryMesId.DBPrepare;

  qryZpth := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryZpth.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZPTH_ABRECHNUNGSZPT_ID, ZPT_ID FROM ' + ENZPTH + ' WHERE ZPTH_ABRECHNUNGSZPT_ID = :ZPT_ID OR ZPT_ID = :ZPT_ID';
  qryZpth.DBPrepare;

  qryZpttr := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryZpttr.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZPT_ID_MALO, ZPT_ID_TRANCHE FROM ' + ENZPTTR + ' WHERE ZPT_ID_MALO = :ZPT_ID OR ZPT_ID_TRANCHE = :ZPT_ID';
  qryZpttr.DBPrepare;

  qryMesIdKnd := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryMesIdKnd.SQL.Text := 'SELECT KND_ID, MES_ID, MES_MASTERMES_ID FROM ' + ENMES + ' WHERE ZPT_ID = :ZPT_ID';
  qryMesIdKnd.DBPrepare;

  qryEanlIdKnd := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryEanlIdKnd.SQL.Text := 'SELECT KND_ID, ezpa.eanl_id ' + CR_LF +
                           ' FROM ' + ENEANB + ' eabw JOIN ' + ENEZPA + ' ezpa ON ezpa.eanl_id = eabw.eanl_id ' + CR_LF +
                           'WHERE ZPT_ID = :ZPT_ID';
  qryEanlIdKnd.DBPrepare;

  qryEavKndId := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryEavKndId.SQL.Text :=  'SELECT DISTINCT mes.ZPT_ID, ezpa.eanl_id, mes.mes_id ' + CR_LF +
                          'FROM ' + ENEAV  + ' eav JOIN '+ ENEAVS + ' eavs on eav.eav_id = eavs.eav_id ' + CR_LF +
                          ' JOIN ' + ENEZPA + ' ezpa ON ezpa.eanl_id = eav.eanl_id ' + CR_LF +
                          ' JOIN ' + ENMES + ' mes ON mes.zpt_id = ezpa.zpt_id ' + CR_LF +
                          ' WHERE mes.knd_id = :KND_ID';
  qryEavKndId.DBPrepare;

  qryEagt := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryEagt.SQL.Text :=  'SELECT DISTINCT eanb.KND_ID, eanb.eanl_id ' + CR_LF +
                          'FROM ' + ENEANB  + ' eanb JOIN '+ ENEAGT + ' eagt on eanb.eanl_id = eagt.eanl_id ' + CR_LF +
                          ' JOIN ' + ENEAGT + ' eagt2 ON eagt2.eag_id = eagt.eag_id ' + CR_LF +
                          ' JOIN ' + ENEANB + ' eanb2 ON eanb2.eanl_id = eagt2.eanl_id ' + CR_LF +
                          ' WHERE eanb2.knd_id = :KND_ID';
  qryEagt.DBPrepare;

  qryZmlkByMlkId := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryZmlkByMlkId.SQL.Text := 'SELECT DISTINCT ZPT_ID ' + CR_LF +
                             'FROM ' + ENZMLK + ' zmlk ' + CR_LF +
                             'WHERE MLK_ID = :MLK_ID';

  qryZmlkByMlkId.DBPrepare;

  qryZmlkByZptId := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryZmlkByZptId.SQL.Text := 'SELECT DISTINCT zmlk.MLK_ID, MLK_CODE ' + CR_LF +
                             'FROM ' + ENZMLK + ' zmlk JOIN ' + ENMLK + ' MLK ON mlk.mlk_id = zmlk.mlk_id' + CR_LF +
                             'WHERE ZPT_ID = :ZPT_ID';

  qryZmlkByZptId.DBPrepare;

  qryKndNextPrev := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryKndNextPrev.SQL.Text := 'SELECT KND_NEXTKND_ID, KND_PREVKND_ID ' + CR_LF +
                             'FROM ' + ENKND + CR_LF +
                             'WHERE KND_ID = :KND_ID';
  qryKndNextPrev.DBPrepare;

  qryKzeNextKnd := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryKzeNextKnd.SQL.Text := 'SELECT KZE_NACHFOLGERKND_ID' + CR_LF +
                             'FROM ' + ENKZE + CR_LF +
                             'WHERE KND_ID = :KND_ID';
  qryKzeNextKnd.DBPrepare;

  qryAbnIds := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryAbnIds.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ABN_ID' + CR_LF +
                        'FROM ' + ENZPT + CR_LF +
                        ' WHERE ZPT_ID = :ZPT_ID';
  qryAbnIds.DBPrepare;

  qryTreByMlkId := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryTreByMlkId.SQL.Text := 'SELECT tre.TRE_ID,  tre.ABN_ID ' + CR_LF +
                             'FROM ' + ENTRMA + ' trma JOIN ' + ENTRE + ' tre ON tre.tre_id = trma.tre_id' + CR_LF +
                             ' WHERE MLK_ID = :MLK_ID';
  qryTreByMlkId.DBPrepare;

  qryTreByZptId := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryTreByZptId.SQL.Text := 'SELECT tre.TRE_ID,  tre.ABN_ID ' + CR_LF +
                             'FROM ' + ENTRME + ' trme JOIN ' + ENTRE + ' tre ON tre.tre_id = trme.tre_id' + CR_LF +
                            ' WHERE ZPT_ID = :ZPT_ID';
  qryTreByZptId.DBPrepare;

  qryZptIdByTreId := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryZptIdByTreId.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZPT_ID ' + CR_LF +
                             'FROM ' + ENTRME + CR_LF +
                            ' WHERE TRE_ID = :TRE_ID';
  qryZptIdByTreId.DBPrepare;

  qryMlkByTreId := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryMlkByTreId.SQL.Text := 'SELECT MLK_ID ' + CR_LF +
                             'FROM ' + ENTRMA + CR_LF +
                             ' WHERE TRE_ID = :TRE_ID';
  qryMlkByTreId.DBPrepare;

  qryLokByZptId := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryLokByZptId.SQL.Text := 'SELECT lokb.lokb_id, lokb.abn_id, ZPT_ID_MELO, MLK_ID, NLK_ID, TRE_ID ' + CR_LF +
                           ' FROM ' + ENLOKB + ' lokb ' + CR_LF +
                           ' JOIN ' + ENLOKBZ + ' lokbz on lokb.lokb_id = lokbz.lokb_id ' + CR_LF +
                           ' JOIN ' + ENLOKBE + ' lokbe on lokbe.lokbz_id = lokbz.lokbz_id ' + CR_LF +
                           'WHERE ZPT_ID_MELO = :ZPT_ID';
  qryLokByZptId.DBPrepare;

  qryLokByMlkId := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryLokByMlkId.SQL.Text := 'SELECT lokb.lokb_id, lokb.abn_id, ZPT_ID_MELO, MLK_ID, NLK_ID, TRE_ID ' + CR_LF +
                           ' FROM ' + ENLOKB + ' lokb ' + CR_LF +
                           ' JOIN ' + ENLOKBZ + ' lokbz on lokb.lokb_id = lokbz.lokb_id ' + CR_LF +
                           ' JOIN ' + ENLOKBE + ' lokbe on lokbe.lokbz_id = lokbz.lokbz_id ' + CR_LF +
                           'WHERE MLK_ID = :MLK_ID';
  qryLokByMlkId.DBPrepare;

  qryLokByTreId := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryLokByTreId.SQL.Text := 'SELECT lokb.lokb_id, lokb.abn_id, ZPT_ID_MELO, MLK_ID, NLK_ID, TRE_ID ' + CR_LF +
                           ' FROM ' + ENLOKB + ' lokb ' + CR_LF +
                           ' JOIN ' + ENLOKBZ + ' lokbz on lokb.lokb_id = lokbz.lokb_id ' + CR_LF +
                           ' JOIN ' + ENLOKBE + ' lokbe on lokbe.lokbz_id = lokbz.lokbz_id ' + CR_LF +
                           'WHERE TRE_ID = :TRE_ID';
  qryLokByTreId.DBPrepare;

  qryLok := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  qryLok.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZPT_ID_MELO, MLK_ID, NLK_ID, TRE_ID ' + CR_LF +
                     ' FROM ' + ENLOKBZ + ' lokbz ' + CR_LF +
                     ' JOIN ' + ENLOKBE + ' lokbe on lokbe.lokbz_id = lokbz.lokbz_id ' + CR_LF +
                     'WHERE LOKB_ID = :LOKB_ID';
  qryLok.DBPrepare;

  qryZptIds := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  maloCodes := TStringList.Create;
  GetMasterIds(fMndId).KndIds.AddRange(AMndKndIdListe);
  try
    FEckdaten.AddInitialKndIds(AMndKndIdListe); // Nur die Zählpunkte dieses Kunden sind vollständig mit allen anderen Kunden exportiert!!

    DoInitialSelects(fMndId, maloCodes);
    if (GetMasterIds(fMndId).ZptIds.Count > 0) and FExportOptionen.DoAndereMandanten then
    begin
      SetStatus('Ermittle Kunden in anderen Mandanten');

      qryZptIds.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZPT_ID, ABN_ID, MND_ID FROM ' + ENZPT + CR_LF +
                            ' WHERE ZPT_METERINGCODE = (SELECT ZPT_METERINGCODE FROM ' +  ENZPT + '  WHERE ZPT_ID = :ZPT_ID)' + CR_LF +
                            '  AND MND_ID <> ' + IntToStr(fMndId);
      qryZptIds.DBPrepare;
      for i := 0 to  GetMasterIds(fMndId).ZptIds.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryZptIds.Params[0].AsLongId := GetMasterIds(fMndId).zptIds[i];
        qryZptIds.open;
        try
          while not qryZptIds.Eof do
          begin
            masterIdsOther := GetMasterIds(qryZptIds.Fields[2].AsInteger);
            masterIdsOther.AddZptId(qryZptIds.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryZptIds(1).Fields[0], ZptId = ' + qryZptIds.Params[0].AsString);
            masterIdsOther.AddAbnId(qryZptIds.Fields[1].AsLongId, 'qryZptIds(1).Fields[0], ZptId = ' + qryZptIds.Params[0].AsString);
            qryZptIds.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryZptIds.Close;
        end;
      end;

      qryZptIds.SQL.Text := 'SELECT zmlk.ZPT_ID, mlk.MND_ID, mlk.MLK_ID FROM ' + ENZMLK + ' zmlk JOIN ' + ENMLK + ' mlk ON mlk.mlk_id = zmlk.mlk_id ' + CR_LF +
                            ' WHERE MLK_CODE = :MLK_CODE AND MND_ID <> ' + IntToStr(fMndId);
      for i := 0 to maloCodes.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qryZptIds.Params[0].AsString := maloCodes[i];
        qryZptIds.open;
        try
          while not qryZptIds.Eof do
          begin
            masterIdsOther := GetMasterIds(qryZptIds.Fields[1].AsInteger);
            masterIdsOther.AddZptId(qryZptIds.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'qryZptIds(2).Fields[0], MlkCode = ' + qryZptIds.Params[0].AsString);
            masterIdsOther.AddMlkId(qryZptIds.Fields[2].AsLongId, 'qryZptIds(2).Fields[0], MlkCode = ' + qryZptIds.Params[0].AsString);
            qryZptIds.Next;
          end;
        finally
          qryZptIds.Close;
        end;
      end;

      for mndId in FMndMasterIds.KeyCollection do
        if mndId <> fMndId then
        begin
          masterIdsOther := GetMasterIds(mndId);
          if (masterIdsOther.ZptIds.Count = 0) then
            Continue;

          DoInitialSelects(mndId, maloCodes);
        end;
    end;
  finally
    qryZptIds.Free;
    qryMesId.Free;
    qryMesIdKnd.Free;
    qryZpth.Free;
    qryZpttr.Free;
    qryEanlIdKnd.Free;
    maloCodes.free;
    qryZmlkByZptId.Free;
    qryZmlkByMlkId.Free;
    qryEavKndId.free;
    qryEagt.free;
    qryAbnIds.Free;
    qryKzeNextKnd.Free;
    qryKndNextPrev.Free;
    qryTreByZptId.Free;
    qryTreByMlkId.Free;
    qryZptIdByTreId.Free;
    qryMlkByTreId.Free;
    qryLokByZptId.Free;
    qryLokByMlkId.Free;
    qryLokByTreId.Free;
    qryLok.Free;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenExport.InitSelects(AMndId: Integer);
begin
  fSelectENEANL         := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENEANL + ' WHERE eanl_id = :eanl_id';

  fSelectENABN := 'SELECT abn_id, mnd_id, abn_status, abn_plz, abn_ort, abn_suchfeldort, abn_strasse, abn_hausnr, abn_hausunternr, '
                + 'abn_suchfeldstrasse, abn_land, bzk_nr FROM ' + ENABN + ' WHERE abn_id = :abn_id';

  fSelectZPT_ENZPTH     := 'SELECT DISTINCT * FROM ' + ENZPT + ' WHERE zpt_id IN (SELECT zpth.zpth_abrechnungszpt_id FROM ' + ENZPTH + ' zpth '
    + 'WHERE zpt_id IN (SELECT zpt_id FROM ' + ENEZPA + ' ezpa WHERE eanl_id = :eanl_id )) '
    + 'OR zpt_id IN (SELECT zpth.zpt_id FROM ' + ENZPTH + ' zpth '
    + 'WHERE zpth_abrechnungszpt_id IN (SELECT zpt_id FROM ' + ENEZPA + ' ezpa WHERE eanl_id = :eanl_id )) ';
  fSelectZPT_ENEANL     := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPT + ' WHERE zpt_id IN (SELECT eanl.zpt_id FROM ' + ENEANL + ' eanl WHERE eanl_id = :eanl_id )';
  fSelectZPT_ENEZPA     := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPT + ' WHERE zpt_id IN (SELECT ezpa.zpt_id FROM ' + ENEZPA + ' ezpa WHERE eanl_id = :eanl_id )';
  fSelectZPT_ENEAVS     := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPT + ' WHERE zpt_id IN (SELECT eavs.zpt_id FROM ' + ENEAV + ' eav, ' + ENEAVS + ' eavs WHERE eav.eav_id = eavs.eav_id AND eav.eanl_id = :eanl_id )';

  fSelectZPT_ENZPTH_DEBITOR := 'SELECT DISTINCT * FROM ' + ENZPT + ' WHERE zpt_id IN ('
    + 'SELECT zpth.zpth_abrechnungszpt_id FROM ' + ENZPTH + ' zpth '
    + 'WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id) '
    + 'OR zpt_id IN (SELECT zpth.zpt_id FROM ' + ENZPTH + ' zpth '
    + 'WHERE zpth_abrechnungszpt_id = :zpt_id) ';

  fSelectENMES     := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENMES + ' WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id';

  fSelectENMESUeberKndId := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENMES + ' WHERE knd_id = :knd_id';

  fSelectENEAPW         := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENEAPW + ' eapw WHERE eapw.eanl_id = :eanl_id';

  fSelectENEZPA         := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENEZPA + ' WHERE eanl_id = :eanl_id';

  fSelectENEZPAV        := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENEZPAV + ' WHERE ezpa_id IN (SELECT ezpa.ezpa_id FROM ' + ENEZPA + ' ezpa WHERE eanl_id = :eanl_id )';

  fSelectENEAV          := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENEAV + ' WHERE eanl_id = :eanl_id';
  fSelectENEAVS         := 'SELECT eavs.* FROM ' + ENEAV + ' eav, ' + ENEAVS + ' eavs WHERE eav.eav_id = eavs.eav_id AND eanl_id = :eanl_id';
  fSelectENEAVSV        := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENEAVSV + ' WHERE eavs_id IN (' +
                              'SELECT eavs.eavs_id FROM ' + ENEAV + ' eav, ' + ENEAVS + ' eavs WHERE eav.eav_id = eavs.eav_id AND eanl_id = :eanl_id)';

  fSelectENZPTH         := 'SELECT DISTINCT * FROM ' + ENZPTH + ' WHERE zpt_id IN (SELECT ezpa.zpt_id FROM ' + ENEZPA + ' ezpa WHERE eanl_id = :eanl_id ) '
   + ' OR zpth_abrechnungszpt_id IN (SELECT ezpa.zpt_id FROM ' + ENEZPA + ' ezpa WHERE eanl_id = :eanl_id ) ';

  fSelectENZPTH_DEBITOR := 'SELECT DISTINCT * FROM ' + ENZPTH + ' WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id '
   + ' OR zpth_abrechnungszpt_id = :zpt_id';

  fSelectENZPT := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPT + ' zpt WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id';

  fSelectENEANB         := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENEANB + ' WHERE eanl_id = :eanl_id';

  fSelectENEAD          := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENEAD + ' WHERE eanl_id = :eanl_id';

  fSelectENZPO := 'SELECT zpo.* FROM ' + ENZPO + ' zpo WHERE zpo.zpt_id = :zpt_id';
  FSelectENZPOVR := 'SELECT * ' + CR_LF +
                   ' FROM ' + ENZPOVR + ' zpovr ' + CR_LF +
                   ' WHERE zpo_id IN (SELECT zpo_id ' + CR_LF +
                   '                   FROM ' + ENZPO + ' zpo  ' + CR_LF +
                   '                  WHERE zpo.zpt_id = :zpt_id)';
  FSelectENZPOV := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPOV + CR_LF +
                   'WHERE zpovr_id  IN (SELECT zpovr_id ' + CR_LF +
                   '                     FROM ' + ENZPOVR + ' zpovr ' + CR_LF +
                   '                    WHERE zpo_id IN (SELECT zpo_id ' + CR_LF +
                   '                                      FROM ' + ENZPO + ' zpo ' + CR_LF +
                   '                                     WHERE zpo.zpt_id = :zpt_id))';

  fSelectENZPOE := 'SELECT zpoe.* FROM ' + ENZPOE + ' zpoe WHERE zpoe.zpo_id IN (SELECT zpo.zpo_id FROM ' + ENZPO + ' zpo '
    + ' WHERE zpo.zpt_id = :zpt_id)';

  fSelectENSMA := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENSMA + ' WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id';

  fSelectENZPMPR := 'SELECT * ' + CR_LF +
                   ' FROM ' + ENZPMPR + ' zpmpr ' + CR_LF +
                    'WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id';

  FSelectENZPMVR := 'SELECT * ' + CR_LF +
                    ' FROM ' + ENZPMVR + ' zpmvr ' + CR_LF +
                    ' WHERE zpmpr_id IN (SELECT zpmpr_id ' + CR_LF +
                    '                     FROM ' + ENZPMPR + ' zpmpr ' + CR_LF +
                    '                   WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id)';
  FSelectENZPMV := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPMV + CR_LF +
                    'WHERE zpmvr_id  IN (SELECT zpmvr_id ' + CR_LF +
                    '                     FROM ' + ENZPMVR + ' zpmvr ' + CR_LF +
                    '                     WHERE zpmpr_id IN (SELECT zpmpr_id ' + CR_LF +
                    '                                         FROM ' + ENZPMPR + ' zpmpr ' + CR_LF +
                    '                                        WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id))';

  fSelectENZPZ := 'SELECT zpz.* FROM ' + ENZPZ + ' zpz, ' + ENZPG + ' zpg WHERE zpg.zpg_id = zpz.zpg_id AND zpg.zpt_id = :zpt_id';

  fSelectENZPS := 'SELECT zps.* FROM ' + ENZPS + ' zps WHERE zpz_id IN (SELECT zpz.zpz_id FROM ' + ENZPZ + ' zpz, '
       + ENZPG + ' zpg WHERE zpg.zpg_id = zpz.zpg_id AND zpg.zpt_id = :zpt_id)';

  fSelectENMVA := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENMVA + ' WHERE mes_id IN (SELECT mes_id FROM ' + ENMES + ' WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id)';

  fSelectENZPB := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPB + ' zpb WHERE mes_id IN (SELECT mes_id FROM ' + ENMES + ' WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id)';

  fSelectENZPBD := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPBD + ' zpbd WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id';

  fSelectENZPN := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPN + ' zpn WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id';

  fSelectENMVAV := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENMVAV + ' WHERE mva_id IN (SELECT mva_id FROM ' + ENMVA +
                     ' WHERE mes_id IN (SELECT mes_id FROM ' + ENMES + ' WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id))';
  fSelectENMPMLK := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENMPMLK + ' WHERE mlk_id IN (SELECT mlk_id FROM ' + ENZMLK + ' WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id)';
  fSelectENZPL := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPL + ' WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id';
  fSelectENZPTB := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPTB + ' WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id';
  fSelectENZPTI := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPTI + ' WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id';
  fSelectENZPH := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPH + ' WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id';
  fSelectENZPHV := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPHV + ' WHERE zpth_id IN (SELECT zpth_id FROM ' + ENZPTH + ' WHERE zpt_id = :zpt_id)';

  fSelectENZPGS  :=  'SELECT * FROM ' + ENZPGS + ' WHERE zpg_id = :zpg_id';
  fSelectENGTW_ALT := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENGTW + ' WHERE zpg_id_alt = :zpg_id';
  fSelectENGTW_NEU := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENGTW + ' WHERE zpg_id_neu = :zpg_id';

  fSelectENTGW  :=  'SELECT * FROM ' + ENTGW + ' WHERE gtw_id = :gtw_id';
  fSelectENTGWS  :=  'SELECT * FROM ' + ENTGWS + ' WHERE tgw_id IN (SELECT tgw_id FROM ' + ENTGW + ' WHERE gtw_id = :gtw_id)';
  fSelectENGTA  :=  'SELECT * FROM ' + ENGTA + ' WHERE gta_id = :gta_id';
  fSelectENATN := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENATN + ' WHERE atn_id = :atn_id';
  fSelectENATZ := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENATZ + ' WHERE atn_id = :atn_id';
  fSelectCRGPVT := 'SELECT * FROM ' + CRGPVT + ' WHERE gpvg_id = :gpvg_id';
  fSelectENVBE := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENVBE + ' WHERE vbe_id = :vbe_id';
  fSelectENVBE_SMTP := 'SELECT DISTINCT vbe.* FROM ' + ENVBE + ' vbe JOIN ' + ENSMTPT +
                        ' smtpt ON smtpt.vbe_id = vbe.vbe_id' + ' WHERE smtp_id = :smtp_id';

  fSelectENABSR := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENABSR + ' WHERE knd_id = :knd_id';
  fSelectENABSZ := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENABSZ + ' WHERE absr_id = :absr_id';
  fSelectENVZR := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENVZR + ' WHERE knd_id = :knd_id';
  fSelectENVZZ := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENVZZ + ' WHERE vzr_id = :vzr_id';
  fSelectENESA := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENESA + ' WHERE mes_id = :mes_id';
  fSelectENEGA := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENEGA + ' WHERE mes_id = :mes_id';
  fSelectENEWA := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENEWA + ' WHERE mes_id = :mes_id';
  fSelectENESAM := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENESAM + ' WHERE esa_id = :esa_id';
  fSelectENEGAM := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENEGAM + ' WHERE ega_id = :ega_id';
  fSelectENEWAM := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENEWAM + ' WHERE ewa_id = :ewa_id';
  fSelectENPRDU := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENPRDU + ' WHERE mes_id = :mes_id';
  fSelectENPRDM := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENPRDM + ' WHERE prdu_id = :prdu_id';
  fSelectENPRDT := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENPRDT + ' WHERE prdu_id = :prdu_id';
  fSelectENTUA := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENTUA + ' WHERE tua_id = :tua_id';
//  fSelectCRRAB := 'SELECT * FROM ' + CRRAB + ' WHERE rab_id = :rab_id';
  fSelectENKFG := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENKFG + ' WHERE mnd_id = :mnd_id AND kfg_wert IS NOT NULL';

end;

Procedure TKundenExport.CheckIsCanceled;
begin
 if (FWartenThread <> nil) and FWartenThread.IsCanceled then
  raise Exception.Create('Abbruch durch Benutzer');
end;

procedure TKundenExport.AddEexIdsByFkLinkId(AFkId: TLongId; AFkTyp: Integer);
var expEpzObj, expEexObj, expLinkObj: TExpImpTableObj;
    key: String;
begin
  if AFkId.IsNull then
    exit;

  if not FExportOptionen.DoEexExport then
    exit;

  key := IntToStr(AFkTyp) + '#' + AFkId.ToStr;

  if (FEexSelectErledigt <> nil) then
  begin
    if (FEexSelectErledigt.IndexOf(key) >= 0) then
      exit;
  end
   else
  begin
    FEexSelectErledigt := TStringList.Create;
    FEexSelectErledigt.Duplicates := dupIgnore;
  end;
  FEexSelectErledigt.Add(key);

  expEpzObj := FExportObjectManager.GetItemByIdentifier(CREPZ);
  expEexObj := FExportObjectManager.GetItemByIdentifier(CREEX);

  expLinkObj := FExportObjectManager.GetItemByIdentifier(CREPZL);
  if FQrySelectEpzl = nil then
  begin
    FQrySelectEpzl := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
    FQrySelectEpzl.SQL.Text :=
      'SELECT EPZ_ID, EPZL_ID' + CR_LF +
      'FROM '+ CREPZL + CR_LF +
      'WHERE EPZL_FKTYP = :EPZL_FKTYP' + CR_LF +
      'AND EPZL_FKID = :EPZL_FKID';
    FQrySelectEpzl.DBPrepare;
  end;

  FQrySelectEpzl.ParamByName('EPZL_FKTYP').AsInteger := AFkTyp;
  FQrySelectEpzl.ParamByName('EPZL_FKID').AsLongId := AFkId;
  try
    FQrySelectEpzl.Open;
    while not FQrySelectEpzl.Eof do
    begin
      expLinkObj.SelectIdForExport(FQrySelectEpzl.fieldByName('EPZL_ID').AsLongId, 'EPZL_FKTYP ' + IntToStr(AFkTyp) + ' / EPZL_FKID ' + AFkId.ToStr);
      expEpzObj.SelectIdForExport(FQrySelectEpzl.fieldByName('EPZ_ID').AsLongId, 'EPZL_FKTYP ' + IntToStr(AFkTyp) + ' / EPZL_FKID ' + AFkId.ToStr);
      FQrySelectEpzl.Next;
    end;
  finally
    FQrySelectEpzl.Close;
  end;

  expLinkObj := FExportObjectManager.GetItemByIdentifier(CREEL);
  if FQrySelectEel = nil then
  begin
    FQrySelectEel := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
    FQrySelectEel.SQL.Text :=
      'SELECT EPZ_ID, eel.EEX_ID, eel.EEL_ID' + CR_LF +
      'FROM '+ CREEL + ' eel JOIN ' + CREEX + ' eex on eex.eex_id = eel.eex_id' + CR_LF +
      'WHERE EEL_FKTYP = :EEL_FKTYP' + CR_LF +
      'AND EEL_FKID = :EEL_FKID';
    FQrySelectEpzl.DBPrepare;
  end;

  FQrySelectEel.ParamByName('EEL_FKTYP').AsInteger := AFkTyp;
  FQrySelectEel.ParamByName('EEL_FKID').AsLongId := AFkId;
  try
    FQrySelectEel.Open;
    while not FQrySelectEpzl.Eof do
    begin
      expLinkObj.SelectIdForExport(FQrySelectEel.fieldByName('EEL_ID').AsLongId, 'EEL_FKTYP ' + IntToStr(AFkTyp) + ' / EEL_FKID ' + AFkId.ToStr);
      expEpzObj.SelectIdForExport(FQrySelectEel.fieldByName('EPZ_ID').AsLongId, 'EEL_FKTYP ' + IntToStr(AFkTyp) + ' / EEL_FKID ' + AFkId.ToStr);
      expEexObj.SelectIdForExport(FQrySelectEel.fieldByName('EEX_ID').AsLongId, 'EEL_FKTYP ' + IntToStr(AFkTyp) + ' / EEL_FKID ' + AFkId.ToStr);
      FQrySelectEel.Next;
    end;
  finally
    FQrySelectEel.Close;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenExport.SetAddonStatus(AStatusText: String);
begin
  SetStatus(FStatusTextBase + ' - ' + StringReplace(AStatusText, 'CREATOR.', '', []), false);
end;

procedure TKundenExport.SetStatus(AStatusText: String; ASetBaseStatusText: Boolean = true);
begin
  if Assigned(FWartenThread) then
    FWartenThread.SetStatus(AStatusText);
  if ASetBaseStatusText then
    FStatusTextBase := AStatusText;
end;

constructor TKundenExport.Create;
begin
  inherited;
  FStatusTextBase := '';
  FExportLog := TStringList.Create;
  FWartenThread := nil;
  FSdManager := nil;
  FMndMasterIds := TelIntegerObjectMap<TExportMndMasterIds>.Create(true);
  FExportObjectManager := TExpImpObjectManager.Create(true);
  FQrySelectEpzl := nil;
  FQrySelectEel := nil;
  FEexSelectErledigt := nil;
end;

function TKundenExport.DoProduktExport(AMndKndIdListe: TelSimpleIntegerList; AMitFrage: Boolean): Boolean;
var
  ExportList: TInterfaceList;
  kndListe: TelSimpleIntegerList;
  Intf: IInterface;
begin
  result := false;
  if not AMitFrage or (ShowConfirmationDialog('Möchten mit dem Kunden verknüpfte Abrechnungsprodukte auch exportieren?') = mrYes) then
  begin
    ExportList := TInterfaceList.Create;
    kndListe := TelSimpleIntegerList.Create;
    try
      kndListe.AddRange(AMndKndIdListe);
      Intf := TProduktExport.Create(True, kndListe);
      ExportList.Add(Intf);
      TfrmEBExport.ShowExportDialog(fViewContext, fConnection, ExportList);
      result := true;
    finally
      ExportList.Free;
      kndListe.Free;
    end;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenExport.CheckNbtIdZuNbtNr(AMndIdStr, ANbtNrStr: String);
var
  Qry: TSQLQuery;
  NbtId: TLongId;
begin
  Qry := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    // In NbtNrList merken, um nicht bei jeder NBT_NR ID erneut zu laden
    FNbtNrAusKnvList.Add(AnbtNrStr);

    // NBT_ID laden
    Qry.SQL.Add('SELECT nbt_id FROM ' + ENNBT);
    Qry.SQL.Add('WHERE mnd_id = ' + AMndIdStr);
    Qry.SQL.Add('AND nbt_nr = ' + QuotedStr(ANbtNrStr));
    Qry.Open;
    if (not Qry.Eof) then
    begin
      NbtId := Qry.FieldByName('nbt_id').AsLongId;
      if (not NbtId.IsNull and (not FNBTIds.Contains(NbtId))) then
      begin
        // in ID List speichern
        FNBTIds.Add(NbtId);
      end;
    end;
  finally
    Qry.Free;
  end;
end;

// Daten müssen mandantscharf selektiert werden, deswegen zuerst wird Selekt initialisiert,
// nach der Initialisierung werden die Listen mit NBT_ID und LIF_ID befüllt
function TKundenExport.GetSelectENMPID(ASelect: String): String;
var
  NbtIdsStr, LifIdsStr, MbtIdsStr: String;
begin
  NbtIdsStr := ConvertLongIdListToSeparatedList(FNBTIds, ', ');
  LifIdsStr := ConvertLongIdListToSeparatedList(FLifIds, ', ');
  MbtIdsStr := ConvertLongIdListToSeparatedList(FMbtIds, ', ');

  // Strings können auch leer sein, dann kommt ORA-Fehler bei AND ((lif_id IN (26)) OR (nbt_id IN ()))
  if ((NbtIdsStr <> NULL_STRING) or (LifIdsStr <> NULL_STRING) or (MbtIdsStr <> NULL_STRING)) then
  begin
    if (NbtIdsStr = NULL_STRING) then
      NbtIdsStr := '0';
    if (LifIdsStr = NULL_STRING) then
      LifIdsStr := '0';
    if (MbtIdsStr = NULL_STRING) then
      MbtIdsStr := '0';

    Result := ASelect + CR_LF + 'AND ((lif_id IN (' + LifIdsStr + ')) OR (nbt_id IN (' + NbtIdsStr + ')) OR (mbt_id IN (' + MbtIdsStr + ')))';
  end
  else
  begin
    Result := ASelect;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenExport.LoadZstepListe(AQry: TSQLQuery; ATable: TTableObj);
var
  InStream: TStream;
  ZipStream: TJclGZIPDecompressionStream;
  JsonStr: UTF8String;
  I: Integer;
begin
  InStream := CreateStreamFromBlob(AQry.FieldByName('ZSTEP_LISTE') );
  try
    ZipStream := TJclGZIPDecompressionStream.Create(InStream);
    try
      SetLength(JsonStr, ZipStream.OriginalDataSize);
      if JsonStr <> '' then
      begin
        ZipStream.Read(PAnsiChar(JsonStr)^, Length(JsonStr));
        for I := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
        begin
          if (TcolumnObj(ATable.Columns.Items[I]).Columnname = 'ZSTEP_LISTE') then
          begin
            TcolumnObj(ATable.Columns.Items[I]).FieldType := ftBlob;
            TcolumnObj(ATable.Columns.Items[I]).Value := UTF8ToString(JsonStr);
          end;
        end;
      end;
    finally
      ZipStream.Free;
    end;
  finally
    InStream.Free;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenExport.RegisterTableIdList(ATableName: String);
begin
  if (not TableIdMapping.Contains(ATableName)) then
  begin
    TableIdMapping.AddObject(ATableName, TelSimpleLongIdList.Create);
  end;
end;

function TKundenExport.AddIdToIdMapping(ATableName: String; AField: TField): Boolean;
begin
  Result := True;
  if (not AField.IsNull) then
  begin
    if (TableIdMapping.Values[ATableName] = nil) then
    begin
      raise Exception.Create('Interner Fehler: ID Liste für ' + ATableName + ' ist nicht registriert!');
    end;
    Result := AddIdToList(TableIdMapping.Values[ATableName], AField.AsLongId);
  end;
end;

{ TExportMndMasterIds }

function TExportMndMasterIds.AddAbnId(AId: TLongId; ASource: String): Boolean;
begin
  result := AddId(aid, FAbnIds, FAbnIdsSelektiertDebugMap, ASource);
end;

begin
  result := AddId(aid, FEanlIds, FEanlIdsSelektiertDebugMap, ASource)
end;

function TExportMndMasterIds.AddId(AId: TLongId; AList:TelSimpleLongIdList; ASelektiertDebugMap: TelLongIdStringMap; ASource: String): Boolean;
begin
  result := false;
  if AId.IsNull then
    exit;

  result := AList.IndexOf(AId) <0;
  if result then
  begin
    AList.Add(AId);
    ASelektiertDebugMap.AddObject(AId, ASource);
  end;
end;

function TExportMndMasterIds.AddKndId(AId: TLongId; ASource: String): Boolean;
begin
  result := AddId(AId, FKndIds, FKndIdsSelektiertDebugMap, ASource)
end;

begin
result := AddId(AId, FLokbIds, FLokbIdsSelektiertDebugMap, ASource)
end;

  result := AddId(AId, FMesIds, FMesIdsSelektiertDebugMap,  ASource)
end;

  result := AddId(AId, FMlkIds, FMlkIdsSelektiertDebugMap, ASource)

 result := AddId(AId, FTreIds, FTreIdsSelektiertDebugMap,  ASource)

end;

constructor TExportMndMasterIds.Create(AMndId: Integer);
begin
  mndId := AMndId;
  FAbnIdsSelektiertDebugMap := TelLongIdStringMap.Create;
  FEanlIdsSelektiertDebugMap := TelLongIdStringMap.Create;
  FKndIdsSelektiertDebugMap := TelLongIdStringMap.Create;
  FMlkIdsSelektiertDebugMap := TelLongIdStringMap.Create;
  FZptIdsSelektiertDebugMap := TelLongIdStringMap.Create;
  FMesIdsSelektiertDebugMap := TelLongIdStringMap.Create;
  FTreIdsSelektiertDebugMap := TelLongIdStringMap.Create;
  FLokbIdsSelektiertDebugMap := TelLongIdStringMap.Create;

  FAbnIds := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  FEanlIds := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  FKndIds := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  FMlkIds := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  FZptIds := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  FMesIds := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  FTreIds := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  FLokbIds := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;

  ResetCounter;
end;

destructor TExportMndMasterIds.Destroy;
begin
  FAbnIdsSelektiertDebugMap.free;
  FEanlIdsSelektiertDebugMap.free;
  FKndIdsSelektiertDebugMap.free;
  FMlkIdsSelektiertDebugMap.free;
  FMesIdsSelektiertDebugMap.Free;
  FZptIdsSelektiertDebugMap.free;
  FTreIdsSelektiertDebugMap.Free;
  FLokbIdsSelektiertDebugMap.free;

  FAbnIds.free;
  FEanlIds.free;
  FKndIds.free;
  FMlkIds.free;
  FMesIds.Free;
  FZptIds.free;
  FTreIds.Free;
  FLokbIds.free;
  inherited;
end;

function TExportMndMasterIds.GetDebugInfos: String;

  function AddDebugInfo(APrefix: String; ASelektiertDebugMap: TelLongIdStringMap):string;
  var i: TLongId;
      idStr: String;
  begin
    result := Aprefix + ': ';
    if ASelektiertDebugMap.Count =0 then
      result := result + 'keine'
    else
    begin
      idStr := '';
      for i in ASelektiertDebugMap.KeyCollection do
      begin
        if idStr <> '' then
          idStr := idStr + ' | ';
        idStr := idStr + 'ID: ' + i.ToStr + ' -> ' + ASelektiertDebugMap.Values[i];
      end;
      result := result + idStr;
    end;
  end;

begin
  result := 'Selektierte IDS im Mandant ' + IntToStr(MndId) + CR_LF +
    AddDebugInfo('ENABN', FAbnIdsSelektiertDebugMap) + CR_LF +
    AddDebugInfo('ENKND', FKndIdsSelektiertDebugMap) + CR_LF +
    AddDebugInfo('ENMES', FMesIdsSelektiertDebugMap) + CR_LF +
    AddDebugInfo('ENMLK', FMlkIdsSelektiertDebugMap) + CR_LF +
    AddDebugInfo('ENZPT', FZptIdsSelektiertDebugMap) + CR_LF +
    AddDebugInfo('ENEANL', FEanlIdsSelektiertDebugMap) + CR_LF +
    AddDebugInfo('ENTRE', FTreIdsSelektiertDebugMap) + CR_LF +
    AddDebugInfo('ENLOKB', FLokbIdsSelektiertDebugMap);
end;

function TExportMndMasterIds.HasNewAbnIdsSinceLastReset: Boolean;
begin
  result := FCntAbnIds <> FAbnIds.Count;
end;

function TExportMndMasterIds.HasNewEanlIdsSinceLastReset: Boolean;
begin
  result := FCntEanlIds <> FEanlIds.Count;
end;

function TExportMndMasterIds.HasNewKndIdsSinceLastReset: Boolean;

  result := FCntKndIds <> FKndIds.Count;
end;

function TExportMndMasterIds.HasNewLokbIdsSinceLastReset: Boolean;

  result := FCntLokbIds <> FLokbIds.Count;

begin
  result := FCntMesIds <> FMesIds.Count;
end;

function TExportMndMasterIds.HasNewMlkIdsSinceLastReset: Boolean;
begin
  result := FCntMlkIds <> FMlkIds.Count;
end;

function TExportMndMasterIds.HasNewTreIdsSinceLastReset: Boolean;
begin
  result := FCntTreIds <> FTreIds.Count;
end;

function TExportMndMasterIds.HasNewZptIdsSinceLastReset: Boolean;
begin
  result := FCntZptIds <> FZptIds.Count;
end;

procedure TExportMndMasterIds.ResetCounter;
begin
  FCntAbnIds := AbnIds.Count;
  FCntMesIds := MesIds.Count;
  FCntEanlIds := EanlIds.Count;
  FCntKndIds := KndIds.Count;
  FCntTreIds := TreIds.Count;
  FCntMlkIds := MlkIds.Count;
  FCntLokbIds := LokbIds.Count;
  FCntZptIds := ZptIds.Count;
end;

end.

interface

  EnergyBasics.ExportFrm, Contnrs, KIS.KundenExportImport.Utils, SystemCore.DBModel,
  KIS.KundenExportImport.XMLObjekte, EnergyBasics.Constants;

type
  TVerarbeiteQueryProc = reference to procedure(ASqlQuery: TSQLQuery);

  TExpImpObjectManager = class;

  IKundenExport=Interface(IInterface)
    procedure AddToMprdList(AMpidNummer: String);
    procedure CheckIsCanceled;
  End;

  IKundenImport=Interface(IInterface)
    function GetMappedMeteringCode(AMeteringCode: String): String;
    function GetMappedMaloCode(AMaloCode: String): String;
    function GetImportObjects: TExpImpObjectManager;

    function GetMappedNwsPaketId(ANwsPaketId: String; ALength: Integer = 11): String;

  End;

  TAnonymisierungsDatenTyp=(pdName, pdVorname, pdStrasse, pdHausUnterNr,
       pdOrt, pdPlz, pdHausnr, pdAnsprechpartner,  pdAnsprechpartnerEmail, pdTelNr, pdMail,
       pdSenderName, pdSenderStrasse, pdSenderHausNr, pdSenderPlz, pdSenderOrt,
       pdEmpfaengerName, pdEmpfaengerStrasse, pdEmpfaengerHausNr, pdEmpfaengerPlz, pdEmpfaengerOrt);


  TIdMapping = class(TDictionary<Int64, Int64>)
  end;

  TTableIdMapping = class
  private
    FDTable: TDBModelTable;
    FTableName: String;
    FSequenceName,
    FIdFieldName: String;
    FIdMapping: TIdMapping;
  public
    constructor Create(ATableName, AIdFieldName, ASequenceName: String);
    destructor Destroy; override;

    property TableName: String read FTableName;
    property SequenceName: String read FSequenceName;
    property IdFieldName: String read FIdFieldName;
    property IdMapping: TIdMapping read FIdMapping;
  end;

  TExpImpTableObj = Class(TObject)
  private
    FOwner: TExpImpObjectManager;
    FDTable: TDBModelTable;
    FIdFeldName,
    FTableName,
    FIdentifier: String;
    FIdsPrepared,
    FIdsChildSelected,
    FIdsSelektiert: TelSimpleLongIdList;
    FIdsSelektiertDebug: TelLongIdStringMap;
    FChildTables: TStringList;
    FImportValues: TelObjectList<TTableObj>;

    FImportNachlauf: TelObjectList<TTableObj>;

    FTableIdMapping: TTableIdMapping;
    FOwnsTableIdMapping: Boolean;
    FCanDoImport: Boolean;
    FCandoExport:Boolean;
    FParents: TelObjectList<TExpImpTableObj>;
    function GetIdDebugInfo(AId: TLongId): String;
    function IsSelbstreferenzierendeSpalte(AColumn: TDBModelColumn): Boolean;

  protected
    FAutoPrepareParentIds: Boolean; // ggf. die ParentIds in abh. der aktuellen ID gleich mit selektieren
    FAutoSelectChildIds: Boolean;  // ggf. alle untergeordnerten Tabellen auch mit selektieren
    function PrepareParentId(AConnection: TecConnection; AId: TLongId; AParent: TExpImpTableObj; AColumnName: String): boolean;
    function PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport; AId: TLongId; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean; virtual;
    function GetRefColumns(ATableName: string; AColumnsRef: TStrings): Boolean;

    procedure SortImportValues; // Für Selbstreferenzierende Tabellen: Zuerst alle ohne Selbstref, und dann sortiert nach ID

    function DoInitialSelect(AConnection: TecConnection; ASql: String; AIdParamName: String; AIdListe: TelSimpleIntegerList; ADoMore: TVerarbeiteQueryProc = nil): Integer; overload;
    function DoInitialSelect(AConnection: TecConnection; ASql: String; AIdParamName: String; AIdListe: TelSimpleLongIdList; ADoMore: TVerarbeiteQueryProc = nil): Integer; overload;

    function SelectOther(AQuery: TSqlQuery; ATableName, AFieldName:String; AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;
    function GetSqlDebugInfo(AQuery: TSqlQuery): String;
  public
    constructor Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATable: String; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>; AIdentifier: String = '');

    procedure Clear;
    destructor Destroy; override;
    function AddChildTable(AChildTable: String): TExpImpTableObj; // Muss auch ein TExpImpTableObj sein! Rückgabewert = aktuelles Objekt!

    // Import
    function DisableImport: TExpImpTableObj; // Muss auch ein TExpImpTableObj sein! Rückgabewert = aktuelles Objekt!
    procedure AnonymisiereFeldWert(AColumn: TColumnObj); virtual; // Aufruf aus Fill-Table
    procedure AddImportValues(ATable: TTAbleObj);
    function HasImportValues: Boolean;

    function GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn:TColumnObj; AKundenImport: IKundenImport;
                         Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean):Boolean; virtual;     // Für Anonymisierungen oder Key-Value-Tabellen-Mappings
    // IDs setzen/generieren, Import-Reihenfolge festlegen etc
    procedure PrepareImport(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenImport: IKundenImport); virtual;
    procedure PreparePksForImport(AConnection: TecConnection; ATable: TTAbleObj; AKundenImport: IKundenImport); virtual;
    function CanDoImport(ATable: TTAbleObj; AKundenImport: IKundenImport): Boolean; virtual;

    procedure DoImportNacharbeiten(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenImport: IKundenImport);

    function PrepareSelectedIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): Boolean; // Falls durch das aktuelle Objekt noch weitere Objekte übernommen werden müssen, z.B. EDINV -> EDDAT, EDEPR. Liefert true, falls etwas zusätzliches selektiert wurde
    function CheckObSchonExportiert(ATableName: String; Qry: TSQLQuery; AMndKndIdListe: TelSimpleLongIdList): Boolean; virtual;
    procedure SelectExportIds(AConnection: TecConnection; ASql: string);
    function SelectIdForExport(AQry: TSQLQuery; ASelectedBy: String; AFieldName:String = ''): Boolean; overload;
    function SelectIdForExport(AId: TLongId; ASelectedBy: String):Boolean; overload;

    function SetExportEnabled(AValue: Boolean): TExpImpTableObj; // Falls das Objekt da sein muss, aber kein Export stattfinden darf
    function SetImportEnabled(AValue: Boolean): TExpImpTableObj;
    function SetAutoSelectChildIds(AValue: Boolean): TExpImpTableObj; // Falls das Objekt nur indirekt durch untergeordnete Elemente selektiert wird, aber automatisch durch den Export des Elements nicht alle (!) untergeordneten Elemente mitgenommen werden müssen

    function SelectIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): Boolean; // Führt die eigentlichen Selects zur ausgewählten ID für den Export aus
    property TableName: String read FTableName;
    property Identifier: String read FIdentifier;
    property DefaultFieldName: String read FIdFeldName;
    property IdsSelektiert : TelSimpleLongIdList read FIdsSelektiert;
    property IdDebugInfo[AID: TLongId]: String read GetIdDebugInfo;
    property ChildTables: TStringList read FChildTables;

    property TableIdMapping: TTableIdMapping read FTableIdMapping;
    property CanDoExport: boolean read FCanDoExport write FCanDoExport;
    property Parents: TelObjectList<TExpImpTableObj> read FParents;
  End;
