uses
  Controls, Kundenabrechnung.ProduktImpExp, EnergyBasics.Version,
  SystemCore.HierarchieManager, GPObjectModel, JclCompression, SystemCore.CommonFunctions,
  EnergyLibrary.Types, SystemCore.DBFunctions, EnergyBasics.EnsuredExec.Constants,
  SystemCore.DBModel.Utils, SystemCore.Constants, EnergyModuleBase.Constants, Math, Lib32SQLDebugFrm, Lib32DebugFrm;


{ TExpImpTableObj }

function TExpImpTableObj.AddChildTable(AChildTable: String): TExpImpTableObj;
begin
  if FChildTables.IndexOf(AChildTable) < 0 then
    FChildTables.Add(AChildTable)
  else
   raise Exception.Create('Interner Fehler: ' + AChildTable + ' wurde bereits hinzugefügt!!');
  result := self;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.SelectIdForExport(AQry: TSQLQuery; ASelectedBy, AFieldName: String): Boolean;
begin
  result := false;
  if not FCanDoExport then
    exit;

  if AFieldName = '' then
    AFieldName := FIdFeldName;

  result := SelectIdForExport(AQry.fieldbyName(AFieldName).AsLongId, ASelectedBy)
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.SelectIdForExport(AId: TLongId; ASelectedBy: String): Boolean;
begin
  result := false;
  if not FCanDoExport then
    exit;

  if AId.IsNull then
    exit;

  if FIdsSelektiert.Contains(AId) then
    exit;

  FIdsSelektiert.Add(AId);
  if FOwner.ExportOptionen.IsExportXmlDebug then
  begin
    FIdsSelektiertDebug.AddObject(AId, ASelectedBy);
    TfrmLib32Debug.AddText(TableName + ': ' + aId.ToStr + ' hinzugefügt wg. ' + ASelectedBy);
  end;
  result := true;
end;

procedure TExpImpTableObj.AddImportValues(ATable: TTAbleObj);
begin
  if (ATable.Tablename = TableName) then
    FImportValues.Add(ATable);
end;

procedure TExpImpTableObj.AnonymisiereFeldWert(AColumn: TColumnObj);
begin
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.CanDoImport(ATable: TTAbleObj; AKundenImport: IKundenImport): Boolean;
begin
  result := not ATable.ImportErledigt;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.CheckObSchonExportiert(ATableName: String; Qry: TSQLQuery;
  AMndKndIdListe: TelSimpleLongIdList): Boolean;
  var ColumnsRef: TStrings;
    i: Integer;
begin
  result := false;
  if not CanDoExport then
    exit;

  if ATableName = TableName then
    result := SelectIdForExport(qry.FieldByName(DefaultFieldName).AsLongId, GetSqlDebugInfo(qry))
  else
  begin
    result := false;
    ColumnsRef := TStringList.Create;
    try
      if GetRefColumns(ATableName, ColumnsRef) then
      begin
         for i := 0 to ColumnsRef.Count - 1 do
           if SelectIdForExport(qry.FieldByName(ColumnsRef[i]).AsLongId, GetSqlDebugInfo(qry)) then
             result := true;
       end;
    finally
      ColumnsRef.Free;
    end;
  end;
end;

procedure TExpImpTableObj.Clear;
begin
  FIdsSelektiert.Clear;
  FIdsPrepared.Clear;
  FIdsChildSelected.Clear;
  if FOwnsTableIdMapping then
    FTableIdMapping.Clear;
end;

constructor TExpImpTableObj.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATable: String; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>; AIdentifier: String = '');
var idx: Integer;
     seq: String;

begin
  if AIdentifier = '' then
    AIdentifier := ATable;
  FParents := TelObjectList<TExpImpTableObj>.create(false);
  FOwner := AOwner;
  FAutoPrepareParentIds := false;
  FAutoSelectChildIds := true;

  FTableName := ATable;
  FCanDoImport := true;
  FCanDoExport := true;

  seq := '';
  FDTable := TDBModelUtils.GetTables.Find(TableName);
  if FDTable = nil then
    raise EInvalidOperation.Create('Tabelle ' + ATable + ' nicht in DB-Modell gefunden');

  if FDTable.PKFieldsCount <> 1 then
    raise EInvalidOperation.Create('Tabelle ' + ATable + ' hat mehr als einen PK');

  FIdentifier := AIdentifier;
  for idx := 0 to FDTable.Columns.Count - 1 do
    if FDTable.Columns[idx].PrimaryKey then
    begin
      FIdFeldName := FDTable.Columns[idx].Name;
      break;
    end;

  seq :=  DBModel.FindSequence(FDTable.Schema, FDTable.Name);
  if (ATableIdMapping <> nil) then
    FTableIdMapping := ATableIdMapping.Values[ATable]
  else
    FTableIdMapping := nil;

  FOwnsTableIdMapping := ATableIdMapping = nil;
  if FTableIdMapping = nil then
  begin
    FTableIdMapping := TTableIdMapping.Create(ATable, FIdFeldName, Seq);
    if ATableIdMapping <> nil  then
      ATableIdMapping.AddObject(ATable, FTableIdMapping);
  end;

  FIdsSelektiert := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  FIdsChildSelected := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  FChildTables := TStringList.Create;;
  FIdsPrepared := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create;
  FImportValues := TelObjectList<TTAbleObj>.Create(false);
  FIdsSelektiertDebug := TelLongIdStringMap.Create;

  FImportNachlauf := TelObjectList<TTAbleObj>.Create(false);
end;

destructor TExpImpTableObj.Destroy;
begin
  FIdsSelektiertDebug.Free;
  FIdsSelektiert.Free;
  FChildTables.Free;
  FIdsPrepared.Free;
  FIdsChildSelected.Free;
  if FOwnsTableIdMapping then
    FTableIdMapping.Free;
  FImportValues.Free;
  FImportNachlauf.Free;
  FParents.Free;
  inherited;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.DisableImport: TExpImpTableObj;
begin
  FCanDoImport := false;
  result := self;
end;

procedure TExpImpTableObj.DoImportNacharbeiten(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenImport: IKundenImport);
var
  i,j: Integer;
  dbColumn: TDBModelColumn;
  tableDts: TTableObj;
  tabCol: TColumnObj;
  id, orgValue: int64;
  sql, pkValue: String;
begin
  for i := 0 to FImportNachlauf.Count - 1 do
  begin
    tableDts := FImportNachlauf[i];
    sql := '';
    for j := 0 to tableDts.Columns.Count - 1 do
    begin
      tabCol := tableDts.Columns[j];
      dbColumn := FDTable.Columns.Find(tabCol.Columnname);

      if dbColumn.PrimaryKey then
      begin
        orgValue := StrToInt64Def(tabCol.Value, NULL_INTEGER);
        if (TableIdMapping.IdMapping.TryGetValue(orgValue, id)) then
        begin
          pkValue := IntToStr(id);
          continue;
        end;

        if FOwner.FImportOptionen.FehlerIgnorieren then
          AKundenImport.AddLog('DoImportNacharbeiten: PkSpalte "' + tabCol.Columnname + '" mit Wert "' + tabCol.Value + '" ist nicht auflösbar --> Kein Update auf FKs')
        else
          raise Exception.Create('DoImportNacharbeiten: PkSpalte "' + tabCol.Columnname + '" mit Wert "' + tabCol.Value + '" ist nicht auflösbar --> Kein Update auf FKs');
        sql := '';
        break;
      end;

      if (tabCol.Value = '') then
        continue;

      if not TableIdMapping.IsRelevant(tableDts.Tablename, tabCol.Columnname) then
        continue;

      if not IsSelbstreferenzierendeSpalte(dbColumn) then
        continue;

      orgValue := StrToInt64Def(tabCol.Value, NULL_INTEGER);
      if (TableIdMapping.IdMapping.TryGetValue(orgValue, id)) then
      begin
        if (sql <> '') then
          sql := sql + ', ';
        sql := sql + dbColumn.Name + ' = ' + IntToStr(id)  + CR_LF;
        continue;
      end;

      if FOwner.FImportOptionen.FehlerIgnorieren then
        AKundenImport.AddLog('DoImportNacharbeiten: Spalte "' + tabCol.Columnname + '" mit Wert "' + tabCol.Value + '" ist nicht auflösbar --> NULL bleibt')
      else
        raise Exception.Create('DoImportNacharbeiten: Spalte "' + tabCol.Columnname + '" mit Wert "' + tabCol.Value + '" ist nicht auflösbar');
    end;

    if (sql <> '') then
    begin
      sql := 'UPDATE CREATOR.' + FDTable.Name + CR_LF +
             ' SET ' + sql +
             'WHERE ' + FIdFeldName + ' = ' + pkValue;
      AConnection.ExecuteDirect(sql);
    end;
  end;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.DoInitialSelect(AConnection: TecConnection; ASql, AIdParamName: String;
  AIdListe: TelSimpleLongIdList; ADoMore: TVerarbeiteQueryProc): Integer;
var qry: TSqlQuery;
    i: integer;
begin
  result := 0;
  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    qry.SQL.Text := ASql;
    qry.DBPrepare;
    if not AnsiContainsText(Asql, FIdFeldName) then
    begin
      if DebugHook > 0 then
        raise Exception.Create('DoInitialSelect, ' + TableName + ': Das Feld ' + FIdFeldName + ' ist nicht im Sql enthalten: ' +  CR_LF + ASql);
      exit;
    end;

    for i := 0 to AIdListe.Count - 1 do
    begin
      qry.ParamByName(AIdParamName).AsLongId := AIdListe[i];
      try
        qry.Open;
        while not qry.Eof do
        begin
          if SelectIdForExport(qry.FieldByName(FIdFeldName).AsLongId, ASql + ' [' + AIdParamName + '=' + AIdListe[i].ToStr + ']') then
            inc(result);
          if assigned(ADoMore) then
            ADoMore(qry);
          qry.Next;
        end;
      finally
        qry.Close;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.DoInitialSelect(AConnection: TecConnection; ASql, AIdParamName: String;
  AIdListe: TelSimpleIntegerList; ADoMore: TVerarbeiteQueryProc): Integer;
var i: integer;
    lIdListe:  TelSimpleLongIdList;
begin
  lIdListe := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create(AIdListe.Count);
  try
    for i := 0 to AIdListe.Count - 1 do
      lIdListe.Add(TLongId.From(AIdListe[i]));
    result := DoInitialSelect(AConnection, ASql, AIdParamName, lIdListe, ADOmORE);
  finally
    lIdListe.Free;
  end;
end;

procedure TExpImpTableObj.PrepareImport(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenImport: IKundenImport);
begin
  SortImportValues;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.SetAutoSelectChildIds(AValue: Boolean): TExpImpTableObj;
begin
  FAutoSelectChildIds := AValue;
  result := Self;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.SetExportEnabled(AValue: Boolean): TExpImpTableObj;
begin
  FCanDoExport := AValue;
  result := Self;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.SetImportEnabled(AValue: Boolean): TExpImpTableObj;
begin
  FCanDoExport := AValue;
  Result := Self;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.GetIdDebugInfo(AId: TLongId): String;
begin
  result := '';
  if FOwner.ExportOptionen.IsExportXmlDebug and FIdsSelektiertDebug.ContainsKey(aid) then
    result := FIdsSelektiertDebug.Values[AId]
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn:TColumnObj; AKundenImport: IKundenImport;
    Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean):Boolean;
var id, orgValue: int64;
    dbColumn: TDBModelColumn;

begin
  result := False;
  ANewValue := AColumn.Value;
  if AColumn.Value = '' then
    exit;

  orgValue := StrToInt64Def(AColumn.Value, NULL_INTEGER);
  if orgValue = NULL_INTEGER then
    exit;

  if TableIdMapping.IsRelevant(ATable.Tablename, AColumn.Columnname) then
  begin
    if (TableIdMapping.IdMapping.TryGetValue(orgValue, id)) then
    begin
      result := true;
      ANewValue := IntToStr(id);
      exit;
    end;

    if not AForInsert then
      exit;

    dbColumn := FDTable.Columns.Find(AColumn.Columnname);
    if IsSelbstreferenzierendeSpalte(dbColumn) then
    begin
      if not dbColumn.Nullable then
         raise Exception.Create('GetNewColumnValue: Selbstreferenzierte Spalte "' + AColumn.Columnname + '" ist nicht Nullable');
      if FImportNachlauf.IndexOf(ATable) < 0 then
          FImportNachlauf.Add(ATable);
      ANewValue := '';
      result := true;
      exit;
    end;

    if FOwner.FImportOptionen.FehlerIgnorieren then
      AKundenImport.AddLog('GetNewColumnValue: Spalte "' + AColumn.Columnname + '" mit Wert "' + AColumn.Value + '" ist nicht auflösbar --> NULL importiert')
    else
      raise Exception.Create('GetNewColumnValue: Spalte "' + AColumn.Columnname + '" mit Wert "' + AColumn.Value + '" ist nicht auflösbar');
  end;
end;

procedure TExpImpTableObj.SortImportValues;

var orgValue: int64;
    tabIdx, colIdx: Integer;
    table: TTAbleObj;
    col: TColumnObj;
    columnsRef: TStrings;
    refTables: TelObjectList<TTAbleObj>;

begin
  columnsRef := TStringList.Create;
  refTables := TelObjectList<TTAbleObj>.Create(false);
  try
    if GetRefColumns(TableName, columnsRef) then
    begin
      for tabIdx := FImportValues.Count - 1 downto 0 do
      begin
        table := FImportValues[tabIdx];
        for colIdx := 0 to columnsRef.Count - 1 do
        begin
          col := table.Columns[colIdx];
          orgValue := StrToInt64Def(col.Value, NULL_INTEGER);
          if orgValue <> NULL_INTEGER then
          begin
            refTables.Add(table);
            FImportValues.Remove(table);
            break;
          end;
        end;
      end;
    end;

    FImportValues.SortList(
          function(Item1, Item2: Pointer): Integer
          var
            id1, id2: Int64;
          begin
            Result := 0;
            if (TObject(Item1) is TTAbleObj) and (TObject(Item2) is TTAbleObj) then begin
              id1 := StrToInt64Def(TTAbleObj(Item1).GetColumnByName(FIdFeldName).Value, NULL_INTEGER);
              id2 := StrToInt64Def(TTAbleObj(Item2).GetColumnByName(FIdFeldName).Value, NULL_INTEGER);
              Result := CompareValue(id1, id2);
            end;
          end);

    if refTables.Count > 0 then
    begin
      RefTables.SortList(function(Item1, Item2: Pointer): Integer
            var
              id1, id2: Int64;
            begin
              Result := 0;
              if (TObject(Item1) is TTAbleObj) and (TObject(Item2) is TTAbleObj) then begin
                id1 := StrToInt64Def(TTAbleObj(Item1).GetColumnByName(FIdFeldName).Value, NULL_INTEGER);
                id2 := StrToInt64Def(TTAbleObj(Item2).GetColumnByName(FIdFeldName).Value, NULL_INTEGER);
                Result := CompareValue(id1, id2);
              end;
            end);

      FImportValues.AddRange(refTables);
    end;

  finally
    refTables.Free;
    columnsRef.Free;
  end;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.GetRefColumns(ATableName: string; AColumnsRef: TStrings): Boolean;
var i: Integer;
begin
  result := false;
  for i := 0 to FDTable.ReferencedBy.Count - 1 do
    if FDTable.ReferencedBy[i].FKTableSchema + '.' + FDTable.ReferencedBy[i].FKTable = ATableName then
    begin
      if length(FDTable.ReferencedBy[i].FKFieldnames) <> 1 then
          raise EInvalidOperation.Create('length(FDTable.ReferencedBy[i].FKFieldnames) <> 1  wird nicht unterstützt!');
      AColumnsRef.Add(FDTable.ReferencedBy[i].FKFieldnames[0]);
      result := true;
    end;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.GetSqlDebugInfo(AQuery: TSqlQuery): String;
var
  I: Integer;
begin
  if FOwner.ExportOptionen.IsExportXmlDebug then
  begin
    result := AQuery.SQL.Text;
    for I := 0 to AQuery.Params.Count - 1 do
      result := result + CR_LF + AQuery.Params[i].Name + ' = ' + VarToStr(AQuery.Params[i].Value);
  end
   else result := '';
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.HasImportValues: Boolean;
var idx: Integer;
begin
  result := false;
  if not FCanDoImport then
   exit;

  for idx := 0 to FImportValues.Count -1 do
    if not FImportValues[idx].ImportErledigt then
    begin
      result := true;
      break;
    end;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.IsSelbstreferenzierendeSpalte(AColumn: TDBModelColumn): Boolean;
var
  ref: TDBModelRef;
  i, j: Integer;
begin
  result := false;
  if AColumn = nil then
    exit;

  for i := 0 to FDTable.ReferencesTo.Count - 1 do
  begin
    ref := FDTable.ReferencesTo[i];
    if ref.FKTableSchema + '.' + ref.FKTable <> FTableName then
      continue;
    for j := 0 to high(ref.FKSourceFieldnames) do
      if (ref.FKSourceFieldnames[j] = AColumn.name) then
      begin
        result := true;
        exit;
      end;
  end;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.PrepareParentId(AConnection: TecConnection;
      AId: TLongId; AParent: TExpImpTableObj; AColumnName: String): boolean;
var qry: TSQLQuery;
begin
  result := false;
  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ' + AColumnName +
                    ' FROM ' + TableName + ' WHERE ' + FIdFeldName + ' = :IDFELD';
    qry.DBPrepare;
    qry.ParamByName('IDFELD').AsLongId := AId;
    qry.Open();
    while not qry.Eof do
    begin
      if AParent.SelectIdForExport(qry.Fields[0].AsLongId,TableName + ' / ' + FIdFeldName + ' = ' + AId.ToStr) then
        result := True;
      qry.Next;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

procedure TExpImpTableObj.PreparePksForImport(AConnection: TecConnection; ATable: TTAbleObj; AKundenImport: IKundenImport);
var id, orgValue: int64;
    col: TColumnObj;
    dbCol: TDBModelColumn;
begin
  // PK-Ids setzen
  col := ATable.GetColumnByName(FIdFeldName, true);
  orgValue := StrToInt64Def(col.Value, NULL_INTEGER);
  if orgValue = NULL_INTEGER then
     raise Exception.Create(TableName + ': PK-Spalte ' + FIdFeldName + ' leer!!');

  dbCol := FDTable.Columns.Find(col.Columnname);
  if (dbCol = nil) then
     raise Exception.Create(TableName + ': PK-Spalte ' + FIdFeldName + ' nicht im DB-Modell gefunden!!');

  if not TableIdMapping.IdMapping.TryGetValue(orgValue, id) then
  begin
    // Neue ID generieren
    id := ConnectionPool.GetSequenceValue(TableIdMapping.SequenceName);
    TableIdMapping.IdMapping.Add(orgValue, id);
  end;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport; AId: TLongId; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;
var refIdx: Integer;
    parent: TExpImpTableObj;
begin
  result := false;
  if FAutoPrepareParentIds then
    for refIdx := 0 to FDTable.ReferencesTo.Count-1 do
    if length(FDTable.ReferencesTo[refIdx].FKSourceFieldnames) = 1 then
    begin
      parent := AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier('CREATOR.' + FDTable.ReferencesTo[refIdx].FKTable);
      if (parent <> nil) then
      begin
        if parent.ChildTables.IndexOf(TableName) >= 0 then
        begin
          if PrepareParentId(AConnection, aid, parent, FDTable.ReferencesTo[refIdx].FKSourceFieldnames[0]) then
              result := True;
        end;
      end;
    end;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.PrepareSelectedIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport;
  AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): Boolean;
var idx: Integer;
    id : TLongId;
begin
  result := false;
  if not CanDoExport then
    exit;


  for idx := 0 to FIdsSelektiert.Count - 1 do
    if not FIdsPrepared.Contains(FIdsSelektiert[idx]) then
    begin
      id := FIdsSelektiert[idx];
      if PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, id, AExpImpManager) then
        result := true;
    end;
  // IDS selektieren, damit müssten wir 90% der Selbstreferenzierten ID-Sachen erledigt kriegen
  FIdsSelektiert.Sort(false);
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.SelectIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager):boolean;
var idx: Integer;
    child: TExpImpTableObj;
    baseSql, sql: String;
    idsChildSelects,
    idsExport: TelSimpleLongIdList;
    qry: TSqlQuery;
begin
  result := false;
  if not CanDoExport then
    exit;

  result := false;
  if FIdsSelektiert.Count = 0 then
    exit;

  if FChildTables.Count = 0 then
    exit;

  if AutoSelectChildIds then
  begin
    idsExport := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create();;
    idsChildSelects := TelSimpleLongIdList.Create();;
    qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
    try
      for idx := 0 to FIdsSelektiert.Count - 1 do
        if not FIdsChildSelected.Contains(FIdsSelektiert[idx]) then
          idsChildSelects.Add(FIdsSelektiert[idx]);

      if idsChildSelects.Count > 0 then
      begin
        for idx := 0 to FChildTables.Count - 1 do
        begin
          idsExport.AddRange(idsChildSelects);
          child := AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(FChildTables[idx]);
          if child = nil then
             raise EInvalidOperation.Create('Kein TExpImpTableObj für ' + FChildTables[idx] + ' gefunden!');
          baseSql := 'SELECT ' + child.DefaultFieldName + ',' + DefaultFieldName + CR_LF +
                 ' FROM ' + child.TableName + CR_LF +
                 ' WHERE ' + DefaultFieldName + ' ' + BASISIDLISTTAG;
          sql :=  GetSqlMitIds(baseSql, idsExport);
          while sql <> '' do
          begin
            qry.SQL.Text := sql;
            qry.Open();
            while not qry.Eof do
            begin
              if child.SelectIdForExport(qry, 'SelectId/Child by ' + DefaultFieldName + ' = ' + qry.FieldByName(DefaultFieldName).AsString) then
                  result := true;
              qry.Next;
            end;
            sql := GetSqlMitIds(baseSql, idsExport);
          end;
        end;
        FIdsChildSelected.AddRange(idsChildSelects);
      end
    finally
      idsExport.Free;
      idsChildSelects.Free;
      qry.Free;
    end;
  end;
end;

function TExpImpTableObj.SelectOther( AQuery: TSqlQuery; ATableName, AFieldName: String; AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;
var el: TExpImpTableObj;
begin
  if AQuery.FieldByName(AFieldName).IsNull then
  begin
    result := false;
    exit;
  end;

  el := AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(ATableName);
  if el = nil then
  begin
    result := false;
    exit;
  end;
  result := el.PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AQuery.FieldByName(AFieldName).AsLongId, AExpImpManager);
end;

procedure TExpImpTableObj.SelectExportIds(AConnection: TecConnection; ASql: string);
var qry: TSqlQuery;
begin
  if not FCanDoExport then
    exit;
  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    qry.SQL.Text := ASql;
    qry.Open();
    while not qry.Eof do
    begin
      SelectIdForExport(qry, TableName + ' / SelectExportIds / ' + ASql, '');
      qry.Next;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

{ TExpImpObjectManager }

function TExpImpObjectManager.AddTable(ATable: String; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>): TExpImpTableObj;
begin
  if GetItemByIdentifier(ATable) <> nil then
    raise EInvalidOperation.Create('TExpImpObjectManager.AddTable: Es existiert bereits ein Eintrag mit ' + ATable);
  result := TExpImpTableObj.Create(self, ATable, ATableIdMapping);
  add(result)
end;

procedure TExpImpObjectManager.CheckChilds;
var idx, idx2: Integer;
begin
  for idx := 0 to Count - 1 do
    for idx2 := 0 to Items[idx].FChildTables.Count - 1 do
      if GetItemByIdentifier(Items[idx].FChildTables[idx2]) = nil  then
        raise EInvalidOperation.Create('TExpImpObjectManager.CheckChilds / ' + Items[idx].TableName +  ': Kein Eintrag zu Child ' + Items[idx].FChildTables[idx2] + ' gefunden!');
end;

destructor TExpImpObjectManager.Destroy;
begin
  if FOwnsExportOptionen then
    FExportOptionen.Free;

  if FOwnsImportOptionen then
    FImportOptionen.Free;
  inherited;
end;

function TExpImpObjectManager.AddTable(ATableObj: TExpImpTableObj): TExpImpTableObj;
begin
  if GetItemByIdentifier(ATableObj.Identifier) <> nil then
    raise EInvalidOperation.Create('TExpImpObjectManager.AddTable: Es existiert bereits ein Eintrag mit ' + ATableObj.Identifier);
  add(ATableObj);

  if ATableObj is TExportEdNachrichtEpv then
    ObjEdepv.RegisterEpvArtObj(ATableObj, TExportEdNachrichtEpv(ATableObj).FEpvArt);
  result := ATableObj;
end;

function TExpImpObjectManager.GetItemByIdentifier(AIdentifier: String): TExpImpTableObj;
var idx: Integer;
begin
  result := nil;
  for idx := 0 to Count - 1 do
    if items[idx].Identifier = AIdentifier then
    begin
      result := items[idx];
      break;
    end;
end;

procedure TExpImpObjectManager.Initialize(AExportOptionen: TExportOptionen; AImportOptionen: TImportOptionen; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);
begin
  Clear;

  if FOwnsExportOptionen then
  begin
    FExportOptionen.Free;
    FExportOptionen := nil;
  end;

  if AExportOptionen = nil then
  begin
    FExportOptionen := TExportOptionen.Create;
    FOwnsExportOptionen := true;
  end
  else
  begin
    FExportOptionen := AExportOptionen;
    FOwnsExportOptionen := false;
  end;

  if FOwnsImportOptionen then
  begin
    FImportOptionen.Free;
    FImportOptionen := nil;
  end;

  if AImportOptionen = nil then
  begin
    FImportOptionen := TImportOptionen.Create;
    FOwnsImportOptionen := True;
  end
  else
  begin
    FImportOptionen := AImportOptionen;
    FOwnsImportOptionen := false;
  end;

  // Muss vor allen Nachricht-Objekten registriert sein!
  FObjEdepv := TExportEdepv(AddTable(TExportEdepv.Create(Self, ATableIdMapping))
   .SetExportEnabled(FExportOptionen.DoNachrichtenExport)); // UTILMD / INV / MSC / REM

  FObjEdepr := AddTable(EDEPR, ATableIdMapping)
   .SetExportEnabled(FExportOptionen.DoNachrichtenExport)
   .SetAutoSelectChildIds(false) // EprIds und EpvIds müssen direkt selektiert werden!
   .AddChildTable(EDEPV);

  FObjENKZB := TExportENKZB.Create(self, ATableIdMapping);
  AddTable(FObjENKZB)
    .AddChildTable(ENKZBZ);

  AddTable(ENKZBZ, ATableIdMapping)
    .AddChildTable(ENKZBB)
    .AddChildTable(ENAPP)
    .AddChildTable(ENSPK);

  AddTable(ENKZBB, ATableIdMapping);
  AddTable(ENAPP, ATableIdMapping);

  FObjENKZE := TExportENKZE.Create(self, ATableIdMapping);
  AddTable(FObjENKZE)
    .AddChildTable(ENKZEZ);

  AddTable(ENKZEZ, ATableIdMapping);

  AddTable(CREPZ, ATableIdMapping)
    .SetExportEnabled(FExportOptionen.DoEexExport)
    .AddChildTable(CREEX)
    .AddChildTable(CREPZI)
    .AddChildTable(CREPZR)
    .AddChildTable(CREPZL)
    .AddChildTable(CREPZP)
    .AddChildTable(CREPZP_H);

  AddTable(ENSPK, ATableIdMapping).DisableImport  // Geht erst dann, wenn die CREPZ auch importiert werden wird.
    .AddChildTable(ENSPKE);

  AddTable(ENSPKE, ATableIdMapping).DisableImport;

  AddTable(CREPZI, ATableIdMapping);

  AddTable(CREPZR, ATableIdMapping);

  AddTable(CREEX, ATableIdMapping)
    .AddChildTable(CREEI)
    .AddChildTable(CREER)
    .AddChildTable(CRAUF)
    .AddChildTable(CREEO)
    .AddChildTable(CREPZP)
    .AddChildTable(CREEL)
    .AddChildTable(CREPZP_H);

  AddTable(CREEO, ATableIdMapping);
  AddTable(CREEI, ATableIdMapping);
  AddTable(CREER, ATableIdMapping);
  AddTable(CREEL, ATableIdMapping);
  AddTable(CREPZL, ATableIdMapping);
  AddTable(CREEP, ATableIdMapping);
  AddTable(CREEP_H, ATableIdMapping);
  AddTable(CREEPP, ATableIdMapping);

  AddTable(CREPZP, ATableIdMapping)
    .AddChildTable(CREPZPP)
    .AddChildTable(CRAUF);

  AddTable(CREPZP_H, ATableIdMapping)
      .AddChildTable(CREPZPP);

  AddTable(CREPZPP, ATableIdMapping);
  AddTable(CRAUF, ATableIdMapping);
  // Todo: Erweitern um EEX-Konfig und EEX-Batch-Tabellen!

   // Inv
  FObjEDINV := TExportEDINV.Create(self, ATableIdMapping);
  AddTable(FObjEDINV)     // Storno -> EDINV
   .SetExportEnabled(FExportOptionen.DoNachrichtenExport and FExportOptionen.DoInvoice)
   .AddChildTable(EDINVP)
   .AddChildTable(EDINVB)
   .AddChildTable(EDINVA)

procedure TExpImpObjectManager.FillParents;
var idx, idx2: Integer;
    master, child: TExpImpTableObj;

begin
  for idx := 0 to Count - 1 do
    items[idx].Parents.Clear;

  for idx := 0 to Count - 1 do
  begin
    master := items[idx];
    if (master.ChildTables.Count > 0) then
    begin
      for idx2 := 0 to master.ChildTables.Count - 1 do
      begin
        child := GetItemByIdentifier(Master.ChildTables[idx2]);
        if (child <> nil) and (child.Parents.IndexOf(master) <0) then
          child.Parents.Add(master);
      end;
    end;
  end;
end;


procedure TExpImpObjectManager.ExportEnabledAnChildsKopieren;

  procedure DisableChilds(AMaster: TExpImpTableObj);
  var child: TExpImpTableObj;
      idx: Integer;
  begin
    for idx := 0 to AMaster.ChildTables.Count -1 do
    begin
      child := GetItemByIdentifier(AMaster.ChildTables[idx]);
      if child <> nil then
      begin
        // nur Disablen, wenn child keine anderen master hat!
        if child.Parents.Count = 1 then
          child.CanDoExport := false;
      end;
    end;
  end;

var idx: Integer;
begin
  for idx := 0 to Count - 1 do
    if not items[idx].CanDoExport and (items[idx].ChildTables.Count > 0) then
      DisableChilds(items[idx]);
end;

procedure TExpImpObjectManager.InitTables(ATables: TTablesObj);
var idx,  tabIdx: Integer;
begin
  for tabIdx := 0 to ATables.Childs.Count - 1 do
    for idx := 0 to Count - 1 do
     items[idx].AddImportValues(TTAbleObj(ATables.Childs.Items[tabIdx]));
end;

{ TExportEDINV }

constructor TExportEDINV.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);
begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, EDINV, ATableIdMapping, TNachrichtenFormat.INVOIC);
  FSelectEdNachricht := nil;
  FSelectEdepv := nil;
  FSelectEdremp := nil;
  AddMpidNummerCol('inv_sender_iln');
  AddMpidNummerCol('inv_empf_iln');
end;

destructor TExportEDINV.Destroy;
begin
  FreeAndNil(FSelectEdNachricht);
  FreeAndNil(FSelectEdepv);
  FreeAndNil(FSelectEdremp);
  inherited;
end;

function TExportEDINV.DoInitialSelectByMlkIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer;
  AMlkIds: TelSimpleLongIdList): Integer;
var qry: TSqlQuery;
    i: integer;
begin
  result := 0;
  if not FOwner.ExportOptionen.DoNachrichtenExport then
    exit;

  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    qry.SQL.Text :='SELECT INV_ID FROM ' + EDINV + ' WHERE MLK_ID = :MLK_ID AND MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(AMndId);
    qry.DBPrepare;
    for i := 0 to AMlkIds.Count - 1 do
    begin
      qry.Params[0].AsLongId := AMlkIds[i];
      try
        qry.Open;
        while not qry.Eof do
        begin
          if SelectIdForExport(qry.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'TExportEDINV.DoInitialSelectByMlkIds(' + IntToStr(AMndId) + '), MlkId: ' + AMlkIds[i].ToStr) then
            inc(result);
          qry.Next;
        end;
      finally
        qry.Close;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

function TExportEDINV.DoInitialSelectByZptIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer;
  AZptIds: TelSimpleLongIdList): Integer;
var qry: TSqlQuery;
    i: integer;
begin
  result := 0;
  if not FOwner.ExportOptionen.DoNachrichtenExport then
    exit;

  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    qry.SQL.Text :='SELECT INV_ID FROM ' + EDINV + ' WHERE ZPT_ID = :ZPT_ID AND MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(AMndId);
    qry.DBPrepare;
    for i := 0 to AZptIds.Count - 1 do
    begin
      qry.Params[0].AsLongId := AZptIds[i];
      try
        qry.Open;
        while not qry.Eof do
        begin
          if SelectIdForExport(qry.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'TExportEDINV.DoInitialSelectByZptIds(' + IntToStr(AMndId) + '), ZptId: ' + AZptIds[i].ToStr) then
            inc(result);
          qry.Next;
        end;
      finally
        qry.Close;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

function TExportEDINV.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport; AId: TLongId; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;
begin
  result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  if FSelectEdNachricht = nil then
  begin
     FSelectEdNachricht := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
     FSelectEdNachricht.SQL.Text := 'SELECT DAT_ID, INV_ID, INV_STORNOINV_ID, INV_SENDER_ILN, INV_EMPF_ILN, MSC_ID FROM ' + EDINV + ' WHERE INV_ID = :INV_ID OR INV_STORNOINV_ID = :INV_ID';
     FSelectEdNachricht.DBPrepare;
  end;

  FSelectEdNachricht.ParamByName('INV_ID').AsLongId := AId;
  try
    FSelectEdNachricht.Open();
    if not FSelectEdNachricht.Eof then
    begin
      if not FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('INV_STORNOINV_ID').IsNull and
         SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('INV_STORNOINV_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDINV.PrepareId, ' + AId.ToStr + ' / INV_STORNOINV_ID') then
         result := true;
      if not FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('INV_ID').IsNull and
         SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('INV_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDINV.PrepareId, ' + AId.ToStr + ' / INV_ID') then
         result := true;

      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDDAT).SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('DAT_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDINV.PrepareId, ' + AId.ToStr + ' / DAT_ID') then
         result := true;

      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDMSC).SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('MSC_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDINV.PrepareId, ' + AId.ToStr + ' / MSC_ID') then
         result := true;

       AKundenExport.AddToMprdList(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('INV_SENDER_ILN').AsString);
       AKundenExport.AddToMprdList(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('INV_EMPF_ILN').AsString);
    end;
  finally
    FSelectEdNachricht.Close;
  end;

  if FSelectEdremp = nil then
  begin
     FSelectEdremp := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
     FSelectEdremp.SQL.Text := 'SELECT DISTINCT REM_ID FROM ' + EDREMP + ' WHERE INV_ID = :INV_ID';
     FSelectEdremp.DBPrepare;
  end;

  FSelectEdremp.ParamByName('INV_ID').AsLongId := AId;
  try
    FSelectEdremp.Open();
    while not FSelectEdremp.Eof do
    begin
      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDREM).SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdremp.FieldByName('REM_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDINV.PrepareId, ' + AId.ToStr + ' / REM_ID') then
         result := true;
      FSelectEdremp.Next;
    end;
  finally
    FSelectEdremp.Close;
  end;
end;

function TExportEDINV.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj;
  AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
var orgValue: String;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;
  if AColumn.Columnname = 'INV_METERINGCODE' then
  begin
    orgValue := AColumn.Value;
    if length(AColumn.Value) = MeteringCodeLaenge then
      ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMeteringCode(AColumn.Value)
     else
      ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMaloCode(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> orgValue;
  end
    else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'INV_NACHRICHTENREFERENZNR') then
  begin
    orgValue := AColumn.Value;
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 14);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> orgValue;
  end
    else
  if ((AColumn.Columnname = 'INV_NACHRICHTNR') or
      (AColumn.Columnname = 'INV_STORNOREF_NR')) then
  begin
    orgValue := AColumn.Value;
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedRechnungsNr(AColumn.Value, 35);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> orgValue;
  end
    else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'INV_ANWEND_REF_NR') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'INV_ORD_REF_NR') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'INV_MSC_REF_NR') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'INV_REFERENZNR') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'INV_SSQ_REF_NR') then
  begin
    orgValue := AColumn.Value;
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 70);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> orgValue;
  end
  else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'INV_KND_NR') then
  begin
    orgValue := AColumn.Value;
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedKndNr1(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> orgValue;
  end;
  // ggf. noch KndNr1 ersetzen
end;

  if result then
    exit;
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'REM_NACHRICHTNR') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 35);
    result := ANewValue <> AColumn.Value;
  end
   else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'REM_NACHRICHTENREFERENZNR') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'REM_DATEIAUSTAUSCHREFERENZ') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 14);
    result := ANewValue <> AColumn.Value;
  end
   else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'REM_REFERENZNR') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 70);
    result := ANewValue <> AColumn.Value;
  end
end;

function TExportEDORD.DoInitialSelectByMlkIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer; AMlkIds: TelSimpleLongIdList): Integer;
var
  qry: TSqlQuery;
  i: integer;
begin
  result := 0;
  if not FOwner.ExportOptionen.DoNachrichtenExport then
    exit;

  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    qry.SQL.Text :='SELECT ORD_ID FROM ' + EDORD + ' WHERE MLK_ID = :MLK_ID AND MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(AMndId);
    qry.DBPrepare;
    for i := 0 to AMlkIds.Count - 1 do
    begin
      qry.Params[0].AsLongId := AMlkIds[i];
      try
        qry.Open;
        while not qry.Eof do
        begin
          if SelectIdForExport(qry.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'TExportEDORD.DoInitialSelectByMlkIds(' + IntToStr(AMndId) + '), MlkId: ' + AMlkIds[i].ToStr) then
            inc(result);
          qry.Next;
        end;
      finally
        qry.Close;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

function TExportEDORD.DoInitialSelectByZptIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer; AZptIds: TelSimpleLongIdList): Integer;
var
  qry: TSqlQuery;
  i: integer;
begin
  result := 0;
  if not FOwner.ExportOptionen.DoNachrichtenExport then
    exit;

  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    qry.SQL.Text :='SELECT ORD_ID FROM ' + EDORD + ' WHERE ZPT_ID = :ZPT_ID AND MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(AMndId);
    qry.DBPrepare;
    for i := 0 to AZptIds.Count - 1 do
    begin
      qry.Params[0].AsLongId := AZptIds[i];
      try
        qry.Open;
        while not qry.Eof do
        begin
          if SelectIdForExport(qry.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'TExportEDORD.DoInitialSelectByZptIds(' + IntToStr(AMndId) + '), ZptId: ' + AZptIds[i].ToStr) then
            inc(result);
          qry.Next;
        end;
      finally
        qry.Close;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

begin
  result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  if FSelectEdNachricht = nil then
  begin
     FSelectEdNachricht := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
     FSelectEdNachricht.SQL.Text := 'SELECT DAT_ID, ORD_ID, ORD_SENDER_ILN, ORD_EMPF_ILN, MSC_ID FROM ' + EDORD + ' WHERE ORD_ID = :ORD_ID';
     FSelectEdNachricht.DBPrepare;
  end;

  FSelectEdNachricht.ParamByName('ORD_ID').AsLongId := AId;
  try
    FSelectEdNachricht.Open();
    if not FSelectEdNachricht.Eof then
    begin
      if not FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('ORD_ID').IsNull and
         SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('ORD_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDORD.PrepareId, ' + AId.ToStr + ' / ORD_ID') then
         result := true;

      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDDAT).SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('DAT_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDORD.PrepareId, ' + AId.ToStr + ' / DAT_ID') then
         result := true;

      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDMSC).SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('MSC_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDORD.PrepareId, ' + AId.ToStr + ' / MSC_ID') then
         result := true;

       AKundenExport.AddToMprdList(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('ORD_SENDER_ILN').AsString);
       AKundenExport.AddToMprdList(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('ORD_EMPF_ILN').AsString);
    end;
  finally
    FSelectEdNachricht.Close;
  end;
end;

function TExportEDORD.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj; AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
var
  orgValue: String;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;
  if AColumn.Columnname = 'ORD_METERINGCODE' then
  begin
    orgValue := AColumn.Value;
    if length(AColumn.Value) = MeteringCodeLaenge then
      ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMeteringCode(AColumn.Value)
     else
      ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMaloCode(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> orgValue;
  end
  else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'ORD_NACHRICHTENREFERENZNR') then
  begin
    orgValue := AColumn.Value;
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 14);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> orgValue;
  end
    else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'ORD_NACHRICHTNR') then
  begin
    orgValue := AColumn.Value;
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedRechnungsNr(AColumn.Value, 35);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> orgValue;
  end
    else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'ord_refnr') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'ord_vorgangrefnr') then
  begin
    orgValue := AColumn.Value;
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 70);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> orgValue;
  end;
end;

function TExportEDMSC.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport;
  AId: TLongId; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;
begin
  result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  if FSelectEdNachricht = nil then
  begin
     FSelectEdNachricht := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
     FSelectEdNachricht.SQL.Text := 'SELECT DAT_ID, MSC_ID, MSC_SENDER_ILN, MSC_EMPF_ILN, MSC_ID_STORNOREF FROM ' + EDMSC  + ' WHERE MSC_ID = :MSC_ID OR MSC_ID_STORNOREF = :MSC_ID';
     FSelectEdNachricht.DBPrepare;
  end;

  FSelectEdNachricht.ParamByName('MSC_ID').AsLongId := AId;
  try
    FSelectEdNachricht.open;
    if not FSelectEdNachricht.Eof then
    begin
      if SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('MSC_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDMSC, ' + AId.ToStr + ' MSC_ID') then
         result := true;

      if SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('MSC_ID_STORNOREF').AsLongId, 'TExportEDMSC, ' + AId.ToStr + ' MSC_ID_STORNOREF') then
         result := true;

      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDDAT).SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('DAT_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDMSC, ' + AId.ToStr + ' DAT_ID') then
         result := true;


       AKundenExport.AddToMprdList(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('MSC_SENDER_ILN').AsString);
       AKundenExport.AddToMprdList(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('MSC_EMPF_ILN').AsString);
    end;
  finally
    FSelectEdNachricht.Close;
  end;
end;

function TExportEDMSC.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj;
  AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'MSC_NACHRICHTENREFERENZNR') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'MSC_DATEIAUSTAUSCHREFERENZ') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 14);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
   else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'MSC_ORD_REF_NR') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'MSC_STORNOREF_NR') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'MSC_REFERENZNR') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'MSC_UTLV_REF_NR') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 70);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
   else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'MSC_ID_STORNOREF') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'MSC_ID_REF') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 12);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
   else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'MSC_NACHRICHTNR') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'MSC_UTLVREFERENZNR') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 35);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end;
end;

{ TExportEdNachrichtEpv }

procedure TExportEdNachrichtEpv.AddMpidNummerCol(AColName: String);
begin
  AColName := UpperCase(AColName);
  if FSenderEmpfFelder.IndexOf(AColName)<0 then
    FSenderEmpfFelder.Add(AColName);
end;

constructor TExportEdNachrichtEpv.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATable: String;  ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>; ANachrichtenformat: TNachrichtenFormat);
begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, ATable, ATableIdMapping);
  Init(Ord(ANachrichtenformat));
end;

constructor TExportEdNachrichtEpv.CreateWithoutEpvArt(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATable: String;
  ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);
begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, ATable, ATableIdMapping);
  init(-1);
end;

destructor TExportEdNachrichtEpv.Destroy;
begin
  FreeAndNil(FSelectEdepv);
  FreeAndNil(FSenderEmpfFelder);
  inherited;
end;

function TExportEdNachrichtEpv.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj; AKundenImport: IKundenImport;
  var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;

  if FSenderEmpfFelder.IndexOf(AColumn.Columnname)>=0 then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMpidNummer(AColumn.Value);
    result := true;
  end;
end;

procedure TExportEdNachrichtEpv.Init(AEpvArt: Integer);
begin
  FEpvArt := AEpvArt;
  FSenderEmpfFelder := TStringList.Create;
  FSelectEdepv := nil;
end;

function TExportEdNachrichtEpv.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport;
  AId: TLongId; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;
begin
  result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  if FEpvArt >= 0 then
  begin
    if FSelectEdepv = nil then
    begin
       FSelectEdepv := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
       FSelectEdepv.SQL.Text := 'SELECT EPR_ID, EPV_ID FROM ' + EDEPV + ' WHERE EDI_ID = :EDI_ID' +
                 IfThen(FEpvArt >= 0, ' AND EPV_ART = :EPV_ART', '');
       FSelectEdepv.DBPrepare;
    end;

    FSelectEdepv.ParamByName('EDI_ID').AsLongId := AId;
    FSelectEdepv.ParamByName('EPV_ART').AsInteger := FEpvArt;
    try
      FSelectEdepv.open;
      while not FSelectEdepv.Eof do
      begin
        if AExpImpManager.ObjEdepr.SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdepv.FieldByName('EPR_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEdNachrichtEpv, ' + TableName + ' / '  + AId.ToStr) then
           result := true;
        if AExpImpManager.ObjEdepv.SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdepv.FieldByName('EPV_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEdNachrichtEpv, ' + TableName + ' / '  + AId.ToStr) then
           result := true;
        FSelectEdepv.Next;
      end;
    finally
      FSelectEdepv.Close;
    end;
  end;
end;

{ TExportEDUTLV }

constructor TExportEDUTLV.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);
begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, EDUTLV, ATableIdMapping, TNachrichtenformat.UTILMD);
  FSelectEdNachricht := nil;
  AddMpidNummerCol('utlv_prev_lief_iln');
  AddMpidNummerCol('utlv_msb_iln');
  AddMpidNummerCol('utlv_lif_iln');
end;

destructor TExportEDUTLV.Destroy;
begin
  FreeAndNil(FSelectEdNachricht);
  inherited;
end;

function TExportEDUTLV.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport;
  AId: TLongId; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;
begin
  result := false;
  if not CanDoExport then
    exit;

  if FSelectEdNachricht = nil then
  begin
     FSelectEdNachricht := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
     FSelectEdNachricht.SQL.Text := 'SELECT UTL_ID FROM ' + EDUTLV  + ' WHERE UTLV_ID = :UTLV_ID';
     FSelectEdNachricht.DBPrepare;
  end;

  result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);

  FSelectEdNachricht.ParamByName('UTLV_ID').AsLongId := AId;
  try
    FSelectEdNachricht.Open();
    if not FSelectEdNachricht.Eof then
    begin
      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDUTL).SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('UTL_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDUTLV, ' + AId.ToStr) then
         result := true;
    end;
  finally
    FSelectEdNachricht.Close;
  end;
end;

function TExportEDUTLV.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj;
  AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;

  result := TExportEDULRefReplace.DoGetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;

  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'UTLV_REFERENZ_ID') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'UTLV_ANFRAGEREF_ID') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'UTLV_VORGANGS_ID') then
  begin
    ANewValue:= AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
   else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'UTLV_ORD_REF_NR') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'UTLV_REF_NACHR_ANTWAUFBEST') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 70);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end;

end;

function TExportEDUTL.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport;
  AId: TLongId; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;
begin
  result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  if FSelectEdNachricht = nil then
  begin
     FSelectEdNachricht := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
     FSelectEdNachricht.SQL.Text := 'SELECT DAT_ID, UTL_SENDER_ILN, UTL_EMPF_ILN FROM ' + EDUTL + ' WHERE UTL_ID = :UTL_ID';
     FSelectEdNachricht.DBPrepare;
  end;

  FSelectEdNachricht.ParamByName('UTL_ID').AsLongId := AId;
  try
    FSelectEdNachricht.Open;
    if not FSelectEdNachricht.Eof then
    begin
      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDDAT).SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('DAT_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDUTL, ' + AId.ToStr) then
         result := true;
       AKundenExport.AddToMprdList(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('UTL_SENDER_ILN').AsString);
       AKundenExport.AddToMprdList(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('UTL_EMPF_ILN').AsString);
    end;
  finally
    FSelectEdNachricht.Close;
  end;
end;

begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, ENKZB, ATableIdMapping);
  FSelectEdepv := nil;

  FreeAndNil(FSelectEdepv);
  inherited;
end;

function TExportENKZB.DoInitialSelectByKndIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer;
  AKndIds: TelSimpleLongIdList): Integer;
var qry: TSqlQuery;
    i: integer;
begin
  result := 0;
  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    qry.SQL.Text :='SELECT KZB_ID FROM ' + ENKZB + ' WHERE KND_ID = :KND_ID AND MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(AMndId);
    qry.DBPrepare;
    for i := 0 to AKndIds.Count - 1 do
    begin
      qry.Params[0].AsLongId := AKndIds[i];
      try
        qry.Open;
        while not qry.Eof do
        begin
          if SelectIdForExport(qry.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'TExportENKZB.DoInitialSelectByKndIds, ' + AKndIds[i].ToStr) then
            inc(result);
          qry.Next;
        end;
      finally
        qry.Close;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

function TExportENKZB.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport;
  AId: TLongId; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;
begin
  result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  if FOwner.ExportOptionen.DoNachrichtenExport then
  begin
    if FSelectEdepv = nil then
    begin
       FSelectEdepv := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
       FSelectEdepv.SQL.Text := 'SELECT epr.EPR_ID, EDI_ID FROM ' + EDEPR + ' epr JOIN ' + EDEPV + ' epv on epv.epr_id = epr.epr_id ' + CR_LF +
                                ' WHERE KZB_ID = :KZB_ID ' + CR_LF +
                                ' AND EPV_ART = 1'; // EDUTLV
       FSelectEdepv.DBPrepare;
    end;

    FSelectEdepv.ParamByName('KZB_ID').AsLongId := AId;
    try
      FSelectEdepv.Open();
      while not FSelectEdepv.Eof do
      begin
        if AExpImpManager.ObjEdepr.SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdepv.FieldByName('EPR_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportENKZB, ' + AId.ToStr) then
           result := true;
        if AExpImpManager.ObjEdepv.SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdepv.FieldByName('EDI_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportENKZB, ' + AId.ToStr) then
           result := true;
        if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDUTLV).SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdepv.FieldByName('EDI_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportENKZB, ' + AId.ToStr) then
           result := true;
        FSelectEdepv.Next;
      end;
    finally
      FSelectEdepv.Close;
    end;
  end;
end;

function TExportENKZE.DoInitialSelectByKndIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer;

var qry: TSqlQuery;
    i: integer;

  result := 0;

    qry.SQL.Text :='SELECT KZE_ID FROM ' + ENKZE + ' WHERE KND_ID = :KND_ID AND MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(AMndId);
    qry.DBPrepare;
    for i := 0 to AKndIds.Count - 1 do

      try

function TExportENKZE.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport;
  AId: TLongId; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;
var kndExpObj: TExpImpTableObj;
begin
  result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  if FOwner.ExportOptionen.DoNachrichtenExport then
  begin
    if FSelectEdepv = nil then
    begin
       FSelectEdepv := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
       FSelectEdepv.SQL.Text := 'SELECT epr.EPR_ID, EDI_ID FROM ' + EDEPR + ' epr JOIN ' + EDEPV + ' epv on epv.epr_id = epr.epr_id ' + CR_LF +
                                ' WHERE KZE_ID = :KZE_ID ' + CR_LF +
                                ' AND EPV_ART = 1'; // EDUTLV
       FSelectEdepv.DBPrepare;
    end;

    FSelectEdepv.ParamByName('KZE_ID').AsLongId := AId;
    try
      FSelectEdepv.Open;
      while not FSelectEdepv.Eof do
      begin
        if AExpImpManager.ObjEdepr.SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdepv.FieldByName('EPR_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportENKZE, ' + AId.ToStr) then
           result := true;
        if AExpImpManager.ObjEdepv.SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdepv.FieldByName('EDI_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportENKZE, ' + AId.ToStr) then
           result := true;
        if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDUTLV).SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdepv.FieldByName('EDI_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportENKZE, ' + AId.ToStr) then
           result := true;
        FSelectEdepv.Next;
      end;
    finally
      FSelectEdepv.Close;
    end;
  end;

  if FSelectKze = nil then
  begin
     FSelectKze := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
     FSelectKze.SQL.Text := 'SELECT KZE_ID, KZE_NACHFOLGERKND_ID, KZE_NACHFOLGERKZB_ID FROM ' + ENKZE + ' WHERE KZE_ID = :KZE_ID';
     FSelectKze.DBPrepare;
  end;

  FSelectKze.ParamByName('KZE_ID').AsLongId := AId;
  try
    FSelectKze.Open;
    while not FSelectKze.Eof do
    begin
      kndExpObj :=  AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(ENKND);
      if (kndExpObj <> nil) and kndExpObj.SelectIdForExport(FSelectKze.FieldByName('KZE_NACHFOLGERKND_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportENKZE, ' + AId.ToStr) then
         result := true;
      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(ENKZB).SelectIdForExport(FSelectKze.FieldByName('KZE_NACHFOLGERKZB_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportENKZE, ' + AId.ToStr) then
         result := true;
      FSelectKze.Next;
    end;
  finally
    FSelectKze.Close;
  end;
end;

function TExportEDUTZP.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport;
  AId: TLongId; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;
begin
  result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  if FSelectTreIdSreId = nil then
  begin
     FSelectTreIdSreId := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
     FSelectTreIdSreId.SQL.Text := 'SELECT SRE_ID, TRE_ID FROM ' + EDUTZP + ' WHERE UTZP_ID = :UTZP_ID';
     FSelectTreIdSreId.DBPrepare;
  end;

  FSelectTreIdSreId.ParamByName('UTZP_ID').AsLongId := AId;
  try
    FSelectTreIdSreId.Open();
    if not FSelectTreIdSreId.Eof then
    begin
      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(ENTRE).SelectIdForExport(FSelectTreIdSreId.FieldByName('TRE_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDUTZP, ' + AId.ToStr) then
         result := true;
      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(ENSRE).SelectIdForExport(FSelectTreIdSreId.FieldByName('SRE_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDUTZP, ' + AId.ToStr) then
         result := true;
    end;
  finally
    FSelectTreIdSreId.Close;
  end;
end;

function TExportEDUTZP.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj;
  AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;

  if AColumn.Columnname = 'UTZP_METERINGCODE' then
  begin
    if length(EntferneHochkomma(AColumn.Value)) = MeteringCodeLaenge then
      ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMeteringCode(AColumn.Value)
     else
      ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMaloCode(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
   else
  result := TExportEDULRefReplace.DoGetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
end;

{ TTableIdMapping }

procedure TTableIdMapping.Clear;
begin
  IdMapping.Clear;
end;

constructor TTableIdMapping.Create(ATableName, AIdFieldName, ASequenceName: String);
begin
  FTableName := ATableName;
  FIdFieldName := AIdFieldName;
  FSequenceName := ASequenceName;
  FIdMapping := TIdMapping.Create;
  FDTable := TDBModelUtils.GetTables.Find(TableName);
  if FDTable = nil then
    raise EInvalidOperation.Create('Tabelle ' + ATableName + ' nicht in DB-Modell gefunden');
end;

destructor TTableIdMapping.Destroy;
begin
  IdMapping.Free;
end;

function TTableIdMapping.IsRelevant(ATableName, AColumnName: String): Boolean;
var i: Integer;
begin
  result := false;
  if (ATableName = TableName) and (AColumnName = IdFieldName) then
  begin
    result := true;
    exit;
  end;
  if FDTable <> nil then
  begin
    for i := 0 to FDTable.ReferencedBy.Count - 1 do
      if FDTable.ReferencedBy[i].FKTableSchema + '.' + FDTable.ReferencedBy[i].FKTable = ATableName then
      begin
        if length(FDTable.ReferencedBy[i].FKFieldnames) <> 1 then
            raise EInvalidOperation.Create('length(FDTable.ReferencedBy[i].FKFieldnames) <> 1  wird nicht unterstützt!');
        if (AColumnName = FDTable.ReferencedBy[i].FKFieldnames[0]) then
        begin
          result := true;
          break;
        end;
      end;
  end;
end;

{ TExportEdepv }

function TExportEdepv.CanDoImport(ATable: TTAbleObj; AKundenImport: IKundenImport): Boolean;
var column: TColumnObj;
    newValue: String;
begin
  result := inherited CanDoImport(ATable, AKundenImport);
  if result then
  begin
    column := ATable.GetColumnByName('EPV_ART', false);
    if column <> nil then
    begin
      result := FEpvArt2Obj.ContainsKey(StrToIntDef(column.Value, -1));
      if result then
      begin
        result := GetNewColumnValue(ATable, ATable.GetColumnByName('EDI_ID'), AKundenImport, newValue, false);
        // SonderLocke: Nur Importieren, falls "Übersetzbar"
      end;
    end
    else
      result := False;
  end;
end;

constructor TExportEdepv.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);
begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, EDEPV, ATableIdMapping);
  FEpvArt2Obj := TelIntegerObjectMap<TExpImpTableObj>.Create(False);
end;

destructor TExportEdepv.Destroy;
begin
  FEpvArt2Obj.Free;
  inherited;
end;

function TExportEdepv.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj;
  AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
var column: TColumnObj;
    orgId, id: Int64;
    art: Integer;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;

  if ATable.Tablename <> TableName then
    exit;

  if AColumn.Columnname <> 'EDI_ID' then
    exit;

  orgId := StrToIntDef(AColumn.Value, NULL_INTEGER);
  id := orgId;
  column := ATable.GetColumnByName('EPV_ART');
  art := StrToIntDef(column.Value, -1);
  if FEpvArt2Obj.ContainsKey(art) then
     result := FEpvArt2Obj.Values[art].TableIdMapping.IdMapping.TryGetValue(orgId, id);

  if result then
    ANewValue := IntToStr(id);
end;

procedure TExportEdepv.RegisterEpvArtObj(AObj: TExpImpTableObj; AEpvArt: Integer);
begin
  if (AObj = nil) or (AEpvArt < 0) then exit;

  if FEpvArt2Obj.ContainsKey(AEpvArt) then
    raise EInvalidArgument.Create('RegisterEpvArtObj: Es existiert bereits ein Eintrag für die EpvArt ' + IntTostr(AEpvArt));
  FEpvArt2Obj.Add(AEpvArt, AObj)
end;

{ TExportEDMSZP }

begin
  inherited CreateWithoutEpvArt(AOwner, EDMSZP, ATableIdMapping);
  FAutoPrepareParentIds := true;
end;

function TExportEDMSZP.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj;
  AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;

  if AColumn.Columnname = 'MSZP_METERINGCODE' then
  begin
    if length(EntferneHochkomma(AColumn.Value)) = MeteringCodeLaenge then
      ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMeteringCode(AColumn.Value)
     else
      ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMaloCode(AColumn.Value);

    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end;
end;

{ TExportEDDAT }

function TExportEDDAT.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport;
  AId: TLongId; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): Boolean;
begin
  result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  if PrepareParentId(AConnection, AId, AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDCTR), 'CTR_ID') then
    result := true;
end;

constructor TExportEDDAT.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);
begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, EDDAT, ATableIdMapping, TNachrichtenFormat.EDIDATEI);
  AddMpidNummerCol('dat_sender_iln');
  AddMpidNummerCol('dat_empf_iln');
end;

function TExportEDDAT.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj;
  AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);

  if result then
    exit;

  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'DAT_DATEINAME') or
     (AColumn.Columnname = 'DAT_DATEINAME_SUCHFELD') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedDateiName(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end;
end;

{ TExportEDULRefReplace }

class function TExportEDULRefReplace.DoGetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj;
  AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
begin
  result := false;
  if AnsiEndsText('_METERINGCODE', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_REFMELO', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_REF_MELO', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_METERINGCODE2', AColumn.Columnname) then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMeteringCode(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
    else
  if AnsiEndsText('_MARKTLOKATION', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_REF_MALO', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_REFMALO', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_REFTRANCHE', AColumn.Columnname) then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMaloCode(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
   else
  if AnsiEndsText('_REF_TR', AColumn.Columnname)  then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedTreCode(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
   else
  if AnsiEndsText('_REF_NELO', AColumn.Columnname)  then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedNeloCode(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
   else
  if AnsiEndsText('_REF_SR', AColumn.Columnname)  then
  begin
   ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedSreCode(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
   else
  if AnsiEndsText('_ZAEHLERNUMMER', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('SUCHF_ZAENR', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_REFZAENR', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_REFMUNR', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_REFSMG', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('SMGW_NR', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('UTMG_NUMMER', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_GERAETENUMMER', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_GERAETENUMMER1', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_GERAETENUMMER2', AColumn.Columnname) or
     AnsiEndsText('_GERAETENUMMER3', AColumn.Columnname) then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedGeraeteNr(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
end;

function TExportEDULRefReplace.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj;
  AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;
  result := DoGetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
end;

begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, ENTRE, ATableIdMapping);
  FSelectSreUndAbn := nil;

destructor TExportENTRE.Destroy;

  FreeAndNil(FSelectSreUndAbn);
  inherited;
end;

  AMlkIds: TelSimpleLongIdList): Integer;

  result := DoInitialSelect(AConnection, 'SELECT TRE_ID FROM ' + ENTRMA + ' WHERE MLK_ID = :MLK_ID', 'MLK_ID', AMlkIds)
end;

end;

function TExportENTRE.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj;
  AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;

  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'TRE_TR_ID')  then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedTreCode(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end;
end;

function TExportENTRE.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport; AId: TLongId;
  AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;

begin
  Result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  if FSelectSreUndAbn = nil then
  begin
     FSelectSreUndAbn := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
     FSelectSreUndAbn.SQL.Text := 'SELECT tre.ABN_ID, trd.NLK_ID, trd.SRE_ID FROM ' + ENTRE + ' tre JOIN ' + ENTRD + ' trd ON tre.tre_id = trd.tre_id WHERE tre.TRE_ID = :TRE_ID';
     FSelectSreUndAbn.DBPrepare;
  end;

  FSelectSreUndAbn.ParamByName('TRE_ID').AsLongId := AId;
  try
    FSelectSreUndAbn.Open();
    if not FSelectSreUndAbn.Eof then
    begin
      if SelectOther(FSelectSreUndAbn, ENABN, 'ABN_ID', AConnection, AKundenExport, AExpImpManager) then
        result := true;
      if SelectOther(FSelectSreUndAbn, ENNLK, 'NLK_ID', AConnection, AKundenExport, AExpImpManager) then
        result := true;
      if SelectOther(FSelectSreUndAbn, ENSRE, 'SRE_ID', AConnection, AKundenExport, AExpImpManager) then
        result := true;
    end;
  finally
    FSelectSreUndAbn.Close;
  end;
end;

{ TExportENSRE }

constructor TExportENSRE.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);
begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, ENSRE, ATableIdMapping);
  FSelectAbn := nil;
end;

destructor TExportENSRE.Destroy;
begin
  FreeAndNil(FSelectAbn);
  inherited;
end;

function TExportENSRE.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj;
  AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'SRE_SR_ID')  then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedSreCode(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end;
end;

function TExportENSRE.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport; AId: TLongId;
  AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;

begin
  Result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  if FSelectAbn = nil then
  begin
     FSelectAbn := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
     FSelectAbn.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ABN_ID FROM ' + ENSRE + ' WHERE SRE_ID = :SRE_ID';
     FSelectAbn.DBPrepare;
  end;

  FSelectAbn.ParamByName('SRE_ID').AsLongId := AId;
  try
    FSelectAbn.Open();
    if not FSelectAbn.Eof then
    begin
      if SelectOther(FSelectAbn,  ENABN, 'ABN_ID', AConnection, AKundenExport, AExpImpManager) then
        result := true;
    end;
  finally
    FSelectAbn.Close;
  end;
end;

function TExportENSRE.DoInitialSelectByAbnIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AAbnIds: TelSimpleLongIdList): Integer;
begin
  result := DoInitialSelect(AConnection, 'SELECT SRE_ID FROM ' + ENSRE + ' WHERE ABN_ID = :ABN_ID', 'ABN_ID', AAbnIds);
end;

{ TExportENZptAbh }

function TExportENZptAbh.AddZptIdFieldName(AZptIdFieldName: String): TExportENZptAbh;
begin
  AZptIdFieldName := UpperCase(AZptIdFieldName);
  if (AZptIdFieldName <> '') and
     (FZptIdFieldNames.IndexOf(AZptIdFieldName) <0) then
     FZptIdFieldNames.Add(AZptIdFieldName);
  result := Self;
end;

constructor TExportENZptAbh.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATableName: String;
  ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>; AZptIdFieldName: String);
begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, ATableName, ATableIdMapping);
  FZptIdFieldNames := TStringList.Create;
  AddZptIdFieldName(AZptIdFieldName);
end;

destructor TExportENZptAbh.Destroy;
begin
  FZptIdFieldNames.Free;
  inherited;
end;

function TExportENZptAbh.DoInitialSelectByZptIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer;
  AZptIds: TelSimpleIntegerList): Integer;
var i: integer;
begin
  result := 0;
  for i := 0 to FZptIdFieldNames.Count - 1 do
    result := result +  DoInitialSelect(AConnection,  'SELECT ' + FIdFeldName + ' FROM ' + FTableName + ' WHERE ' + FZptIdFieldNames[i] + ' = :ZPT_ID', 'ZPT_ID', AZptIds);
end;

{ TExportEDCTR }

function TExportEDCTR.CanDoImport(ATable: TTAbleObj; AKundenImport: IKundenImport): Boolean;
begin
  result := false; // Hierfür bräuchten wir ein "INSERT EDDAT OHNE CTR_ID mit späterem UPDATE CTR_ID". Todo MKI inherited CanDoImport(ATable);
end;

constructor TExportEDCTR.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);
begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, EDCTR, ATableIdMapping, TNachrichtenFormat.CONTRL);
  AddMpidNummerCol('ctr_sender_iln');
  AddMpidNummerCol('ctr_empf_iln');
  AddMpidNummerCol('ctr_ref_sender_iln');
  AddMpidNummerCol('ctr_ref_empf_iln');
end;

function TExportEDCTR.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj;
  AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;

  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'CTR_DATEINAME') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedDateiName(AColumn.Value);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
  else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'CTR_DATEIAUSTAUSCHREFERENZ') or
      (AColumn.Columnname = 'CTR_REF_DATEIAUSTAUSCHREF') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 35);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
   else
  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'CTR_NACHRICHTENREFERENZNR') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 14);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
end;

  { TExportEDQUO }
constructor TExportEDQUO.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);
begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, EDQUO, ATableIdMapping, TNachrichtenformat.QUOTES);
  FSelectEdNachricht := nil;
  AddMpidNummerCol('quo_sender_iln');
  AddMpidNummerCol('quo_empf_iln');
end;

destructor TExportEDQUO.Destroy;
begin
  FreeAndNil(FSelectEdNachricht);
  inherited;
end;

function TExportEDQUO.DoInitialSelectByMlkIds(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer; AMlkIds: TelSimpleLongIdList): Integer;
var
  Qry: TSqlQuery;
  I: Integer;
begin
  Result := 0;
  if not FOwner.ExportOptionen.DoNachrichtenExport then
    exit;

  Qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    Qry.SQL.Text :='SELECT QUO_ID FROM ' + EDQUO + ' WHERE MLK_ID = :MLK_ID AND MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(AMndId);
    Qry.DBPrepare;
    for I := 0 to AMlkIds.Count - 1 do
    begin
      Qry.Params[0].AsLongId := AMlkIds[I];
      try
        Qry.Open;
        while not Qry.Eof do
        begin
          if SelectIdForExport(Qry.Fields[0].AsLongId, 'TExportEDQUO, DoInitialSelectByMlkIds,  ' + AMlkIds[i].ToStr) then
            inc(result);
          Qry.Next;
        end;
      finally
        Qry.Close;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    Qry.Free;
  end;
end;

function TExportEDQUO.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport; AId: TLongId; AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): Boolean;
begin
  Result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  if FSelectEdNachricht = nil then
  begin
     FSelectEdNachricht := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
     FSelectEdNachricht.SQL.Text := 'SELECT DAT_ID, QUO_ID, QUO_SENDER_ILN, QUO_EMPF_ILN FROM ' + EDQUO + ' WHERE QUO_ID = :QUO_ID';
     FSelectEdNachricht.DBPrepare;
  end;

  FSelectEdNachricht.ParamByName('QUO_ID').AsLongId := AId;
  try
    FSelectEdNachricht.Open();
    if not FSelectEdNachricht.Eof then
    begin
      if not FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('QUO_ID').IsNull and SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('QUO_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDQUO,  ' + AId.ToStr) then
         Result := True;

      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(EDDAT).SelectIdForExport(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('DAT_ID').AsLongId, 'TExportEDQUO,  ' + AId.ToStr) then
         Result := True;

       AKundenExport.AddToMprdList(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('QUO_SENDER_ILN').AsString);
       AKundenExport.AddToMprdList(FSelectEdNachricht.FieldByName('QUO_EMPF_ILN').AsString);
    end;
  finally
    FSelectEdNachricht.Close;
  end;
end;

function TExportEDQUO.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj; AKundenImport: IKundenImport; Var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
begin
  Result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if Result then
    Exit;

  if AColumn.Columnname = 'QUO_METERINGCODE' then
  begin
    if length(EntferneHochkomma(AColumn.Value)) = MeteringCodeLaenge then
      ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMeteringCode(AColumn.Value)
     else
      ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedMaloCode(AColumn.Value);
    Result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
  else if (AColumn.Columnname = 'QUO_NACHRICHTENREFERENZNR') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 14);
    Result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end
  else if (AColumn.Columnname = 'QUO_NACHRICHTNR') then
  begin
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedEdiRef(AColumn.Value, 70);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> ANewValue;
  end;
end;

{ TExportEDREMP }

constructor TExportEDREMP.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);
begin
  inherited CreateWithoutEpvArt(AOwner, EDREMP, ATableIdMapping);
end;

function TExportEDREMP.GetNewColumnValue(ATable: TTAbleObj; AColumn: TColumnObj; AKundenImport: IKundenImport;
  var ANewValue: String; AForInsert: Boolean): Boolean;
var orgValue: String;
begin
  result := inherited GetNewColumnValue(ATable, AColumn, AKundenImport, ANewValue, AForInsert);
  if result then
    exit;

  if (AColumn.Columnname = 'REMP_RECHNUNGS_NR') then
  begin
    orgValue := AColumn.Value;
    ANewValue := AKundenImport.GetMappedRechnungsNr(AColumn.Value, 35);
    result := Acolumn.Value <> orgValue;
  end;
end;

{ TExportENLOKSA }

constructor TExportENLOKSA.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);
begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, ENLOKSA, ATableIdMapping);
  FSelectENLOKSO := nil;
end;

destructor TExportENLOKSA.Destroy;
begin
  Freeandnil(FSelectENLOKSO);
  inherited;
end;

function TExportENLOKSA.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport; AId: TLongId;
  AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;
begin
  result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);
  // Automatisch die entsprechenden LokSo-Objekte mit selektieren

  if FSelectENLOKSO = nil then
  begin
    FSelectENLOKSO := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
    FSelectENLOKSO.SQL.Text := 'SELECT lokso_id ' + CR_LF +
                               ' FROM ' + ENLOKSA +  CR_LF +
                               ' WHERE LOKSA_ID = :LOKSA_ID';
    FSelectENLOKSO.DBPrepare;
  end;

  try
    FSelectENLOKSO.ParamByName('LOKSA_ID').AsLongId := AId;
    FSelectENLOKSO.Open;
    while not FSelectENLOKSO.Eof do
    begin
      if AExpImpManager.GetItemByIdentifier(ENLOKSO).PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, FSelectENLOKSO.FieldByName('LOKSO_ID').AsLongIdZero, AExpImpManager) then
         result := True;
      FSelectENLOKSO.Next;
    end;
  finally
    FSelectENLOKSO.Close;
  end;
end;

{ TExportENLOKB }

constructor TExportENLOKB.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);

  inherited Create(AOwner, ENLOKB, ATableIdMapping);

{ TExportENLOKBE }

constructor TExportENLOKBE.Create(AOwner: TExpImpObjectManager; ATableIdMapping: TelStringObjectMap<TTableIdMapping>);
begin
  inherited Create(AOwner, ENLOKBE, ATableIdMapping);
end;

destructor TExportENLOKBE.Destroy;
begin
  FreeAndNil(FSelectLokSo);
  inherited;
end;

function TExportENLOKBE.PrepareId(AConnection: TecConnection; AKundenExport: IKundenExport; AId: TLongId;
  AExpImpManager: TExpImpObjectManager): boolean;

begin
  result := inherited PrepareId(AConnection, AKundenExport, AId, AExpImpManager);

  if FSelectLokSo = nil then
  begin
    FSelectLokSo := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
    FSelectLokSo.SQL.Text := 'SELECT LOKSO_ID, ZPT_ID_MELO, NLK_ID, MLK_ID, TRE_ID FROM ' + ENLOKBE + ' WHERE LOKBE_ID = :LOKBE_ID';
    FSelectLokSo.DBPrepare;
  end;

  try
    FSelectLokSo.ParamByName('LOKBE_ID').AsLongId := AId;
    FSelectLokSo.Open();
    if not FSelectLokSo.Eof then
    begin
      if SelectOther(FSelectLokSo, ENLOKSO, 'LOKSO_ID', AConnection, AKundenExport, AExpImpManager) then
        result := true;
      if SelectOther(FSelectLokSo, ENZPT, 'ZPT_ID_MELO', AConnection, AKundenExport, AExpImpManager) then
        result := true;
      if SelectOther(FSelectLokSo, ENNLK, 'NLK_ID', AConnection, AKundenExport, AExpImpManager) then
        result := true;
      if SelectOther(FSelectLokSo, ENMLK, 'MLK_ID', AConnection, AKundenExport, AExpImpManager) then
        result := true;
      if SelectOther(FSelectLokSo, ENTRE, 'TRE_ID', AConnection, AKundenExport, AExpImpManager) then
        result := true;
    end;
  finally
    FSelectLokSo.Close;
  end;
end;

  inherited Create(AOwner, ENNWS, ATableIdMapping);
  FAutoSelectChildIds := false;
