procedure TGpAdrInfoExportManager.PrepareExport();

var idx, idx2: Integer;
    addOn: String;
    gpInfos: TObjectList;
    abnInfos: TObjectList;
    gpInfo1, gpInfo2: TGpInfo;
    abnInfo1, abnInfo2: TAbnInfo;
    DPSpace: TDPSpace;
    rolle: TGSPRolle;
    qry: TSQLQuery;
begin
  FGpInfos.clear;
  DPSpace := TDPSpace.Create(fConnection, 'KIS', fViewContext.GetRolId);
  gpInfos := TObjectList.Create(false);
  abnInfos := TObjectList.Create(false);
  qry := FConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    // Erst mal nur direkt die GP-Haupt-Adresse, keine Beachtung von abweichenden Adressen oder Adressverwendungen!!
    for idx := 0 to FRoIds.Count - 1 do
    begin
      rolle :=  TGSPRolle.Find(DPSpace, FRoIds[idx]);
      if rolle = nil then
        Continue; // Darf eigentlich nicht vorkommen
      gpInfo1 := TGpInfo.Create;
      gpInfo1.Name := rolle.Partner.Person.Name.AsString;
      gpInfo1.Vorname := rolle.Partner.Person.Vorname.AsString;
      gpInfo1.Strasse := rolle.Partner.Adresse.Strasse.AsString;
      gpInfo1.Ort := rolle.Partner.Adresse.Ort.AsString;
      gpInfo1.Plz := rolle.Partner.Adresse.Plz.AsString;
      gpInfo1.HausNr := rolle.Partner.Adresse.HausNrVon.AsString;
      gpInfo1.HausNrVonZusatz := rolle.Partner.Adresse.HausNrVonZusatz.AsString;
      gpInfo1.HausNrBis := rolle.Partner.Adresse.HausNrBis.AsString;
      gpInfo1.HausNrBisZusatz := rolle.Partner.Adresse.HausNrBisZusatz.AsString;
      gpInfo1.PlzVerwendung := rolle.Partner.Adresse.PlzVerwendung.AsString;
      gpInfo1.Postfach := rolle.Partner.Adresse.Postfach.AsString;
      gpInfo1.HausUnterNrVom := rolle.Partner.Adresse.HausUnternrVon.AsString;
      gpInfo1.HausUnterNrBis := rolle.Partner.Adresse.HausUnterNrBis.AsString;
      gpInfo1.PlzPostfach := rolle.Partner.Adresse.PlzPostfach.AsString;
      gpInfo1.OrtPostfach := rolle.Partner.Adresse.OrtPostfach.AsString;
      gpInfo1.PlzGrosskunde := rolle.Partner.Adresse.PlzGrosskunde.AsString;

      // Vorbelegung 1:1
      gpInfo1.PlzVerwendungEx := gpInfo1.PlzVerwendung;
      gpInfo1.HausNrEx := gpInfo1.HausNr;
      gpInfo1.HausNrVonZusatzEx := gpInfo1.HausNrVonZusatz;
      gpInfo1.HausNrBisEx := gpInfo1.HausNrBis;
      gpInfo1.HausNrBisZusatzEx := gpInfo1.HausNrBisZusatz;
      gpInfo1.HausUnterNrVomEx := gpInfo1.HausUnterNrVom;
      gpInfo1.HausUnterNrBisEx := gpInfo1.HausUnterNrBis;

      gpInfo1.RoId := FRoIds[idx];
      gpInfos.Add(gpInfo1);
      FGpInfos.AddObject(FRoIds[idx], gpInfo1);
    end;

    for Idx := 0 to gpInfos.Count - 1 do
    begin
      gpInfo1 := TGpInfo(gpInfos[idx]);
      if Idx > 0 then
        addOn := Vextend(IntToStr(idx), 2, '0')
      else
        addOn := '';

      if (gpInfo1.name <> '') and (gpInfo1.NameEx = '') then
      begin
        gpInfo1.NameEx :=  'ImpName' + addOn;
        for Idx2 := idx+1 to gpInfos.Count - 1 do
        begin
          gpInfo2 := TGpInfo(gpInfos[idx2]);
          if (gpInfo2.name = gpInfo1.name) and (gpInfo2.NameEx = '') then
            gpInfo2.NameEx := gpInfo1.NameEx;
        end;
      end;

      if (gpInfo1.vorname <> '') and (gpInfo1.vornameEx = '') then
      begin
        gpInfo1.vornameEx := 'ImpVorname' + addOn;
        for Idx2 := idx+1 to gpInfos.Count - 1 do
        begin
          gpInfo2 := TGpInfo(gpInfos[idx2]);
          if (gpInfo2.vorname = gpInfo1.vorname) and (gpInfo2.vornameEx = '') then
            gpInfo2.vornameEx := gpInfo1.vornameEx;
        end;
      end;

      if (gpInfo1.strasse <> '') and (gpInfo1.strasseEx = '') then
      begin
        gpInfo1.strasseEx := 'Importweg' + addOn;
        for Idx2 := idx+1 to gpInfos.Count - 1 do
        begin
          gpInfo2 := TGpInfo(gpInfos[idx2]);
          if (gpInfo2.strasse = gpInfo1.strasse) and (gpInfo2.strasseEx = '') and
             (gpInfo2.Plz = gpInfo1.Plz) then
          begin
            gpInfo2.strasseEx := gpInfo1.strasseEx;
          end;
        end;
      end;

      if (gpInfo1.Ort <> '') and (gpInfo1.OrtEx = '') then
      begin
        gpInfo1.OrtEx := 'ImpOrt' + addOn;
        for Idx2 := idx+1 to gpInfos.Count - 1 do
        begin
          gpInfo2 := TGpInfo(gpInfos[idx2]);
          if (gpInfo2.Ort = gpInfo1.Ort) and (gpInfo2.OrtEx = '') then
            gpInfo2.OrtEx := gpInfo1.OrtEx;
        end;
      end;

      if (gpInfo1.Plz <> '') and (gpInfo1.PlzEx = '') then
      begin
        gpInfo1.PlzEx := VExtend(IntToStr(idx), 5, '9');

        for Idx2 := idx+1 to gpInfos.Count - 1 do
        begin
          gpInfo2 := TGpInfo(gpInfos[idx2]);
          if (gpInfo2.Plz = gpInfo1.Plz) and (gpInfo2.PlzEx = '') then
            gpInfo2.PlzEx := gpInfo1.PlzEx;
        end;
      end;

      if (gpInfo1.PlzGrosskunde <> '') and (gpInfo1.PlzGrosskundeEx = '') then
      begin
        gpInfo1.PlzGrosskundeEx := VExtend(IntToStr(idx), 5, '9');

        for Idx2 := idx+1 to gpInfos.Count - 1 do
        begin
          gpInfo2 := TGpInfo(gpInfos[idx2]);
          if (gpInfo2.PlzGrosskunde = gpInfo1.PlzGrosskunde) and (gpInfo2.PlzGrosskundeEx = '') then
            gpInfo2.PlzGrosskundeEx := gpInfo1.PlzGrosskundeEx;
        end;
      end;

      if (gpInfo1.Postfach <> '') and (gpInfo1.PostfachEx = '') then
      begin
        gpInfo1.PostfachEx := VExtend(IntToStr(idx), length(gpInfo1.Postfach), '9');
        for Idx2 := idx+1 to gpInfos.Count - 1 do
        begin
          gpInfo2 := TGpInfo(gpInfos[idx2]);
          if (gpInfo2.Postfach = gpInfo1.Postfach) and (gpInfo2.PostfachEx = '') then
            gpInfo2.postfachEx := gpInfo1.postfachEx;
        end;
      end;

      if (gpInfo1.OrtPostfach <> '') and (gpInfo1.OrtPostfachEx = '') then
      begin
        gpInfo1.OrtPostfachEx := 'ImpOrt' + addOn;
        for Idx2 := idx+1 to gpInfos.Count - 1 do
        begin
          gpInfo2 := TGpInfo(gpInfos[idx2]);
          if (gpInfo2.OrtPostfach = gpInfo1.OrtPostfach) and (gpInfo2.OrtPostfachEx = '') then
            gpInfo2.OrtPostfachEx := gpInfo1.OrtPostfachEx;
        end;
      end;

      if (gpInfo1.PlzPostfach <> '') and (gpInfo1.PlzPostfachEx = '') then
      begin
        gpInfo1.PlzPostfachEx := VExtend(IntToStr(idx), 5, '9');

        for Idx2 := idx+1 to gpInfos.Count - 1 do
        begin
          gpInfo2 := TGpInfo(gpInfos[idx2]);
          if (gpInfo2.PlzPostfach = gpInfo1.PlzPostfach) and (gpInfo2.PlzPostfachEx = '') then
            gpInfo2.PlzPostfachEx := gpInfo1.PlzPostfachEx;
        end;
      end;
    end;

    qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ENABN + ' WHERE ABN_ID = :ABN_ID';
    qry.DBPrepare;

    // Erst mal nur die Haupt-Adresse
    for idx := 0 to FAbnIds.Count - 1 do
    begin
      SetDBParam(qry.ParamByName('ABN_ID'), StrToInt64Def(FAbnIds[idx], NULL_INT64));
      try
        qry.Open();
        if qry.Eof then
          Continue;

        abnInfo1 := TAbnInfo.Create;
        abnInfo1.AbnId := FAbnIds[idx];
        abnInfo1.Strasse := qry.FieldByName('ABN_STRASSE').AsString;
        abnInfo1.HausNr := qry.FieldByName('ABN_HAUSNR').AsString;
        abnInfo1.HausUnterNr := qry.FieldByName('ABN_HAUSUNTERNR').AsString;
        abnInfo1.Plz := qry.FieldByName('ABN_PLZ').AsString;
        abnInfo1.Ort := qry.FieldByName('ABN_ORT').AsString;

        // 1;1
        abnInfo1.HausNrEx := abnInfo1.HausNr;
        abnInfo1.HausUnterNrEx := abnInfo1.HausUnterNr;
        abnInfos.Add(abnInfo1);
        FAbnInfos.Add(abnInfo1.AbnId, abnInfo1);
      finally
        qry.Close;
      end;
    end;

    for Idx := 0 to abnInfos.Count - 1 do
    begin
      abnInfo1 := TAbnInfo(abnInfos[idx]);
      if Idx > 0 then
        addOn := Vextend(IntToStr(idx), 2, '0')
      else
        addOn := '';

      if (abnInfo1.strasse <> '') and (abnInfo1.strasseEx = '') then
      begin
        for Idx2 := 0 to gpInfos.Count - 1 do
        begin
          gpInfo1 := TGpInfo(gpInfos[idx2]);
          if (gpInfo1.strasse = abnInfo1.strasse)  and
             (gpInfo1.Plz = abnInfo1.Plz) then
          begin
            abnInfo1.strasseEx := gpInfo1.strasseEx;
            break;
          end;
        end;
      end;

      if (abnInfo1.strasse <> '') and (abnInfo1.strasseEx = '') then
        abnInfo1.strasseEx := 'Importweg' + addOn;

      if abnInfo1.PlzEx = '' then
      begin
        for idx2 := 0 to gpInfos.Count - 1 do
        begin
          gpInfo1 := TGpInfo(gpInfos[idx2]);
          if (gpInfo1.Plz = abnInfo1.Plz) and
             (gpInfo1.Ort = abnInfo1.Ort) then
          begin
            abnInfo1.PlzEx := gpInfo1.PlzEx;
            abnInfo1.OrtEx := gpInfo1.OrtEx;
            break;
          end;
        end;
      end;

      if abnInfo1.PlzEx = '' then
          abnInfo1.PlzEx := VExtend(IntToStr(idx), 5, '9');

      if abnInfo1.OrtEx = '' then
          abnInfo1.OrtEx := 'ImpOrt' + addOn;

      for Idx2 := idx+1 to abnInfos.Count - 1 do
      begin
        abnInfo2 := TAbnInfo(abnInfos[idx2]);
        if (abnInfo2.Plz = abnInfo2.Plz) and (abnInfo2.PlzEx = '') then
          abnInfo2.PlzEx := abnInfo1.PlzEx;
        if (abnInfo2.Ort = abnInfo2.Ort) and (abnInfo2.OrtEx = '') then
          abnInfo2.OrtEx := abnInfo1.OrtEx;
        if (abnInfo2.Strasse = abnInfo2.Strasse) and (abnInfo2.StrasseEx = '') then
          abnInfo2.StrasseEx := abnInfo1.StrasseEx;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    DPSpace.Free;
    gpInfos.Free;
    abnInfos.Free;
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

procedure TGpAdrInfoExportManager.RegisterExportAbnId(AAbnId: String);
begin
 if FAbnIds.IndexOf(AAbnId) <0 then
    FAbnIds.Add(AAbnId);
end;

procedure TGpAdrInfoExportManager.RegisterExportRoId(ARoId: String);
begin
  if FRoIds.IndexOf(ARoId) <0 then
    FRoIds.Add(ARoId);
end;

procedure TGpAdrInfoExportManager.WriteExportData(AFileStream: TStream);
var gpInfo: TGpInfo;
    abnInfo: TAbnInfo;
begin
  PrepareExport;
  if FGpInfos.Count >0 then
  begin
    TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream,' <' +  TAG_GPINFOS + '>');
    for gpInfo in FGpInfos.ValueCollection do
    begin
      TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, '  <' + TAG_GPINFO + ' ' + TAG_ROID + '="' + gpInfo.RoId + '">');
      TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, '      <' + TAG_NAME + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.NameEx) +  '</' + TAG_NAME + '>', false);
      if (gpInfo.VornameEx <> '') then
        TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_VORNAME + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.VornameEx) +  '</' + TAG_VORNAME + '>', false);
      if (gpInfo.StrasseEx <> '') then
        TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_STRASSE + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.StrasseEx) +  '</' + TAG_STRASSE + '>', false);
      if (gpInfo.OrtEx <> '') then
        TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_ORT + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.OrtEx) +  '</' + TAG_ORT + '>', false);

      TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_PLZ + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.PlzEx) +  '</' + TAG_PLZ + '>', false);
      if (gpInfo.HausNrEx <> '') then
        TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_HAUSNR + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.HausNrEx) +  '</' + TAG_HAUSNR + '>', false);

      if (gpInfo.HausNrVonZusatzEx <> '') then
        TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_HAUSNRVONZUSATZ + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.HausNrVonZusatzEx) +  '</' + TAG_HAUSNRVONZUSATZ + '>', false);

      if (gpInfo.HausNrBisEx <> '') then
        TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_HAUSNRBIS + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.HausNrBisEx) +  '</' + TAG_HAUSNRBIS + '>', false);

      if (gpInfo.HausNrBisZusatzEx <> '') then
        TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_HAUSNRBISZUSATZ + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.HausNrBisZusatzEx) +  '</' + TAG_HAUSNRBISZUSATZ + '>', false);

      TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_PLZVERWENDUNG + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.PlzVerwendungEx) +  '</' + TAG_PLZVERWENDUNG + '>', false);
      if (gpInfo.PostfachEx <> '') then
         TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_POSTFACH + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.PostfachEx) +  '</' + TAG_POSTFACH + '>', false);

      if (gpInfo.HausUnterNrVomEx <> '') then
        TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_HAUSUNTERNR_VON + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.HausUnterNrVomEx) +  '</' + TAG_HAUSUNTERNR_VON + '>', false);

      if (gpInfo.HausUnterNrBis <> '') then
        TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_HAUSUNTERNR_BIS + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.HausUnterNrBis) +  '</' + TAG_HAUSUNTERNR_BIS + '>', false);

      if (gpInfo.OrtPostfachEx <> '') then
         TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_ORTPOSTFACH + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.OrtPostfachEx) +  '</' + TAG_ORTPOSTFACH + '>', false);

      if (gpInfo.PlzPostfachEx <> '') then
         TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_PLZPOSTFACH + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.PlzPostfachEx) +  '</' + TAG_PLZPOSTFACH + '>', false);

      if (gpInfo.PlzGrosskundeEx <> '') then
         TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_PLZGROSSKUNDE + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(gpInfo.PlzGrosskundeEx) +  '</' + TAG_PLZGROSSKUNDE + '>', false);

      TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, '  </' + TAG_GPINFO + '>');
    end;
    TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' </' + TAG_GPINFOS + '>');
  end;

  if FAbnInfos.Count > 0 then
  begin
    TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream,' <' +  TAG_ABNINFOS + '>');
    for abnInfo in FAbnInfos.ValueCollection do
    begin
      TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, '  <' + TAG_ABNINFO + ' ' + TAG_ABNID + '="' + abnInfo.AbnId + '">');
      TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_STRASSE + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(abnInfo.StrasseEx) +  '</' + TAG_STRASSE + '>', false);
      TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_ORT + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(abnInfo.OrtEx) +  '</' + TAG_ORT + '>', false);
      TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_PLZ + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(abnInfo.PlzEx) +  '</' + TAG_PLZ + '>', false);
      if (abnInfo.HausNrEx <> '') then
        TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_HAUSNR + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(abnInfo.HausNrEx) +  '</' + TAG_HAUSNR + '>', false);

      if (abnInfo.HausUnterNrEx <> '') then
        TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' <' + TAG_HAUSNRVONZUSATZ + '>' + TXmlUtils.ReplaceBackend2Xml(abnInfo.HausUnterNrEx) +  '</' + TAG_HAUSNRVONZUSATZ + '>', false);
      TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, '   </' + TAG_ABNINFO + '>');
    end;
    TXmlUtils.writeXmlStr(AFileStream, ' </' + TAG_ABNINFOS + '>');
  end;
end;

{ TImportMapHelper }

procedure TImportMapHelper.CheckImpTables;
begin
  if not TableExists(QSKIMP) then
  begin
    ConnectionPool.AdminConnection.ExecuteDirect(
        'CREATE TABLE ' + QSKIMP + ' (' + CR_LF +
        '       IMP_ID                   NUMBER(12) NOT NULL,' + CR_LF +
        '       IMP_DATEINAME            VARCHAR2(500) NULL,' + CR_LF +
        '       IMP_EXPORT_IDENTIFIER    VARCHAR2(500) NULL,' + CR_LF +
        '       IMP_ANGELEGT             DATE NOT NULL,' + CR_LF +
        '       CONSTRAINT QS_KUNDENIMPORT_PK' + CR_LF +
        '       PRIMARY KEY (IMP_ID)' + CR_LF +
        '       USING INDEX' + CR_LF +
        '              TABLESPACE CSEN_SPACE_INDEX' + CR_LF +
        ')' + CR_LF +
        '       TABLESPACE CSEN_SPACE');

    ConnectionPool.AdminConnection.ExecuteDirect(
        'CREATE INDEX CREATOR.QSKIMP_IE_EXPID ON CREATOR.QS_KUNDENIMPORT ' + CR_LF +
        '(' + CR_LF +
        '       IMP_EXPORT_IDENTIFIER    ASC,' + CR_LF +
        '       IMP_DATEINAME            ASC' + CR_LF +
        ')' + CR_LF +
        '       TABLESPACE CSEN_SPACE_INDEX');
  end;

  if not TableExists(QSKIMPM) then
  begin
    ConnectionPool.AdminConnection.ExecuteDirect(
        'CREATE TABLE ' + QSKIMPM + ' (' + CR_LF +
        '       IMPM_ID           NUMBER(12) NOT NULL,' + CR_LF +
        '       IMP_ID            NUMBER(12) NOT NULL,' + CR_LF +
        '       IMPM_TYP          NUMBER(3) NOT NULL,' + CR_LF +       // Nachträglich von Number(1) auf Number(3) geändert. Muss ggf auf internen Umgebungen noch manuell angepasst werden
        '       IMPM_OLD_VALUE    VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL,' + CR_LF +
        '       IMPM_NEW_VALUE    VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL,' + CR_LF +
        '       CONSTRAINT QSKIMPM_PK' + CR_LF +
        '       PRIMARY KEY (IMPM_ID)' + CR_LF +
        '       USING INDEX' + CR_LF +
        '              TABLESPACE CSEN_SPACE_INDEX,' + CR_LF +
        '       CONSTRAINT QSKIMPM_FK_QSKIMP' + CR_LF +
        '              FOREIGN KEY (IMP_ID)' + CR_LF +
        '       REFERENCES CREATOR.QS_KUNDENIMPORT' + CR_LF +
        ')' + CR_LF +
        '       TABLESPACE CSEN_SPACE');

    ConnectionPool.AdminConnection.ExecuteDirect(
        'CREATE INDEX CREATOR.QSKIMPM_FI_QSKIMP ON CREATOR.QS_KUNDENIMPORT_MAP' + CR_LF +
        '(' + CR_LF +
        '       IMP_ID    ASC' + CR_LF +
        ')' + CR_LF +
        '       TABLESPACE CSEN_SPACE_INDEX');

    ConnectionPool.AdminConnection.ExecuteDirect(
        '    CREATE UNIQUE INDEX CREATOR.QSKIMPM_AK_TYP ON CREATOR.QS_KUNDENIMPORT_MAP' + CR_LF +
        '(' + CR_LF +
        '       IMP_ID            ASC,' + CR_LF +
        '       IMPM_OLD_VALUE    ASC,' + CR_LF +
        '       IMPM_TYP          ASC' + CR_LF +
        ')' + CR_LF +
        '       TABLESPACE CSEN_SPACE_INDEX');
  end;
end;

constructor TImportMapHelper.Create(AConnection: TecConnection);
begin
  FConnection := AConnection;
  FMeteringCodeMap := TelStringStringMap.Create;
  FMaloCodeMap := TelStringStringMap.Create;
  FRollenMap := TelStringStringMap.Create;
  FForeignKeyTabelleMap := TelStringStringMap.Create;
  FGeraeteNrMap := TelStringStringMap.Create;
  FNeloCodeMap := TelStringStringMap.Create;
  FSreCodeMap := TelStringStringMap.Create;
  FTreCodeMap := TelStringStringMap.Create;
  FRecNrMap := TelStringStringMap.Create;
  FEdiRefMap := TelStringStringMap.Create;
  FKonfigIdMap := TelStringStringMap.Create;
  FNwsPaketIdMap := TelStringStringMap.Create;
  CheckImpTables;
end;

destructor TImportMapHelper.Destroy;
begin
  FreeAndNil(FMeteringCodeMap);
  FreeAndNil(FMaloCodeMap);
  FreeAndNil(FRollenMap);
  FreeAndNil(FForeignKeyTabelleMap);
  FreeAndNil(FGeraeteNrMap);
  FreeAndNil(FNeloCodeMap);
  FreeAndNil(FSreCodeMap);
  FreeAndNil(FTreCodeMap);
  FreeAndNil(FEdiRefMap);
  FreeAndNil(FRecNrMap);
  FreeAndNil(FKonfigIdMap);
  FreeAndNil(FNwsPaketIdMap);
  inherited;
end;

function TImportMapHelper.ExportIdentifier2ImpId(ADateiName, AExportIdentifier: String): Integer;
var qry: TSqlQuery;
begin
  result := NULL_INTEGER;
  qry := FConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT imp_id FROM ' + QSKIMP + CR_LF +
                    ' WHERE imp_export_identifier = ' + QuotedStr(AExportIdentifier) + CR_LF +
                    '   AND imp_dateiname = ' + QuotedStr(ADateiName) + CR_LF +
                    ' ORDER BY 1 DESC';
    qry.Open();
    if not qry.Eof then
      result := qry.Fields[0].AsInteger;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

procedure TImportMapHelper.Initialize(ADateiName, AExportIdentifier: String; AImpId: TLongId; AAllowCreate: Boolean = true);
var qry: TSqlQuery;
    loadData: Boolean;
begin
  FImpId := AImpId;
  FMeteringCodeMap.Clear;
  FMaloCodeMap.Clear;
  FRollenMap.Clear;
  FDateiname := ADateiName;
  qry := FConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    loadData := not AImpId.IsNull;
    qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT * FROM ' + QSKIMP + ' WHERE IMP_ID = ' + AImpId.ToStr;
    if loadData then
      qry.Open();
    if loadData and not qry.Eof then
    begin
      FDateiname := qry.FieldByName('IMP_DATEINAME').AsString;
      qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT * FROM ' + QSKIMPM + CR_LF +
                      ' WHERE IMP_ID = ' + AImpId.ToStr;
      qry.Open;
      while not qry.Eof do
      begin
        if qry.FieldByName('impm_typ').AsInteger = IMP_MAP_METERINGCODE then
          FMeteringCodeMap.AddObject(qry.FieldByName('impm_old_value').AsString, qry.FieldByName('impm_new_value').AsString)
        else
        if qry.FieldByName('impm_typ').AsInteger = IMP_MAP_MALOCODE then
          FMaloCodeMap.AddObject(qry.FieldByName('impm_old_value').AsString, qry.FieldByName('impm_new_value').AsString)
        else
        if qry.FieldByName('impm_typ').AsInteger = IMP_MAP_ROLLENNR then
          FRollenMap.AddObject(qry.FieldByName('impm_old_value').AsString, qry.FieldByName('impm_new_value').AsString)
        else
        if qry.FieldByName('impm_typ').AsInteger = IMP_MAP_GERAETENR then
          FGeraeteNrMap.AddObject(qry.FieldByName('impm_old_value').AsString, qry.FieldByName('impm_new_value').AsString)
        else
        if qry.FieldByName('impm_typ').AsInteger = IMP_MAP_NELOCODE then
          FNeloCodeMap.AddObject(qry.FieldByName('impm_old_value').AsString, qry.FieldByName('impm_new_value').AsString)
        else
        if qry.FieldByName('impm_typ').AsInteger = IMP_MAP_SRECODE then
          FSreCodeMap.AddObject(qry.FieldByName('impm_old_value').AsString, qry.FieldByName('impm_new_value').AsString)
         else
        if qry.FieldByName('impm_typ').AsInteger = IMP_MAP_TRECODE then
          FTreCodeMap.AddObject(qry.FieldByName('impm_old_value').AsString, qry.FieldByName('impm_new_value').AsString)
         else
        if qry.FieldByName('impm_typ').AsInteger = IMP_MAP_EDIREF then
          FEdiRefMap.AddObject(qry.FieldByName('impm_old_value').AsString, qry.FieldByName('impm_new_value').AsString)
        else
        if qry.FieldByName('impm_typ').AsInteger = IMP_MAP_REC_NR then
          FRecNrMap.AddObject(qry.FieldByName('impm_old_value').AsString, qry.FieldByName('impm_new_value').AsString)
         else
        if qry.FieldByName('impm_typ').AsInteger = IMP_MAP_KONFIGID then
          FKonfigIdMap.AddObject(qry.FieldByName('impm_old_value').AsString, qry.FieldByName('impm_new_value').AsString)
         else
        if qry.FieldByName('impm_typ').AsInteger = IMP_MAP_NWSPAKETID then
          FNwsPaketIdMap.AddObject(qry.FieldByName('impm_old_value').AsString, qry.FieldByName('impm_new_value').AsString)
         else
          raise Exception.Create('Ungültiger Wert ' + qry.FieldByName('impm_typ').AsString + ' für  impm_typ');
        qry.Next;
      end;
    end
     else
    begin
      if not AAllowCreate then
        raise Exception.Create('Keinen Import-Datensatz zu ImpId = ' + AImpId.ToStr + ' gefunden');
      qry.SQL.Text := 'INSERT INTO ' + QSKIMP +  CR_LF +
        '(IMP_ID, IMP_DATEINAME, imp_export_identifier, imp_angelegt) ' +  CR_LF +
          'VALUES' + CR_LF +
          '(' + AImpId.ToStr +', ' + QuotedStr(ADateiName) + ', ' + QuotedStr(AExportIdentifier) + ', Sysdate)';
      qry.ExecSQL();
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

procedure TImportMapHelper.RegisterMaloCodeMapping(AMaloCodeAlt, AMaloCodeNeu: String);
begin
 if FMaloCodeMap.ContainsKey(AMaloCodeAlt) then
   exit;
 FMaloCodeMap.AddObject(AMaloCodeAlt, AMaloCodeNeu);
end;

 if FMeteringCodeMap.ContainsKey(AMeteringCodeAlt) then
   exit;
 FMeteringCodeMap.AddObject(AMeteringCodeAlt, AMeteringCodeNeu);
end;

 if FNeloCodeMap.ContainsKey(ACodeAlt) then

  if FRecNrMap.ContainsKey(ARecNrAlt) then

procedure TImportMapHelper.RegisterRollenMapping(AMndIdAlt: Integer; ANummerAlt: String; AMndIdNeu: Integer; ANummerNeu: String);
var keyAlt, keyNeu: String;
begin
 keyAlt := IntToStr(AMndIdAlt) + '#' + ANummerAlt;
 keyNeu := IntToStr(AMndIdNeu) + '#' + ANummerNeu;
 if FRollenMap.ContainsKey(keyAlt) then
   exit;

 FRollenMap.AddObject(keyAlt, keyNeu);
end;

begin
  if FSreCodeMap.ContainsKey(ACodeAlt) then
   exit;
  FSreCodeMap.AddObject(ACodeAlt, ACodeNeu);
end;

begin
  if FTreCodeMap.ContainsKey(ACodeAlt) then
   exit;
  FTreCodeMap.AddObject(ACodeAlt, ACodeNeu);
end;

procedure TImportMapHelper.SaveMappings;
var qry: TSqlQuery;
    procedure SaveMap(AMap: TelStringStringMap; ATyp: Integer);
    var idx: Integer;
    begin
      for idx := 0 to AMap.Count - 1 do
      begin
        qry.ParamByName('IMPM_OLD_VALUE').AsString := AMap.Items[idx];
        qry.ParamByName('IMPM_NEW_VALUE').AsString := AMap.Values[qry.ParamByName('IMPM_OLD_VALUE').AsString];
        qry.ParamByName('IMPM_TYP').AsInteger := ATyp;
        qry.ExecSQL();
      end;
    end;

begin
  qry := FConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    qry.SQL.Text := 'INSERT INTO ' + QSKIMPM +  CR_LF +
               '(IMP_ID, IMPM_ID, IMPM_TYP, IMPM_OLD_VALUE, IMPM_NEW_VALUE)' + CR_LF +
               'SELECT ' + FImpId.ToStr + ', ' +  SEQ_ENKUNDE + '.nextVal, :IMPM_TYP, :IMPM_OLD_VALUE, :IMPM_NEW_VALUE'  +
               ' FROM DUAL ' +  CR_LF +
               'WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM ' + QSKIMPM + ' b' + CR_LF +
               '                   WHERE IMP_ID = ' + FImpId.ToStr + CR_LF +
               '                    AND IMPM_TYP = :IMPM_TYP ' + CR_LF +
               '                    AND IMPM_OLD_VALUE = :IMPM_OLD_VALUE)';
    qry.DBPrepare;
    SaveMap(FMeteringCodeMap, IMP_MAP_METERINGCODE);
    SaveMap(FMaloCodeMap, IMP_MAP_MALOCODE);
    SaveMap(FRollenMap, IMP_MAP_ROLLENNR);
    SaveMap(FGeraeteNrMap, IMP_MAP_GERAETENR);
    SaveMap(FNeloCodeMap, IMP_MAP_NELOCODE);
    SaveMap(FSreCodeMap, IMP_MAP_SRECODE);
    SaveMap(FTreCodeMap, IMP_MAP_TRECODE);
    SaveMap(FEdiRefMap, IMP_MAP_EDIREF);
    SaveMap(FRecNrMap, IMP_MAP_REC_NR);
    SaveMap(FKonfigIdMap, IMP_MAP_KONFIGID);
    SaveMap(FNwsPaketIdMap, IMP_MAP_NWSPAKETID);
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

procedure TImportMapHelper.RegisterEdiRefMapping(ARefALt, ARefALtNeu: String);
begin
 if FEdiRefMap.ContainsKey(ARefALt) then
    exit;
  FEdiRefMap.AddObject(ARefALt, ARefALtNeu);
end;

procedure TImportMapHelper.RegisterForeignKeyTabelleMap(APkIdAlt, AFkIdAlt, ASpaltenname, ATabellenname: String);
var
  PkFkIdsAlt, SpaltenTabellenNamen: String;
begin
  PkFkIdsAlt := APkIdAlt + '#' + AFkIdAlt;
  if FForeignKeyTabelleMap.ContainsKey(PkFkIdsAlt) then
    exit;

  SpaltenTabellenNamen := ASpaltenname + '#' + ATabellenname;
  FForeignKeyTabelleMap.AddObject(PkFkIdsAlt, SpaltenTabellenNamen);
end;

procedure TImportMapHelper.RegisterGeraeteNrMapping(AGeraeteNrAlt, AGeraeteNrNeu: String);
begin
 if FGeraeteNrMap.ContainsKey(AGeraeteNrAlt) then
    exit;
  FGeraeteNrMap.AddObject(AGeraeteNrAlt, AGeraeteNrNeu);
end;

procedure TImportMapHelper.RegisterNwsPaketIdMapping(ANwsPaketIdAlt, ANwsPaketIdNeu: String);
begin
 if FNwsPaketIdMap.ContainsKey(ANwsPaketIdAlt) then
    exit;
  FNwsPaketIdMap.AddObject(ANwsPaketIdAlt, ANwsPaketIdNeu);
end;

function TImportMapHelper.GetTabellenname(ASpaltenTabellenNamen: String): String;
begin
  Result := Copy(ASpaltenTabellenNamen, pos('#', ASpaltenTabellenNamen)+1);
end;

function TImportMapHelper.GetSpaltenname(ASpaltenTabellenNamen: String): String;
begin
  Result := Copy(ASpaltenTabellenNamen, 1, pos('#', ASpaltenTabellenNamen)-1);
end;

function TImportMapHelper.GetFkIdAlt(APkFkIdsAlt: String): String;
begin
  Result := Copy(APkFkIdsAlt, pos('#', APkFkIdsAlt)+1);
end;

function TImportMapHelper.GetPkIdAlt(APkFkIdsAlt: String): String;
begin
  Result := Copy(APkFkIdsAlt, 1, pos('#', APkFkIdsAlt)-1);
end;

{ TEneStammdaten }

constructor TEneStammdaten.Create(AParent: TStammdatenExportImportManager);
begin
  // Rein Informativ etwas mehr exportieren
  inherited Create(AParent, ENENE, 'ENE_ID', ftInteger,
     'SELECT MND_ID, ENE_ID, ENE_TEXT, ENE_EINSPEISUNG, ENE_MUELL, ENE_KOMBI, ENE_ENERGIEARTTYP, ENE_KOMBI2,' +
     'ENE_EIGENVERBRAUCH, ENE_TECHNIK, ENE_EDM_AKTIV_NETZ, ENE_EDM_STARTDATUM_NETZ, ENE_EDM_AKTIV_VERTRIEB, ENE_EDM_STARTDATUM_VERTRIEB, ENE_PFLICHTABL_MONATE,' +
     'ENE_PFLICHTABL_ERSATZWERTE, ENE_PFLICHTABL_SELBSTABL, ENE_EDM_BILANZ_START_NETZ, ENE_EDM_BILANZ_START_VERTRIEB, ENE_EDM_KBKABR_ENDE_NETZ, ENE_EDM_KBKABR_ENDE_VERTRIEB,' +
     'ENE_MDM_STARTDATUM_KME, ENE_MDMSYCHRONISATION_KME, ENE_STORNOGRUND_PFLICHT, ENE_MDMSYCHRONISATION_RD, ENE_MDM_STARTDATUM_RD, ENE_SCHAETZALGO_ABL FROM ' + ENENE);
  FResolveDataList := TelObjectList<TEneDaten>.Create(true);
end;

destructor TEneStammdaten.Destroy;
begin
  FreeAndNil(FResolveDataList);
  inherited;
end;

function TEneStammdaten.GetDefaultValue(AIdAlt: string; AMndId: Integer): string;
var idx: Integer;
    data: TEneDaten;
    eneTypDefault: Integer;
begin
  eneTypDefault := NULL_INTEGER;
  if FResolveDataList.Count = 0 then
  begin
    // Energieart nicht exportiert !!!
    result := AIdAlt;
    exit;
  end;

  for idx := 0 to FResolveDataList.Count - 1 do
  begin
    data := FResolveDataList[idx];
    if (data.EneIdAlt = AIdAlt) and (data.MndId = IntToStr(AMndId)) then
    begin
      if data.EneIdResolved <> '' then
      begin
        result := data.EneIdResolved;
        exit;
      end
      else
      begin
        eneTypDefault := StrToIntDef(data.EneTyp, NULL_INTEGER);
        break;
      end;
    end;
  end;

  if (eneTypDefault <> NULL_INTEGER) then
  begin
    dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.First;
    while not dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Eof do
    begin
      if not dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_ENERGIEARTTYP.IsNull and
         (dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_ENERGIEARTTYP.AsInteger = eneTypDefault) then
      begin
        result := dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_ID.AsString;
        exit;
      end;
      dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Next;
    end;
  end;

  if (StrToIntDef(AIdAlt, NULL_INTEGER) >0) and
     (StrToIntDef(AIdAlt, NULL_INTEGER) <=3) then
  begin
    result := AIdAlt;
    exit;
  end;

  dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.First;
  while not dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Eof do
  begin
    if dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_ENERGIEARTTYP.IsNull then
    begin
      result := dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_ID.AsString;
      exit;
    end;
    dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Next;
  end;
end;

function TEneStammdaten.LoadExportData(ANode: IXMLNode; AExportMndId: Integer): Boolean;
var idx, idx2: Integer;
    node, nodeCol: IXMLNode;
    data: TEneDaten;
    value: String;
begin
  result := false;
  if ANode = nil then
    exit;
  if not ANode.HasAttribute(TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME) then
    exit;
  if ANode.Attributes[TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME] <> TableName then
    exit;
  Clear;
  result := true;
  if not ANode.HasChildNodes then
   exit;

  for idx := 0 to ANode.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
  begin
    node := ANode.ChildNodes[idx];
    if ExportMndIdAbweichend(node, AExportMndId) then
      continue;

    data := TEneDaten.Create;
    for idx2 := 0 to node.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
    begin
      nodeCol := node.ChildNodes[idx2];
      value := TXmlUtils.ReplaceXml2Backend(nodeCol.Text);
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'MND_ID' then
        data.MndId := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'ENE_ID' then
        data.EneIdAlt := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'ENE_ENERGIEARTTYP' then
        data.EneTyp := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'ENE_TEXT' then
        data.EneText := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'ENE_EINSPEISUNG' then
        data.EneEinspeisung := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'ENE_MUELL' then
        data.EneMuell := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'ENE_EDM_AKTIV_NETZ' then
        data.EneEdmAktivNetz := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'ENE_EDM_AKTIV_VERTRIEB' then
        data.EneEdmAktivVertieb := value
    end;
    if (data.MndId <> '') and (StrToIntDef(data.MndId, AExportMndId) <> AExportMndId) then
    begin
      data.Free;
      continue;
    end;

    FResolveDataList.add(data);
  end;
end;

{ TEneStammdaten.TEneDaten }

constructor TEneStammdaten.TEneDaten.Create;
begin
  EneIdAlt := '';
  EneIdResolved := '';
  MndId := '';
  EneTyp := '';
  EneEinspeisung := '';
  EneMuell := '';
  EneEdmAktivNetz := '';
  EneEdmAktivVertieb := '';
end;

procedure TEneStammdaten.Resolve(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer);

var idx: Integer;
    data: TEneDaten;

begin
  if FInResolving then
    exit;
  StartResolving;
  try
    for idx := 0 to FResolveDataList.Count - 1 do
    begin
      data := FResolveDataList[idx];
      if data.EneIdResolved <> '' then
        Continue;
       if dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Locate('ENE_ID', data.EneIdAlt, []) then
       begin
         if (dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_ENERGIEARTTYP.AsString = data.EneTyp) and
            (data.EneEinspeisung  = dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_EINSPEISUNG.AsString) then
         begin
           data.EneIdResolved := dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_ID.AsString;
           AddResolvedValue(data.EneIdAlt, data.EneIdResolved);
           Continue;
         end;
       end;

      if (data.EneTyp = IntToStr(ENERGIEARTTYP_STROM)) or
         (data.EneTyp = IntToStr(ENERGIEARTTYP_GAS)) then
      begin
        dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Filter := 'ENE_ENERGIEARTTYP = ' + data.EneTyp;
        dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Filtered := true;
        try
          dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.First;
          while not dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Eof do
          begin
            if (data.EneEinspeisung  = dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_EINSPEISUNG.AsString) then
            begin
              data.EneIdResolved := dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_ID.AsString;
              AddResolvedValue(data.EneIdAlt, data.EneIdResolved);
              break;
            end;
            dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Next;
          end;
        finally
          dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Filtered := false;
        end;
      end
      else
      begin
        // für undefinierte Energiearttypen
        dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Filter := 'ENE_ENERGIEARTTYP IS NULL';
        dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Filtered := true;
        try
          dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.First;
          while not dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Eof do
          begin
            if (data.EneEinspeisung  = dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_EINSPEISUNG.AsString) and
                (data.EneMuell  = dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_MUELL.AsString) and
                (data.EneEdmAktivNetz  = dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_EDM_AKTIV_NETZ.AsString) and
                (data.EneEdmAktivVertieb  = dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_EDM_AKTIV_VERTRIEB.AsString) then
            begin
              data.EneIdResolved := dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENEENE_ID.AsString;
              AddResolvedValue(data.EneIdAlt, data.EneIdResolved);
              break;
            end;
            dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Next;
          end;
        finally
          dtmBasicsStammdaten.qdsENENE.Filtered := false;
        end;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    EndResolving;
  end;
end;

begin
  // Rein Informativ etwas mehr exportieren

begin
  FResolveDataList.Free;
  inherited;

function TFTPStammdaten.GetDefaultValue(AIdAlt: string; AMndId: Integer): string;
begin
  result := '1'; // Das muss es eigentlich immer geben
end;

function TFTPStammdaten.LoadExportData(ANode: IXMLNode; AExportMndId: Integer): Boolean;
var idx, idx2: Integer;
    node, nodeCol: IXMLNode;
    data: TFtpData;
    value: String;
begin
  result := false;
  if ANode = nil then
    exit;
  if not ANode.HasAttribute(TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME) then
    exit;
  if ANode.Attributes[TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME] <> TableName then
    exit;
  Clear;
  result := true;
  if not ANode.HasChildNodes then
   exit;

  for idx := 0 to ANode.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
  begin
    node := ANode.ChildNodes[idx];
    if ExportMndIdAbweichend(node, AExportMndId) then
      continue;

    data := TFtpData.Create;
    for idx2 := 0 to node.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
    begin
      nodeCol := node.ChildNodes[idx2];
      value := TXmlUtils.ReplaceXml2Backend(nodeCol.Text);
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'MND_ID' then
        data.mndId := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'FTP_BEZEICHNUNG' then
        data.FtpBezeichnung := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'FTP_LANDISO' then
        data.FtpLandIso := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'FTP_NR' then
        data.IdAlt := value;
    end;

    if (data.MndId <> '') and (StrToIntDef(data.MndId, AExportMndId) <> AExportMndId) then
    begin
      data.Free;
      continue;
    end;

    FResolveDataList.add(data);
  end;
end;

procedure TFTPStammdaten.Resolve(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer);
var idx: Integer;
    data: TFtpData;
    qry: TSqlQuery;
begin
  if FInResolving then
    exit;
  StartResolving;
  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    for idx := 0 to FResolveDataList.Count - 1 do
    begin
      data := FResolveDataList[idx];
      if data.IdResolved <> '' then
         Continue;
      qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT FTP_NR, FTP_BEZEICHNUNG, FTP_LANDISO ' + CR_LF +
                      ' FROM ' + ENFTP + CR_LF +
                      ' WHERE MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(AMndId) +
                      '  AND FTP_BEZEICHNUNG = ' + QuotedStr(data.FtpBezeichnung);
      qry.Open();
      if not qry.Eof then
      begin
         data.IdResolved := qry.FieldByName('FTP_NR').AsString;
         AddResolvedValue(data.IdAlt, data.IdResolved);
         Continue;
      end;

      qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT FTP_NR, FTP_BEZEICHNUNG, FTP_LANDISO ' + CR_LF +
                      ' FROM ' + ENFTP + CR_LF +
                      ' WHERE MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(AMndId) +
                      '  AND FTP_NR = ' + data.IdAlt;
      qry.Open();
      if not qry.Eof then
      begin
         data.IdResolved := qry.FieldByName('FTP_NR').AsString;
         AddResolvedValue(data.IdAlt, data.IdResolved);
         Continue;
      end;

    end;
  finally
    qry.free;
    EndResolving;
  end;
end;

{ TFTPStammdaten.TFtpData }

constructor TFTPStammdaten.TFtpData.Create;
begin
  MndId := '';
  FtpLandIso := '';
  FtpBezeichnung := '';
  IdAlt := '';
  IdResolved := '';
end;

  FResolveDataList := TelObjectList<TInfData>.Create(True);

begin
  FreeAndNil(FResolveDataList);
  inherited;
end;


function TInfStammdaten.LoadExportData(ANode: IXMLNode; AExportMndId: Integer): Boolean;
var idx, idx2: Integer;
    node, nodeCol: IXMLNode;
    data: TInfData;
    value: String;
begin
  result := false;
  if ANode = nil then
    exit;
  if not ANode.HasAttribute(TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME) then
    exit;
  if ANode.Attributes[TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME] <> TableName then
    exit;
  Clear;
  result := true;
  if not ANode.HasChildNodes then
   exit;

  for idx := 0 to ANode.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
  begin
    node := ANode.ChildNodes[idx];
    if ExportMndIdAbweichend(node, AExportMndId) then
      continue;
    data := TInfData.Create;
    for idx2 := 0 to node.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
    begin
      nodeCol := node.ChildNodes[idx2];
      value := TXmlUtils.ReplaceXml2Backend(nodeCol.Text);
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'MND_ID' then
        data.MndId := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'INF_FELD_ID' then
        data.InfFeldIdAlt := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'INF_TYP' then
        data.InfTyp := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'INF_FELDNAME' then
        data.InfFeldname := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'INF_ART' then
        data.InfArt := value
    end;
    if (data.MndId <> '') and (StrToIntDef(data.MndId, AExportMndId) <> AExportMndId) then
    begin
      data.Free;
      continue;
    end;

    FResolveDataList.add(data);
  end;
end;

procedure TInfStammdaten.Resolve(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer);
var idx: Integer;
    qry: TSQLQuery;
    inf: TInfData;
begin
  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  if FInResolving then
    exit;
  StartResolving;
  try
    for idx := 0 to FResolveDataList.Count - 1 do
    begin
      inf := FResolveDataList[idx];
      if inf.InfFeldIdResolved <> '' then
         Continue;

       qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT INF_FELD_ID ' + CR_LF +
                       ' FROM ' + ENINF + CR_LF +
                       ' WHERE MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(AMndId) + CR_LF +
                       IfThen(inf.InfTyp <> '', 'AND INF_TYP = ' + QuotedStr(inf.InfTyp) + CR_LF, '') +
             IfThen(inf.InfArt <> '', 'AND INF_ART = ' + QuotedStr(inf.InfArt) + CR_LF, '') +
                       IfThen(inf.InfFeldname <> '', 'AND INF_FELDNAME = ' + QuotedStr(inf.InfFeldname) + CR_LF, '') +
                       ' ORDER BY INF_FELD_ID DESC';
      qry.Open;
      try
        if not qry.Eof then
        begin
          inf.InfFeldIdResolved := qry.FieldByName('INF_FELD_ID').AsString;
          AddResolvedValue(inf.InfFeldIdAlt, inf.InfFeldIdResolved);
        end;
      finally
        qry.Close;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
    EndResolving;
  end;
end;


constructor TInfStammdaten.TInfData.Create();
begin
  InfFeldIdAlt := '';
  InfFeldIdResolved := '';
  InfTyp := '';
  InfArt := '';
  InfFeldname := '';
end;

{TGerStammdaten}

constructor TGerStammdaten.Create(AParent: TStammdatenExportImportManager);
begin
  inherited Create(AParent, ENGER, 'GER_ID', ftLargeint,
    'SELECT GER_ID, GER_BEZEICHNUNG, GER_GERAETEGRUNDTYP FROM ' + ENGER, false);
  FResolveDataList := TelObjectList<TGerData>.Create(True);
  FDefaultValue :=  '';
end;

destructor TGerStammdaten.Destroy;
begin
  FreeAndNil(FResolveDataList);
  inherited;
end;


function TGerStammdaten.GetDefaultValue(AIdAlt: string; AMndId: Integer): string;
begin
  if (StrToIntDef(AIdAlt, -1) >= 1) and (StrToIntDef(AIdAlt, -1) <= 10) then
    result := AIdAlt
  else
    result := '1'; // Standard-Fallback
end;

function TGerStammdaten.LoadExportData(ANode: IXMLNode; AExportMndId: Integer): Boolean;
var idx, idx2: Integer;
    node, nodeCol: IXMLNode;
    data: TGerData;
    value: String;
begin
  result := false;
  if ANode = nil then
    exit;
  if not ANode.HasAttribute(TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME) then
    exit;
  if ANode.Attributes[TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME] <> TableName then
    exit;
  Clear;
  result := true;
  if not ANode.HasChildNodes then
   exit;

  for idx := 0 to ANode.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
  begin
    node := ANode.ChildNodes[idx];
    if ExportMndIdAbweichend(node, AExportMndId) then
      continue;
    data := TGerData.Create;
    for idx2 := 0 to node.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
    begin
      nodeCol := node.ChildNodes[idx2];
      value := TXmlUtils.ReplaceXml2Backend(nodeCol.Text);
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'GER_ID' then
        data.GerIdAlt := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'GER_BEZEICHNUNG' then
        data.GerBezeichnung := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'GER_GERAETEGRUNDTYP' then
        data.GerGeraetegrundtyp := value
    end;

    FResolveDataList.add(data);
  end;
end;

procedure TGerStammdaten.Resolve(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer);
var idx: Integer;
    qry: TSQLQuery;
    ger: TGerData;
    std: String;
begin
  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  if FInResolving then
    exit;
  StartResolving;
  try
    for idx := 0 to FResolveDataList.Count - 1 do
    begin
      ger := FResolveDataList[idx];
      if ger.GerIdResolved <> '' then
         Continue;
      if (ger.GerGeraetegrundtyp <> '') then  // Altlast aus ganz alten Kunden-Exporten
      begin
        std := '';
        qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT GER_ID, GER_BEZEICHNUNG, GER_STANDARD' + CR_LF +
                         ' FROM ' + ENGER + CR_LF +
                         ' WHERE GER_GERAETEGRUNDTYP = ' + QuotedStr(ger.GerGeraetegrundtyp) + CR_LF +
                         ' ORDER BY GER_ID ASC';
        qry.Open;
        try
          // Erste GerId nehmen, die die gleiche Bezeichnung und den gleichen Grundtyp hat
          while not qry.Eof do
          begin
            if (qry.FieldByName('GER_STANDARD').AsString = '1') then
              std := qry.FieldByName('GER_ID').AsString;
            if SameText(ger.GerBezeichnung, qry.FieldByName('GER_BEZEICHNUNG').AsString) then
              ger.GerIdResolved := qry.FieldByName('GER_ID').AsString;
            qry.Next;
          end;

          if (ger.GerIdResolved = '') then
          begin
            // Standard-GerId zum Grundtyp nehmen
            ger.GerIdResolved := std;
          end;

          if (ger.GerIdResolved <> '') then
            AddResolvedValue(ger.GerIdAlt, ger.GerIdResolved);
        finally
          qry.Close;
        end;
      end;

     if (ger.GerIdResolved = '') then
     begin
        // 0..9 sind normalerweise ausgelieferte Standard-Gerätetypen die nicht vom Benutzer geändert werden können
        ger.GerIdResolved := ger.GerIdAlt;
        AddResolvedValue(ger.GerIdAlt, ger.GerIdResolved);
     end;
    end;


  finally
    qry.Free;
    EndResolving;
  end;
end;


constructor TGerStammdaten.TGerData.Create();
begin
  GerIdAlt := '';
  GerIdResolved := '';
  GerBezeichnung := '';
  GerGeraetegrundtyp := '';
end;

function TZzdStammdaten.LoadExportData(ANode: IXMLNode; AExportMndId: Integer): Boolean;
var idx, idx2: Integer;
    node, nodeCol: IXMLNode;
    data: TZzdData;
    value: String;
begin
  result := false;
  if ANode = nil then
    exit;
  if not ANode.HasAttribute(TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME) then
    exit;
  if ANode.Attributes[TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME] <> TableName then
    exit;
  Clear;
  result := true;
  if not ANode.HasChildNodes then
   exit;

for idx := 0 to ANode.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
  begin
    node := ANode.ChildNodes[idx];
    if ExportMndIdAbweichend(node, AExportMndId) then
      continue;
    data := TZzdData.Create;
    for idx2 := 0 to node.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
    begin
      nodeCol := node.ChildNodes[idx2];
      value := TXmlUtils.ReplaceXml2Backend(nodeCol.Text);
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'ZZD_ID' then
        data.ZzdIdAlt := ''
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'LIF_ID' then
        data.LifId := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'NBT_ID' then
        data.NbtId := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'MBT_ID' then
        data.MbtID := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'MPT_ID' then
        data.MptID := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'ZZD_CODE' then
        data.ZzdCode := value
    end;
   FResolveDataList.add(data);
  end;
end;

procedure TZzdStammdaten.Resolve(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer);
var idx: Integer;
    qry: TSQLQuery;
    zzd: TZzdData;
    idNeu: string;
begin
  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  if FInResolving then
    exit;
  StartResolving;
  try
    for idx := 0 to FResolveDataList.Count - 1 do
    begin
      Zzd := FResolveDataList[idx];
      if Zzd.ZzdIdResolved <> '' then
         Continue;
      if zzd.LifId <> ''  then
      begin
        idNeu := Parent.GetValue('LIF_ID', zzd.LifId);
        if idNeu = '' then
          Continue;
        qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZZD_ID ' + CR_LF +
                       ' FROM ' + ENZZD + CR_LF +
                       ' WHERE ZZD_CODE = ' + QuotedStr(zzd.ZzdCode) + CR_LF +
                       ' AND LIF_ID = ' + idNeu;
      end
      else if zzd.NbtId <> ''  then
        begin
        idNeu := Parent.GetValue('NBT_ID', zzd.NbtId);
        if idNeu = '' then
          Continue;
        qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZZD_ID ' + CR_LF +
                       ' FROM ' + ENZZD + CR_LF +
                       ' WHERE ZZD_CODE = ' + QuotedStr(zzd.ZzdCode) + CR_LF +
                       ' AND NBT_ID = ' + idNeu;
        end
      else if zzd.MbtId <> ''  then
        begin
        idNeu := Parent.GetValue('MBT_ID', zzd.MbtId);
        if idNeu = '' then
          Continue;
        qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZZD_ID ' + CR_LF +
                       ' FROM ' + ENZZD + CR_LF +
                       ' WHERE ZZD_CODE = ' + QuotedStr(zzd.ZzdCode) + CR_LF +
                       ' AND MBT_ID = ' + idNeu;
        end
      else if zzd.MptId <> ''  then
        begin
        idNeu := Parent.GetValue('MPT_ID', zzd.MptId);
        if idNeu = '' then
          Continue;
        qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZZD_ID ' + CR_LF +
                       ' FROM ' + ENZZD + CR_LF +
                       ' WHERE ZZD_CODE = ' + QuotedStr(zzd.ZzdCode) + CR_LF +
                       ' AND MPT_ID = ' + idNeu;
      end;

      qry.Open;
      try
        if not qry.Eof then
        begin
          zzd.ZzdIdResolved := qry.FieldByName('ZZD_ID').AsString;
          AddResolvedValue(zzd.ZzdIdAlt, zzd.ZzdIdResolved);
        end;
      finally
        qry.Close;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
    EndResolving;
  end;
end;

constructor TZzdStammdaten.TZzdData.Create();
begin
        ZzdIdAlt := '';
        ZzdIdResolved := '';
        LifId := '';
        NbtId := '';
        MbtId := '';
        MptId := '';
        ZzdCode := '';
end;

  inherited Create(AParent, ENZZDR, 'ZZDR_CODE', ftString, 'SELECT DISTINCT ZZDR_ID, ZZDR_CODE, lif_id, nbt_id, mbt_id, mpt_id  ' +
                    '  FROM ' + ENZZDR + ' zzdr JOIN ' + ENZZDZ + ' zzdz on zzdz.zzdr_id = zzdr.zzdr_id ' +
                    '  JOIN ' + ENZZD + ' zzd ON zzd.zzd_id = zzdz.zzd_id', false);

  FResolveDataList := TelObjectList<TZzdrData>.Create(True);
  FDefaultValue :=  '';
end;

  inherited;
end;

function TZzdrStammdaten.LoadExportData(ANode: IXMLNode; AExportMndId: Integer): Boolean;
var idx, idx2: Integer;
    node, nodeCol: IXMLNode;
    data: TZzdrData;
    value: String;
begin
  result := false;
  if ANode = nil then
    exit;
  if not ANode.HasAttribute(TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME) then
    exit;
  if ANode.Attributes[TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME] <> TableName then
    exit;
  Clear;
  result := true;
  if not ANode.HasChildNodes then
   exit;

  for idx := 0 to ANode.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
  begin
    node := ANode.ChildNodes[idx];
    if ExportMndIdAbweichend(node, AExportMndId) then
      continue;
    data := TZzdrData.Create;
    for idx2 := 0 to node.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
    begin
      nodeCol := node.ChildNodes[idx2];
      value := TXmlUtils.ReplaceXml2Backend(nodeCol.Text);
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'ZZDR_ID' then
        data.ZzdrIdAlt := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'ZZDR_CODE' then
        data.ZzdrCode := value
      else
        if nodeCol.LocalName = 'LIF_ID' then
        data.LifId := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'NBT_ID' then
        data.NbtId := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'MBT_ID' then
        data.MbtID := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'MPT_ID' then
        data.MptID := value
      else
    end;

    FResolveDataList.add(data);
  end;
end;


procedure TZzdrStammdaten.Resolve(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer);
var idx: Integer;
    qry: TSQLQuery;
    zzdr: TZzdrData;
    idNeu: String;

begin
  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  if FInResolving then
    exit;
  StartResolving;
  try
    for idx := 0 to FResolveDataList.Count - 1 do
    begin
      zzdr := FResolveDataList[idx];
      if zzdr.ZzdrIdResolved <> '' then
         Continue;

      if zzdr.LifId <> ''  then
      begin
        idNeu := Parent.GetValue('LIF_ID', zzdr.LifId);
        if idNeu = '' then
          Continue;
        qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZZDR_ID ' + CR_LF +
                       ' FROM ' + ENZZDR + CR_LF +
                       ' WHERE ZZDR_CODE = ' + QuotedStr(zzdr.ZzdrCode) + CR_LF +
                       ' AND LIF_ID = ' + idNeu +
                       ' ORDER BY ZZDR_ID DESC';
      end

      else if zzdr.NbtId <> ''  then
      begin
        idNeu := Parent.GetValue('NBT_ID', zzdr.LifId);
        if idNeu = '' then
          Continue;
        qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZZDR_ID ' + CR_LF +
                       ' FROM ' + ENZZDR + CR_LF +
                       ' WHERE ZZDR_CODE = ' + QuotedStr(zzdr.ZzdrCode) + CR_LF +
                       ' AND NBT_ID = ' + idNeu +
                       ' ORDER BY ZZDR_ID DESC';
      end

      else if zzdr.MbtId <> ''  then
      begin
        idNeu := Parent.GetValue('MBT_ID', zzdr.MbtId);
        if idNeu = '' then
          Continue;
        qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZZDR_ID ' + CR_LF +
                       ' FROM ' + ENZZDR + CR_LF +
                       ' WHERE ZZDR_CODE = ' + QuotedStr(zzdr.ZzdrCode) + CR_LF +
                       ' AND MBT_ID = ' + idNeu +
                       ' ORDER BY ZZDR_ID DESC';
      end

      else if zzdr.MptId <> ''  then
      begin
        idNeu := Parent.GetValue('MPT_ID', zzdr.MptId);
        if idNeu = '' then
          Continue;
        qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT ZZDR_ID ' + CR_LF +
                       ' FROM ' + ENZZDR + CR_LF +
                       ' WHERE ZZDR_CODE = ' + QuotedStr(zzdr.ZzdrCode) + CR_LF +
                       ' AND MPT_ID = ' + idNeu +
                       ' ORDER BY ZZDR_ID DESC';

      end;
      qry.Open;
      try
        if not qry.Eof then
        begin
          zzdr.ZzdrIdResolved := qry.FieldByName('ZZDR_ID').AsString;
          AddResolvedValue(zzdr.ZzdrIdAlt, zzdr.ZzdrIdResolved);
        end;
      finally
        qry.Close;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
    EndResolving;
  end;
end;

begin
  ZzdrIdAlt := '';
  ZzdrIdResolved := '';
  ZzdrCode := '';
  LifId := '';
  NbtId := '';
  MbtId := '';
  MptId := '';
end;

  inherited Create(AParent, ENWFS, 'WFS_ID', ftLargeint, 'SELECT WFS_ID, WFS_BEZEICHNUNG, WFS_ADAPTER_NR FROM ' + ENWFS, false);
  FResolveDataList := TelObjectList<TWfsData>.Create(True);
  FDefaultValue :=  '';
end;

begin
  FreeAndNil(FResolveDataList);
  inherited;
end;

function TWfsStammdaten.LoadExportData(ANode: IXMLNode; AExportMndId: Integer): Boolean;
var idx, idx2: Integer;
    node, nodeCol: IXMLNode;
    data: TWfsData;
    value: String;
begin
  result := false;
  if ANode = nil then
    exit;
  if not ANode.HasAttribute(TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME) then
    exit;
  if ANode.Attributes[TAG_RESOLVE_TABLENAME] <> TableName then
    exit;
  Clear;
  result := true;
  if not ANode.HasChildNodes then
   exit;

for idx := 0 to ANode.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
  begin
    node := ANode.ChildNodes[idx];
    if ExportMndIdAbweichend(node, AExportMndId) then
      continue;
    data := TWfsData.Create;
    for idx2 := 0 to node.ChildNodes.Count - 1 do
    begin
      nodeCol := node.ChildNodes[idx2];
      value := TXmlUtils.ReplaceXml2Backend(nodeCol.Text);
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'WFS_ID' then
        data.WfsIdAlt := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'WFS_ADAPTER_NR' then
        data.WfsAdapterNr := value
      else
      if nodeCol.LocalName = 'WFS_BEZEICHNUNG' then
        data.WfsBezeichnung := value
    end;
   FResolveDataList.add(data);
  end;
end;

procedure TWfsStammdaten.Resolve(AConnection: TecConnection; AMndId: Integer);
var idx: Integer;
    qry: TSQLQuery;
    wfs: TWfsData;
begin
  qry := AConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  if FInResolving then
    exit;
  StartResolving;
  try
    for idx := 0 to FResolveDataList.Count - 1 do
    begin
      wfs := FResolveDataList[idx];
      if wfs.WfsIdResolved <> '' then
         Continue;

       qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT WFS_ID ' + CR_LF +
                       ' FROM ' + ENWFS + CR_LF +
                       ' WHERE 1=1 ' + CR_LF +
                       IfThen(wfs.WfsBezeichnung <> '', ' AND WFS_BEZEICHNUNG = ' + QuotedStr(wfs.WfsBezeichnung) + CR_LF, '') +
                       IfThen(wfs.WfsAdapterNr <> '', ' AND WFS_ADAPTER_NR = ' + QuotedStr(wfs.WfsAdapterNr) + CR_LF, '') +
                       ' ORDER BY WFS_ID DESC';
      qry.Open;
      try
        if not qry.Eof then
        begin
          wfs.WfsIdResolved := qry.FieldByName('WFS_ID').AsString;
          AddResolvedValue(wfs.WfsIdAlt, wfs.WfsIdResolved);
        end
        else
        begin
          qry.SQL.Clear;
          qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT WFS_ID ' + CR_LF +
                           ' FROM ' + ENWFS + CR_LF +
                           ' WHERE hie_id IN (' + IntToStr(HierarchieManager.GetHieIdByCS2Mandant(EnergyMandantToCS2(AMndId))) +
                           ', ' + IntToStr(HierarchieManager.RootHierarchie.HieId) + ')'  + CR_LF +
                           ' AND WFS_ADAPTER_NR = ' + QuotedStr(wfs.WfsAdapterNr) + CR_LF +
                           ' ORDER BY WFS_ID DESC';
          qry.Open;
          if not qry.Eof then
          begin
            wfs.WfsIdResolved := qry.FieldByName('WFS_ID').AsString;
            AddResolvedValue(wfs.WfsIdAlt, wfs.WfsIdResolved);
            Parent.AddLog('WFS_ID alt "' + wfs.WfsIdAlt + '" wurde durch "' + qry.FieldByName('WFS_ID').AsString + '" ohne Bezeichnung und Adapter aufgelöst');
          end
          else
          begin
            qry.SQL.Text := 'SELECT WFS_ID ' + CR_LF +
                           ' FROM ' + ENWFS + CR_LF +
                           ' WHERE hie_id IN (' + IntToStr(HierarchieManager.GetHieIdByCS2Mandant(EnergyMandantToCS2(AMndId))) +
                           ', ' + IntToStr(HierarchieManager.RootHierarchie.HieId) + ')'  + CR_LF +
                           ' AND WFS_ADAPTER_NR = 99999 '; // FallBack auf QS-Adapter
            qry.Open;
            if not qry.Eof then
            begin
              wfs.WfsIdResolved := qry.FieldByName('WFS_ID').AsString;
              AddResolvedValue(wfs.WfsIdAlt, wfs.WfsIdResolved);
              Parent.AddLog('WFS_ID alt "' + wfs.WfsIdAlt + '" wurde als FallBack durch "' + qry.FieldByName('WFS_ID').AsString + '" aufgelöst');
            end
             else
              raise Exception.Create('Keinen Eintrag in ENWORKFORCESERVICE für HIE_ID =  "'
                                  + IntToStr(HierarchieManager.GetHieIdByCS2Mandant(EnergyMandantToCS2(AMndId)))
                                  + '" und Adapter = "' + wfs.WfsAdapterNr + '" gefunden. Bitte legen Sie ENWFS an.');
          end;
        end;
      finally
        qry.Close;
      end;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
    EndResolving;
  end;
end;

constructor TWfsStammdaten.TWfsData.Create();
begin
    WfsIdAlt := '';
    WfsIdResolved := '';
    WfsBezeichnung := '';
    WfsAdapterNr := '';
end;

begin
  Nummer := ANummer;
  Bezeichnung := ABezeichnung;
  Art := AArt;
  ObisCodes := TStringList.Create;
  SmtpIdAlt := NULL_INTEGER;
  SmtpIdNeu := NULL_INTEGER;
end;

constructor TSmtpStammdaten.TSmtpData.Create;
begin
  Nummer := '';
  Bezeichnung := '';
  Art := NULL_INTEGER;
  ObisCodes := TStringList.Create;
  SmtpIdAlt := NULL_INTEGER;
  SmtpIdNeu := NULL_INTEGER;
end;
